and recall their gauge covariant evolution equations

\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\underline{\alpha}^{F}+\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\chi\underline{\alpha}^{F}=-\widehat{\nabla}\rho^{F}+\prescript{*}{}{\widehat{\nabla}}\sigma^{F}-2\prescript{*}{}{\underline{\eta}}\sigma^{F}-2\underline{\eta}\rho^{F}+2\omega\underline{\alpha}^{F}-\underline{\hat{\chi}}\cdot\alpha^{F},

(2.94)

\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\rho^{F}+\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\rho^{F}=-\widehat{\text{div}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}+\left(\eta-\underline{\eta}\right)\cdot\underline{\alpha}^{F},

(2.95)

\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\sigma^{F}+\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\sigma^{F}=-\widehat{\text{curl}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}+\left(\eta-\underline{\eta}\right)\cdot\prescript{*}{}{\underline{\alpha}}^{F}.

(2.96)

Now define
f_{1}:=|\underline{\alpha}^{F}|^{2}_{\gamma,\delta},~f_{2}=|\rho^{F}|^{2}_{\gamma,\delta}
, and
f_{3}:=|\sigma^{F}|^{2}_{\gamma,\delta}
. With these definitions in mind, let us apply the integration lemma to
f_{1}
,
f_{2}
, and
f_{3}
to yield

\displaystyle\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}\nabla_{4}f_{1}+\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}\nabla_{3}f_{2}+\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}\nabla_{3}f_{3}=\int_{\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}}f_{1}+\int_{H_{u}}f_{2}+\int_{H_{u}}f_{3}-\int_{\underline{H}_{\underline{u}_{0}}}f_{1}

(2.97)

\displaystyle-\int_{H_{u_{0}}}f_{2}-\int_{H_{u_{0}}}f_{3}+\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}(2\omega-\mbox{tr}\chi)f_{1}+\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}(2\underline{\omega}-\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})(f_{2}+f_{3}).

In order for these equations to exhibit a hyperbolic characteristic, the left hand side should simplify to terms that are algebraic in
\underline{\alpha}^{F},\rho^{F},
and
\sigma^{F}
upon using the null evolution equations. Now we note the most important point:
f_{1}
,
f_{2}
, and
f_{3}
are gauge-invariant objects and therefore we have the following as a consequence of the compatibility of the gauge covariant connection
\hat{\nabla}
with the metrics
\gamma
and
\delta
of the fibres:

\displaystyle\nabla_{4}f_{1}=2\langle\underline{\alpha}^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\rangle_{\gamma,\delta},~\nabla_{3}f_{2}=2\langle\rho^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\rho^{F}\rangle_{\gamma,\delta},~\nabla_{3}f_{3}=2\langle\sigma^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\sigma^{F}\rangle_{\gamma,\delta}.

(2.98)

Now we only focus on the principal terms for the hyperbolicity argument.

\begin{split}\langle\underline{\alpha}^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\rangle_{\gamma,\delta}&=\langle\underline{\alpha}^{F},-\widehat{\nabla}\rho^{F}+\prescript{*}{}{\widehat{\nabla}}\sigma^{F}+\cdot\cdot\cdot\cdot\rangle_{\gamma,\delta}\\
&=-\text{div}\langle\underline{\alpha}^{F},\rho^{F}\rangle_{\gamma,\delta}+\langle\widehat{\text{div}}\underline{\alpha}^{F},\rho^{F}\rangle_{\gamma,\delta}+\text{div}~^{*}\langle\underline{\alpha}^{F},\sigma^{F}\rangle_{\gamma,\delta}+\langle\widehat{\text{curl}}\underline{\alpha}^{F},\sigma^{F}\rangle+\text{l.o.t}\end{split}

(2.99)

\langle\rho^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\rho^{F}\rangle_{\gamma,\delta}=\langle\rho^{F},-\widehat{\text{div}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}+\cdot\cdot\cdot\cdot\rangle_{\gamma,\delta}

(2.100)

\langle\sigma^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\sigma^{F}\rangle_{\gamma,\delta}=\langle\sigma^{F},-\widehat{\text{curl}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}+\cdot\cdot\cdot\cdot\rangle_{\gamma,\delta}.

(2.101)

Now upon addition, we have

\begin{split}&\langle\underline{\alpha}^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\rangle_{\gamma,\delta}+\langle\rho^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\rho^{F}\rangle_{\gamma,\delta}+\langle\sigma^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\sigma^{F}\rangle_{\gamma,\delta}\\
=&-\text{div}\langle\underline{\alpha}^{F},\rho^{F}\rangle_{\gamma,\delta}+\langle\widehat{\text{div}}\underline{\alpha}^{F},\rho^{F}\rangle_{\gamma,\delta}+\text{div}~^{*}\langle\underline{\alpha}^{F},\sigma^{F}\rangle_{\gamma,\delta}+\langle\widehat{\text{curl}}\underline{\alpha}^{F},\sigma^{F}\rangle\\
&\hskip 142.26378pt-\langle\rho^{F},\widehat{\text{div}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\rangle_{\gamma,\delta}-\langle\sigma^{F},\widehat{\text{curl}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\rangle_{\gamma,\delta}+\text{l.o.t}\\
=&-\text{div}\langle\underline{\alpha}^{F},\rho^{F}\rangle_{\gamma,\delta}+\text{div}~^{*}\langle\underline{\alpha}^{F},\sigma^{F}\rangle_{\gamma,\delta}+\text{l.o.t}\end{split}

(2.102)

which, upon integration over the topological
2-
sphere, yields terms that are algebraic in
\underline{\alpha}^{F},\rho^{F}
, and
\sigma^{F}
. Here
\langle\underline{\alpha}^{F},\rho^{F}\rangle_{\gamma,\delta}=(\underline{\alpha}^{F})^{P}~_{Qab}(\rho^{F})^{Q}~_{P}~{}^{b}
and
~{}^{*}\langle\underline{\alpha}^{F},\sigma^{F}\rangle_{\gamma,\delta}=\epsilon^{ca}(\underline{\alpha}^{F})^{P}~_{Qab}\rho^{Q}~_{P}~{}^{b}
. The most vital property that is utilized here is the compatibility of the connection
\widehat{\nabla}
with the inner product
\langle~,~\rangle_{\gamma,\delta}
induced by the fibre metrics
\gamma
and
\delta
together with the Hodge structure present in the null Yang-Mills equations. Notice that nowhere in the procedure did we require explicit information about the Yang-Mills connection
1-
form
A^{P}~_{Q\mu}dx^{\mu}
. The remaining Yang-Mills null evolution equations may be utilized in a similar manner to obtain energy identities associated with the triple
\alpha^{F},\rho^{F},\sigma^{F}
. This concludes the proof of the hyperbolic characteristics of the null Yang-Mills equations.

\square

3
Preliminary estimates

3.1
Preliminary bootstrap assumptions

We will be employing a bootstrap argument to obtain a priori bounds on

\textstyle\mathbb{L}

,
\mathcal{R}
and
\mathbb{YM}
. Along the initial hypersurfaces
H_{u_{\infty}}
and
\underline{H}_{0}
, an analysis of the initial data using transport equations (see for example, [LukLocal]) yields

{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{0}+\mathcal{R}_{0}+\mathbb{YM}_{0}\lesssim\mathcal{I}.

Our goal is to show that in the entire region

\mathcal{D}:=\begin{Bmatrix}(u,\underline{u},\theta^{1},\theta^{2})\hskip 2.84526pt\mid\hskip 2.84526ptu_{\infty}\leq u\leq-\frac{a}{4},\hskip 1.42262pt0\leq\underline{u}\leq 1\end{Bmatrix}

there exists a constant
c(\mathcal{I})=\mathcal{I}^{4}+\mathcal{I}^{2}+\mathcal{I}+1
such that

{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}+\mathcal{R}+\mathbb{YM}\lesssim c(\mathcal{I}).

We assume, as a bootstrap assumption the following:

{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}\leq\Gamma,\hskip 5.69054pt\mathcal{R}\leq R,\hskip 5.69054pt\mathbb{YM}\leq M,

(3.1)

where
\Gamma,\hskip 1.42262ptR
and
M
are large so that

\mathcal{I}^{4}+\mathcal{I}^{2}+\mathcal{I}+1\ll\text{min}\begin{Bmatrix}\Gamma,\hskip 1.42262ptR,\hskip 1.42262ptM\end{Bmatrix}

but also such that

(\Gamma+R+M)^{20}\leq a^{\frac{1}{16}}.

3.2
Estimates on the metric components

For the metric component
\Omega
, the induced metric
\gamma
of
S_{u,\underline{u}}
and for the area of
S_{u,\underline{u}}
the following propositions hold:

Proposition 3.1

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
3.1
), we have

\lVert\Omega-1\rVert_{L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{\Gamma}{\lvert u\rvert}.

Proposition 3.2

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
3.1
), there exist two constants
c
and
C
depending only on the initial data such that the bounds

c\leq\text{det}\hskip 1.42262pt\gamma\leq C.

and

\lvert\gamma_{AB}\rvert+\lvert\gamma^{-1}_{AB}\rvert\leq C

hold throughout the slab of existence
\mathcal{D}
.

Proposition 3.3

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
3.1
), fix a point
(u,\theta)
on the initial hypersurface
\underline{H}_{0}
. Let
\Lambda(u)
and
\lambda(u)
be the largest and smallest eigenvalues of
\gamma^{-1}(u,0,\theta)\hskip 1.42262pt\gamma(u,\underline{u},\theta)
respectively, along the outgoing null geodesics emanating from
(u,\theta)
. There holds

\lvert\Lambda(u)-1\rvert+\lvert\lambda(u)-1\rvert\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

Proposition 3.4

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
3.1
), for the area of the
2-
sphere
S_{u,\underline{u}}
, there holds

\sup_{\underline{u}}\lvert\text{Area}(S_{u,\underline{u}})-\text{Area}(S_{u,0})\rvert\lesssim\frac{\Gamma^{\frac{1}{2}}}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lvert u\rvert^{2}.

The proofs of Propositions
3.1
to
3.4
are the same as in [An]

3.3
Estimates for transport equations

We shall be using two fundamental bounds on transport equations throughout this work.

Proposition 3.5

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
3.1
), the following hold for an arbitrary
\mathcal{G}\in\Gamma(^{N}\otimes T^{*}S\otimes P_{AD,\mathfrak{g}}):

\lVert{\mathcal{G}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\lVert\mathcal{G}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime\prime}})}+\int_{\underline{u}^{\prime\prime}}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\mathcal{G}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

(3.2)

\lVert{\mathcal{G}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\lVert\mathcal{G}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime\prime},\underline{u}})}+\int_{u^{\prime\prime}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\mathcal{G}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

(3.3)

There are, however, cases which are borderline and require more delicate control than what the above Proposition provides. These have to do with components
X
satisfying an equation of the form
\widehat{\nabla}_{3}X=-\lambda\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}X+\dots
, where
\lambda>0
. Keeping in mind that
\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}
is the worst Ricci coefficient in terms of peeling, one would hope to be able to get rid of its appearance and thus obtain stronger bounds regarding the peeling properties of
X
. The following weighted transport inequality achieves this.

Proposition 3.6

Let
\mathcal{G},\mathcal{H}\in\Gamma(^{N}\otimes T^{*}S\otimes P_{AD,\mathfrak{g}})
and assume that the following equation holds:

\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\mathcal{G}+\lambda_{0}\hskip 1.42262pt\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\hskip 1.42262pt\mathcal{G}=\mathcal{H}.

Then, under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
3.1
), the following is true:

\lvert u\rvert^{\lambda_{1}}\lVert{\mathcal{G}}\rVert_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\lvert u_{\infty}\rvert^{\lambda_{1}}\lVert\mathcal{G}\rVert_{L^{2}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{\lambda_{1}}\lVert\mathcal{H}\rVert_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

for
\lambda_{1}=2\lambda_{0}-1
.

Proof.

The variation of area formula for a scalar function
f
reads:

\underline{L}\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}f=\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}\underline{L}f+\Omega\hskip 1.42262pt\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\hskip 1.42262ptf=\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}\Omega\hskip 1.42262pt\left(e_{3}(f)+\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\hskip 1.42262ptf\right).

(3.4)

Plugging in
f=\lvert u\rvert^{2\lambda_{1}}\lvert\mathcal{G}\rvert_{\gamma,\delta}^{2}
and exploiting the compatibility of the gauge-covariant derivative with the fibre metrics
\gamma,\delta
, we calculate:

\begin{split}&\underline{L}\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}\lvert u\rvert^{2\lambda_{1}}\lvert\mathcal{G}\rvert_{\gamma,\delta}^{2}\\
=&\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}\Omega\left(-2\hskip 1.42262pt\lambda_{1}\lvert u\rvert^{2\hskip 1.42262pt\lambda_{1}-1}e_{3}(u)\lvert\mathcal{G}\rvert_{\gamma,\delta}^{2}+2\lvert u\rvert^{2\hskip 1.42262pt\lambda_{1}}\langle\mathcal{G},\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\mathcal{G}\rangle_{\gamma,\hskip 1.42262pt\delta}+\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\hskip 1.42262pt\lvert u\rvert^{2\lambda_{1}}\hskip 1.42262pt\lvert\mathcal{G}\rvert_{\gamma,\delta}^{2}\right)\\
=&\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}\Omega\left(2\lvert u\rvert^{2\lambda_{1}}\langle\mathcal{G},\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\mathcal{G}+\lambda_{0}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\mathcal{G}\rangle_{\gamma,\delta}\right)+\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}\Omega\lvert u\rvert^{2\lambda_{1}}\left(\frac{-2\lambda_{1}e_{3}(u)}{\lvert u\rvert}+(1-2\lambda_{0})\hskip 1.42262pt\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\right)\lvert\mathcal{G}\rvert_{\gamma,\delta}^{2}.\end{split}

(3.5)

Notice that

\begin{split}&\frac{-2\lambda_{1}e_{3}(u)}{\lvert u\rvert}+(1-2\lambda_{0})\hskip 1.42262pt\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\\
=&\frac{-2\lambda_{1}(\Omega^{-1}-1)}{\lvert u\rvert}+(1-2\lambda_{0})(\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}+\frac{2}{\lvert u\rvert})-\frac{2\lambda_{1}+2-4\lambda_{0}}{\lvert u\rvert}\\
\leq&\frac{\Gamma}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}},\end{split}

(3.6)

where we have used the bootstrap assumption
\lVert{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}+\frac{2}{\lvert u\rvert}}\rVert_{L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\frac{\Gamma}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}
and the definition of
\lambda_{1}
. For the first term in the last line of (
3.5
) we then use Cauchy-Schwartz and for the second we apply Grönwall’s inequality to get:

\begin{split}&\lvert u\rvert^{2\lambda_{1}}\lVert{\mathcal{G}}\rVert_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\lesssim&\mathrm{e}^{\Gamma\lVert u^{-2}\rVert_{L_{u}^{1}}}\left(\lvert u_{\infty}\rvert^{\lambda_{1}}\lVert\mathcal{G}\rVert_{L^{2}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{\lambda_{1}}\lVert\mathcal{H}\rVert_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\right)\\
\lesssim&\lvert u_{\infty}\rvert^{\lambda_{1}}\lVert\mathcal{G}\rVert_{L^{2}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{\lambda_{1}}\lVert\mathcal{H}\rVert_{L^{2}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime},\end{split}

(3.7)

where we have used the fact that
\mathrm{e}^{\Gamma\lVert u^{-2}\rVert_{L_{u}^{1}}}\lesssim\mathrm{e}^{\Gamma/a}\lesssim 1
.

\square

3.4
Sobolev embedding

With the derived estimates for the metric
\gamma
, we can obtain a bound on the isoperimetric constant for a topological
2-
sphere
S
:

\displaystyle I(S):=\sup_{U\subset S,~\partial U\in C^{1}}\frac{\min\left\{Area(U),Area(U^{c})\right\}}{[Perimeter(\partial U)]^{2}}.

(3.8)

The following proposition yields an upper bound for
I(S)
.

Proposition 3.7

Under the assumption on the initial data and the bootstrap assumption (2.10), the isoperimetric constant obeys the following bound

\displaystyle I(S_{u,\underline{u}})\leq\frac{1}{\pi}

(3.9)

for
u\in[u_{\infty},-\frac{a}{4}]
and
\underline{u}\in[0,1]
.

Proof.
Fix a
u
. For
U_{\underline{u}}
a subset of
S_{u,\underline{u}}
, denote by
U_{0}\subset S_{u,0}
the backward image of
U_{\underline{u}}
under the diffeomorphism generated by the equivariant vector field
L.
Using Propositions
3.2
and
3.3
and their proof, we can obtain the estimates

\frac{\text{Perimeter}(\partial U_{\underline{u}})}{\text{Perimeter}(\partial U_{0})}\geq\sqrt{\inf_{S_{u,0}}\lambda(\underline{u})}

and

\frac{\text{Area}(U_{\underline{u}})}{\text{Area}(U_{0})}\leq\sup_{S_{u,0}}\frac{\det(\gamma_{\underline{u}})}{\det(\gamma_{0})},\hskip 5.69054pt\frac{\text{Area}(U_{\underline{u}}^{c})}{\text{Area}(U_{0}^{c})}\leq\sup_{S_{u,0}}\frac{\det(\gamma_{\underline{u}})}{\det(\gamma_{0})}.

The conclusion then follows from the fact that
I(S_{u,0})=\frac{1}{2\pi}
and the bounds from Propositions
3.2
,
3.3
.
\square

Throughout this work, we will be using an
L^{2}-L^{\infty}
Sobolev estimate. To obtain it, utilizing the basic estimates above, we may proceed to write down the following gauge-invariant Sobolev inequalities for the topological
2-
sphere
S
.

Proposition 3.8

Let
(S,\gamma)
be a Riemannian
2-
manifold with the isoperimetric constant
I(S)
. Then the following Sobolev embedding holds for any
\mathcal{G}\in\Gamma(^{N}\otimes T^{*}S\otimes P_{AD,\mathfrak{g}})

\displaystyle\left(Area(S)\right)^{-\frac{1}{p}}||\mathcal{G}||_{L^{p}(S)}\leq C_{p}\left(\max(1,I(S))\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(||\widehat{\nabla}\mathcal{G}||_{L^{2}(S)}+Area(S)^{-\frac{1}{2}}||\mathcal{G}||_{L^{2}(S)}\right)

(3.10)

for any
p\in(2,\infty)
.

Proof.
We know that the standard Sobolev inequality with
p\in(2,\infty)

\displaystyle\left(Area(S)\right)^{-\frac{1}{p}}||f||_{L^{p}(S)}\leq C_{p}\left(\max(1,I(S))\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(||\nabla f||_{L^{2}(S)}+Area(S)^{-\frac{1}{2}}||f||_{L^{2}(S)}\right)

(3.11)

holds for a function
f
. Now we set

\displaystyle f=\sqrt{\mathcal{G}^{P}~_{QA_{1}A_{2}A_{3}\cdot\cdot\cdot\cdot A_{N}}\mathcal{G}^{Q}~_{PB_{1}B_{2}B_{3}\cdot\cdot\cdot\cdot B_{N}}\gamma^{A_{1}B_{1}}\gamma^{A_{2}B_{2}}\gamma^{A_{3}B_{3}}\cdot\cdot\cdot\cdot\gamma^{A_{N}B_{N}}+\epsilon}

(3.12)

with
\epsilon>0
to yield

\displaystyle||\nabla f||_{L^{2}(S)}=||\frac{\langle\mathcal{G},\widehat{\nabla}\mathcal{G}\rangle_{\gamma,\delta}}{\sqrt{\mathcal{G}^{P}~_{QA_{1}A_{2}A_{3}\cdot\cdot\cdot\cdot A_{N}}\mathcal{G}^{Q}~_{PB_{1}B_{2}B_{3}\cdot\cdot\cdot\cdot B_{N}}\gamma^{A_{1}B_{1}}\gamma^{A_{2}B_{2}}\gamma^{A_{3}B_{3}}\cdot\cdot\cdot\cdot\gamma^{A_{N}B_{N}}+\epsilon}}||_{L^{2}(S)}

\displaystyle\leq||\frac{|\mathcal{G}|_{\gamma,\delta}|\widehat{\nabla}\mathcal{G}|_{\gamma,\delta}}{\sqrt{\mathcal{G}^{P}~_{QA_{1}A_{2}A_{3}\cdot\cdot\cdot\cdot A_{N}}\mathcal{G}^{Q}~_{PB_{1}B_{2}B_{3}\cdot\cdot\cdot\cdot B_{N}}\gamma^{A_{1}B_{1}}\gamma^{A_{2}B_{2}}\gamma^{A_{3}B_{3}}\cdot\cdot\cdot\cdot\gamma^{A_{N}B_{N}}+\epsilon}}||_{L^{2}(S)},

since
f
is a gauge-invariant object and the gauge covariant derivative is compatible with fibre metrics
\gamma
and
\delta
. Substituting this inequality in (
3.11
) and taking the limit
\epsilon\to 0
yields the desired gauge-invariant Sobolev inequality.
\square

Proposition 3.9

Let
(S,\gamma)
be a Riemannian
2-
manifold with the isoperimetric constant
I(S)
. Then the following Sobolev embedding holds for any
\mathcal{G}\in\Gamma(^{N}\otimes T^{*}S\otimes P_{AD,\mathfrak{g}})

\displaystyle||\mathcal{G}||_{L^{\infty}(S)}\leq C_{p}\left(\max(1,I(S))\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}[Area(S)]^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{p}}\left(||\widehat{\nabla}\mathcal{G}||_{L^{p}(S)}+Area(S)^{-\frac{1}{2}}||\mathcal{G}||_{L^{p}(S)}\right)

(3.13)

for any
p\in(2,\infty)
.

Proof.
A calculation similar to the previous one and the standard
L^{\infty}-L^{p}
Sobolev inequality on the Riemannian manifold
(S,\gamma)
yield the result.
\square

The two inequalities above, together with Propositions
3.1
-
3.4
, allow us to control the
L^{2}
-norm of
\mathcal{G}
in terms of its
H^{2}
-norm. Following the area estimates, we have
Area(S_{u,\underline{u}})\approx u^{2}
. Therefore, we obtain the following important inequality.

Proposition 3.10

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
3.1
), the following Sobolev embedding holds for any
\mathcal{G}\in\Gamma(^{N}\otimes T^{*}S_{u,\underline{u}}\otimes P_{AD,\mathfrak{g}}):

\displaystyle||\mathcal{G}||_{L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\sum_{0\leq I\leq 2}|||u|^{I-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\mathcal{G}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})},

(3.14)

which, in scale-invariant norms, reads

\displaystyle||\mathcal{G}||_{{L}^{\infty}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\sum_{0\leq I\leq 2}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{I}\mathcal{G}||_{{L}^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}.

(3.15)

Proof.
Substitute
p=4
in the previous proposition (
3.13
) and estimate the right hand side by means of (
3.10
), the estimate on the area given by Proposition
3.4
and on the isoperimetric constant of
S_{u,\underline{u}}
given by Proposition
3.9
.
\square

4
Scale-invariant estimates on the Ricci coefficients and Yang-Mills components

4.1
Preliminary estimates

In this section we list some necessary and useful estimates, to be used extensively in the following sections. Let

\mathrm{\Upsilon}\in\begin{Bmatrix}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\alpha^{F},\rho^{F},\sigma^{F},\underline{\alpha}^{F}\end{Bmatrix},\hskip 5.69054pt\Psi\in\begin{Bmatrix}\frac{\alpha}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}},\beta^{R},\rho,\sigma,\underline{\beta}^{R},\underline{\alpha}\end{Bmatrix},\hskip 5.69054pt\tilde{\mathrm{\Upsilon}}\in\begin{Bmatrix}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F},\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\end{Bmatrix},

\psi_{g}\in\begin{Bmatrix}\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi},\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\hat{\chi},\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert}\underline{\hat{\chi}},\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}},\underline{\omega},\zeta,\underline{\eta},\eta,\omega,\mbox{tr}\chi\end{Bmatrix}.

Finally, we note that by
\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}
we mean an expression of the form

\nabla^{k_{1}}\psi_{g}\dots\nabla^{k_{i_{2}}}\psi_{g},

where
k_{1}+\dots+k_{i_{2}}=i_{1}
.

Proposition 4.1

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
3.1
), the following hold:

\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}\leq N+4}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{1}+i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\lvert u\rvert,

\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}\leq N+4}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\mathrm{\Upsilon})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\Gamma,

\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}\leq N+4}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\mathrm{\Upsilon})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\psi_{g},\mathrm{\Upsilon})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert},

\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}\leq N+5}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\mathrm{\Upsilon})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\psi_{g},\mathrm{\Upsilon})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{5}}(\psi_{g},\mathrm{\Upsilon})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\frac{\Gamma^{3}}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}},

\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}\leq N+3}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\Psi}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\Gamma,

,

\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}\leq N+3}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\mathrm{\Upsilon})\nabla^{i_{4}}\Psi}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert},

\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}\leq N+3}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\mathrm{\Upsilon})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\psi_{g},\mathrm{\Upsilon})\nabla^{i_{5}}\Psi}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\frac{\Gamma^{3}}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}},

\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}\leq N+2}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\tilde{\mathrm{\Upsilon}}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\Gamma,

\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}\leq N+2}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\mathrm{\Upsilon},\Psi)\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\tilde{\mathrm{\Upsilon}}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert}.

Proof.

We only prove the first two statements and the rest follow in the same way (see also [An] for more details), taking into account the bootstrap assumptions (
3.1
) and the fact that these assumptions help us control up to
N+4
derivatives of
(\psi_{g},\mathrm{\Upsilon})
,
N+3
derivatives of
\Psi
and
N+2
derivatives of
\tilde{\mathrm{\Upsilon}}
. Working on the first inequality, we see that if
i_{2}=0
then it trivially holds, since

\lVert{1}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}=\lvert u\rvert.

If
i_{2}\geq 1
, we write
\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}
as
\nabla^{k_{1}}\psi_{g}\dots\nabla^{k_{i_{2}}}\psi_{g},
for
k_{1}+\dots+k_{i_{2}}=i_{1}
. Assume, without loss of generality, that
k_{i_{2}}
is the largest among the coefficients. We then write

(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{1}+i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}=(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}}(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{k_{i_{2}}}\psi_{g}\Pi_{\lambda=1}^{i_{2}-1}(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{k_{\lambda}}\psi_{g}

and proceed to bound
(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{k_{i_{2}}}\psi_{g}
in
L^{2}_{(sc)}
and the rest of the terms in
L^{\infty}_{(sc)}
. By iterating (
2.66
) a total of
(i_{2}-1)
times, we obtain

\begin{split}&\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}\leq N+4}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{1}+i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}\leq N+4}\frac{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert^{i_{2}-1}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{k_{i_{2}}}\psi_{g}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\Pi_{\lambda=1}^{i_{2}-1}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{k_{\lambda}}\psi_{g}}\rVert_{L^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\left(\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{\lvert u\rvert}\right)^{i_{2}}\leq 1.\end{split}

(4.1)

Moving on to the second inequality, if
i_{2}=0
, it trivially holds by the definition of
\Gamma
. If
i_{2}\geq 1
, in the case where
i_{1}\leq N+2
, we bound
(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i_{4}}(\psi_{g},\mathrm{\Upsilon})
in
L^{2}_{(sc)}
and the rest of the terms in
L^{\infty}_{(sc)}
, thus getting a bound of

\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert}\cdot\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{1}+i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}}\rVert_{L^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\cdot\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\mathrm{\Upsilon})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\frac{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{2}}\Gamma^{i_{2}+1}}{\lvert u\rvert^{i_{2}}}\leq\Gamma.

If, on the other hand,
i_{1}\geq N+3
, we bound
(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i_{4}}(\psi_{g},\mathrm{\Upsilon})
in
L^{\infty}_{(sc)}
and the rest of the expression in
L^{2}_{(sc)}
. Using the bound obtained in the first inequality of the proposition, we then arrive at a bound of
\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert}\cdot\lvert u\rvert\cdot\Gamma=\Gamma
. The result follows.

\square

4.2
Estimates on the Ricci coefficients

Proposition 4.2

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
3.1
), there holds

\sum_{i\leq N+4}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\omega}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(1+\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\mathcal{R}}[\rho]\right).

Proof.

We begin by recalling that
\omega
satisfies the schematic equation

\nabla_{3}\omega=\frac{1}{2}\rho+\psi_{g}\psi_{g}+\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}.

Crucially, the schematic product
\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}
is
gauge-invariant
(in this case, it equals
\lvert\rho^{F}\rvert^{2}+\lvert\sigma^{F}\rvert^{2}
). Using the commutation formula (
2.87
) and the notation of Section
2.7
, we have, for a general
i
:

\begin{split}\nabla_{3}\nabla^{i}\omega+\frac{i}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{i}\omega=&\nabla^{i}\rho+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\rho+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\psi_{g}\nabla^{i_{4}}\psi_{g}\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}})\nabla^{i_{4}}\omega\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{i_{4}}\omega\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-2}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\nabla^{i_{4}}\omega\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}\nabla^{i_{5}}\omega.\end{split}

(4.2)

Note that since
\omega
is not a section of the vector bundle
~{}^{k}\otimes T^{*}S\otimes P_{Ad,\mathfrak{g}}
, the last term on the right-hand side of (
2.87
) does not appear. Passing to scale-invariant norms, we get

\displaystyle\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\omega}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\lVert(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\omega\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u_{\infty}},0)}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\rho}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\rho}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\psi_{g}\nabla^{i_{4}}\psi_{g}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}})\nabla^{i_{4}}\omega}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{i_{4}}\omega}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-2}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\nabla^{i_{4}}\omega}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}\nabla^{i_{5}}\omega}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}.

(4.3)

For
0\leq i\leq N+4
, the first term, by virtue of the fact that
\Omega\equiv 1
initially, vanishes. The second term can be bounded, using Hölder’s inequality, by
\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert^{\frac{1}{2}}}\underline{\mathcal{R}}[\rho].
The third, fourth and fifth terms can be bounded above by

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\leq\frac{a\hskip 1.42262pt\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\leq\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

The sixth term is controlled as follows:

\begin{split}&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}})\nabla^{i_{4}}\omega}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\frac{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert^{\frac{1}{2}}}.\end{split}

(4.4)

The seventh term is controlled as follows:

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{i_{4}}\omega}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\frac{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}{a}\frac{\Gamma^{3}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{\Gamma^{3}}{\lvert u\rvert}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

(4.5)

For the eighth and most borderline term, we estimate:

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-2}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\nabla^{i_{4}}\omega}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-2}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\nabla^{i_{4}}\omega}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\frac{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}{a}\frac{\Gamma^{3}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\Gamma^{3}}{\lvert u\rvert}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

(4.6)

Finally, the ninth term can be estimated as follows:

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}\nabla^{i_{5}}\omega}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\frac{\Gamma^{3}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

(4.7)

The result follows.

\square

We move on to estimates for
\underline{\hat{\chi}}
and
\hat{\chi}
, which are the same as in [AnAth] or [An2019], because of the absence of matter terms in the corresponding structure equations.

Proposition 4.3

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
3.1
), there hold

\sum_{i\leq N+4}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\underline{\hat{\chi}}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim 1,\hskip 5.69054pt\sum_{i\leq N+4}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\mathcal{R}[\alpha]+1.

Proof.

This is the same as in [An2019].

\square

Remark 5

As will be shown later on, there holds

\sum_{0\leq i\leq N+3}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\alpha}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim 1.

As a consequence, when
i\neq N+4
, the result of Proposition
4.3
can be improved to

\sum_{0\leq i\leq N+3}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim 1.

By Sobolev embedding, this also implies the pointwise estimate

\sum_{0\leq i\leq N+1}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{L^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim 1.

This will be useful later on, for example in Proposition
4.6
.

The estimates for
\underline{\omega}
are, in a sense, dual to those for
\omega
.

Proposition 4.4

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
3.1
), there holds

\sum_{i\leq N+4}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\underline{\omega}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\mathcal{R}[\rho]+1.

Proof.

We have, schematically,

\nabla_{4}\underline{\omega}=\frac{1}{2}\rho+\psi_{g}\psi_{g}+\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}.

As before, the schematic product of the Yang-Mills components is gauge-invariant. Using the commutation formula (
2.86
), we have, for a general
i
:

\begin{split}\nabla_{4}\nabla^{i}\underline{\omega}=&\nabla^{i}\rho+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\rho+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\psi_{g}\nabla^{i_{4}}\psi_{g}\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{i_{4}}\underline{\omega}\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-2}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{i_{4}}\underline{\omega}\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{i_{5}}\underline{\omega}.\end{split}

(4.8)

Passing to scale-invariant norms, we have, since
\lVert(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\underline{\omega}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,0})}
vanishes,

\begin{split}&\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\underline{\omega}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\lesssim&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\rho}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
+&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\rho}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
+&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\psi_{g}\nabla^{i_{4}}\psi_{g}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
+&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
+&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{i_{4}}\underline{\omega}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
+&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-2}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{i_{4}}\underline{\omega}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
+&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{i_{5}}\underline{\omega}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}.\end{split}

(4.9)

For
0\leq i\leq N+4
, the first four terms are controlled as in Proposition
4.2
and are bounded above by
\mathcal{R}[\rho]+1
. For the next terms, there holds

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{i_{4}}\underline{\omega}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\frac{\hat{\chi}}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}},\frac{\mbox{tr}\chi}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}})\nabla^{i_{4}}\underline{\omega}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert}\lesssim 1.\end{split}

(4.10)

Working similarly, there hold

\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-2}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{i_{4}}\underline{\omega}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a\hskip 1.42262pt\Gamma^{3}}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\lesssim 1,

(4.11)

\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{i_{5}}\underline{\omega}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\Gamma^{3}}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\lesssim 1.

(4.12)

The claim follows.

\square

We move on to estimates for
\eta
.

Proposition 4.5

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
3.1
), there holds

\sum_{0\leq i\leq N+4}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\eta}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\mathcal{R}[\beta^{R}]+1.

Proof.

We begin with the schematic structure equation for
\eta
:

\nabla_{4}\eta=\beta^{R}+(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\cdot(\eta,\underline{\eta})+\alpha^{F}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}},

with the product of Yang-Mills curvature terms being once again gauge-invariant. Here, the
\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}
term is
(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})
. Using the commutation formula (
2.86
) for the
\nabla_{4}-
direction, we have

\begin{split}\nabla_{4}\nabla^{i}\eta=&\nabla^{i}\beta^{R}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\beta^{R}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{i_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{i_{5}}\eta.\end{split}

(4.13)

Estimating in scale-invariant norms, we have

\begin{split}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\eta}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}&\lesssim\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\beta^{R}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
&+\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\beta^{R}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
&+\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{i_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
&+\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\\
&+\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{i_{5}}\eta}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}.\end{split}

(4.14)

For
0\leq i\leq N+4
, the first term is bounded by
\mathcal{R}[\beta^{R}]
. The second term is bounded by

\begin{split}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\beta^{R}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert}\lesssim 1.\end{split}

(4.15)

Notice that, since
i_{3}\leq i-1\leq N+3
, we can bound
i_{3}
derivatives of
\beta^{R}
using the bootstrap assumption (
3.1
) on the total norm

\textstyle\mathbb{L}

. For the third term there holds

\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{i_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert}\lesssim 1.

Here we have picked up an
a^{\frac{1}{2}}
-term because of the existence of
\hat{\chi}
in the expression. For the fourth term, we have

\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}O^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert}\lesssim 1

and finally, for the fifth term, we have

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{i_{5}}\eta}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
=&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}a\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\bigg\lVert(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\left(\frac{\alpha^{F}}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right)\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{i_{5}}\eta\bigg\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\frac{a\Gamma^{3}}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\lesssim 1.\end{split}

(4.16)

The result follows.

\square

Proposition 4.6

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
3.1
), there holds

\sum_{0\leq i\leq N+4}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\mbox{tr}\chi}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\mathcal{R}[\alpha]+\underline{\mathbb{YM}}[\rho^{F},\sigma^{F}]+1.

Proof.

We begin by recalling the schematic equation

\nabla_{4}\mbox{tr}\chi=\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert^{2}+\psi_{g}\psi_{g}+\lvert\alpha^{F}\rvert^{2}.

Commuting with
i
angular derivatives using (
2.86
), we obtain

\begin{split}\nabla_{4}\nabla^{i}\mbox{tr}\chi=&\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\hat{\chi}\nabla^{i_{4}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\psi_{g}\nabla^{i_{4}}\psi_{g}\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\alpha^{F}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-2}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{i_{4}}\psi_{g}\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{i_{5}}\psi_{g}.\end{split}

(4.17)

Passing to scale-invariant norms, we have

\begin{split}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\mbox{tr}\chi}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\hat{\chi}\nabla^{i_{4}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
+&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\psi_{g}\nabla^{i_{4}}\psi_{g}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
+&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
+&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-2}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{i_{4}}\psi_{g}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
+&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\sum_{i_{1}+\dots+i_{5}=i-1}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{i_{5}}\psi_{g}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}.\end{split}

(4.18)

From the first term, the most dangerous case is when
i_{4}
falls on
\hat{\chi}
, so we only give details for that. We distinguish two cases:

•

If in the term
\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}
there exists some
\psi_{g}
whose derivative is of order
>N+3
, we bound that term in
L^{2}_{(sc)}
and the rest of the terms in
L^{\infty}_{(sc)}
. Notice, crucially, that from Remark
5
, we can bound
\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{L^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim a^{\frac{1}{2}}
for small
i
. As a consequence, we have the bound

\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\hat{\chi}\nabla^{i_{4}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{L^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert}\lesssim 1.

•

Otherwise, in the expression
\nabla^{i_{3}}\hat{\chi}\nabla^{i_{4}}\hat{\chi}
, at most one index
i_{3},i_{4}
is greater than
N+1
(in which case, we cannot bound that term in
L^{\infty}_{(sc)}
). Say without loss of generality, that
i_{3}>N+1
. We bound
(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{3}-1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\hat{\chi}
in
L^{2}_{(sc)}
above by
\mathcal{R}[\alpha]+1
and the rest of the terms in
L^{\infty}_{(sc)}
above by
1
(using the improvement mentioned in Remark
5
), whence

\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\hat{\chi}\nabla^{i_{4}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\left(\mathcal{R}[\alpha]+1\right)\cdot 1.

The second term is handled as in the previous propositions. For the third term, we can bound

\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\left(\underline{\mathbb{YM}}[\rho^{F},\sigma^{F}]+1\right)\cdot 1,

using the same idea as for
\hat{\chi}
and the improvement given in Remark
6
. The last two terms are bounded above by
1
, as in previous Propositions.

\square

Proposition 4.7

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
3.1
), the following estimates hold:

\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\sum_{0\leq i\leq N+4}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim 1,\hskip 5.69054pt\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\sum_{0\leq i\leq N+4}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim 1.

Proof.

Notice that
\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}
satisfies the following structure equation:

\nabla_{3}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}+\frac{1}{2}(\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})^{2}=-\lvert\underline{\hat{\chi}}\rvert^{2}-2\underline{\omega}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}-\lvert\underline{\alpha}^{F}\rvert^{2}.

Commuting with
i
angular derivatives using (
2.87
), we get

\begin{split}\nabla_{3}\nabla^{i}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}+\frac{i+1}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{i}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}=&\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\nabla^{i_{4}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\\
+&\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\underline{\omega}\nabla^{i_{4}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\\
+&\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\\
+&\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}})\nabla^{i_{4}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\\
+&\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{i_{4}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\\
+&\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-2}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\nabla^{i_{4}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\\
+&\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{i_{5}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}:=G_{i}.\end{split}

(4.19)

Passing to scale invariant norms and using the weighted transport inequality from Proposition
3.6
, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\hskip 1.42262pt

\displaystyle\frac{a}{\lvert u_{\infty}\rvert^{2}}\lVert(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}

(4.20)

\displaystyle+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}G_{i}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}.

(4.21)

We focus on
0\leq i\leq N+4
. For the first term in
G_{i}
, there holds

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\nabla^{i_{4}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\cdot\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim 1.

(4.22)

The second and third terms are handled in the same way. For the fourth term, there holds

\begin{split}&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}})\nabla^{i_{4}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}\cdot\frac{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}{a}\frac{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\cdot\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert}\lesssim 1.\end{split}

(4.23)

For the fifth term, we bound

\begin{split}&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{i_{4}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}\cdot\frac{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}{a^{2}}\cdot\frac{\Gamma^{3}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{\Gamma^{3}}{\lvert u\rvert}\lesssim 1.\end{split}

(4.24)

For the sixth term, we can bound

\begin{split}&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-2}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\nabla^{i_{4}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\\
=&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{5}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i-1}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+1=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\nabla^{i_{4}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{5}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}\cdot\frac{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}{a^{2}}\cdot\frac{\Gamma^{3}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\Gamma^{3}}{\lvert u\rvert}\lesssim 1.\end{split}

(4.25)

Finally, there holds

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{i_{5}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

(4.26)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{5}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i-1}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{i_{5}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

(4.27)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{5}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}\cdot\frac{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}{a}\cdot\frac{\Gamma^{3}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\Gamma^{3}}{\lvert u\rvert^{3}}\lesssim 1.

(4.28)

Crucially, this implies that

\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\sum_{0\leq i\leq N+2}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}\rVert_{L^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\mathcal{I}+1,

(4.29)

by Sobolev embedding. This will prove useful in the estimates for
\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}
. For this component, we have the following structure equation:

\nabla_{3}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}+\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}=\frac{2}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}(\Omega^{-1}-1)+\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}+\psi_{g}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}-\lvert\underline{\hat{\chi}}\rvert^{2}-\lvert\underline{\alpha}^{F}\rvert^{2}:=H_{0}.

Commuting with
i
angular derivatives, we arrive at

\displaystyle\nabla_{3}\nabla^{i}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}+\frac{i+2}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{i}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}H_{0}

(4.30)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}})\nabla^{i_{4}}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}

(4.31)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{i_{4}}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}

(4.32)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-2}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\nabla^{i_{4}}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}

(4.33)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{i_{5}}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}:=H_{i}.

(4.34)

Estimating in scale-invariant norms, we have

\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{a}{\lvert u_{\infty}\rvert}\lVert(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}H_{i}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}.

We restrict attention to
0\leq i\leq N+4
. The first term

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}H_{0}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

can be bounded above by
\mathcal{R}[\rho]+\underline{\mathcal{R}}[\rho]+1
, precisely as in [AnAth]. The second, third and fourth of the terms can be bounded as in previous propositions by
1
. For the last term, we have

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{i_{5}}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}

(4.35)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\frac{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}{a}\frac{\Gamma^{3}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim 1.

(4.36)

Here, once again, we have made use of the bootstrap assumptions on the gauge-covariantly differentiated Yang-Mills curvature components, bounded above by
\Gamma
. The result follows.

\square

We conclude this section with the corresponding estimate on
\underline{\eta}
and its angular derivatives.

Proposition 4.8

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
3.1
), there holds

\sum_{0\leq i\leq N+4}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\underline{\eta}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\mathcal{R}[\beta^{R}]+\underline{\mathcal{R}}[\underline{\beta}^{R}]+1.

Proof.

The schematic equation for
\underline{\eta}
is as follows:

\nabla_{3}\underline{\eta}+\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\eta}=\underline{\beta}^{R}+\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\eta+\psi_{g}\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\underline{\alpha}^{F}\cdot(\rho^{F},\hskip 1.42262pt\sigma^{F}).

(4.37)

\displaystyle\nabla_{3}\nabla^{i}\underline{\eta}+\frac{i+1}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{i}\underline{\eta}=

\displaystyle\nabla^{i}\underline{\beta}^{R}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\underline{\beta}^{R}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\psi_{g}\nabla^{i_{4}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}})\nabla^{i_{4}}\underline{\eta}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{i_{4}}\underline{\eta}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-2}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\nabla^{i_{4}}\underline{\eta}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{i_{5}}\underline{\eta}:=H_{i}.

(4.38)

Calculating in scale-invariant norms, we arrive at

\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\underline{\eta}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{1}{\lvert u_{\infty}\rvert}\lVert(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\underline{\eta}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}H_{i}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}.

(4.39)

We restrict attention to
0\leq i\leq N+4
. For the first term, there holds

\frac{1}{\lvert u_{\infty}\rvert}\lVert(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\underline{\eta}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{\mathcal{I}}{\lvert u_{\infty}\rvert}\lesssim\frac{\mathcal{I}}{\lvert u\rvert}.

(4.40)

There holds

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\underline{\beta}^{R}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\underline{\beta}^{R}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\underline{\mathcal{R}}[\underline{\beta}^{R}]\cdot\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert^{\frac{3}{2}}}\lesssim\frac{\underline{\mathcal{R}}[\underline{\beta}^{R}]}{\lvert u\rvert}.

(4.41)

For the next term, there holds

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\underline{\beta}^{R}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\cdot\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert}.

(4.42)

For the third term, there holds

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\psi_{g}\nabla^{i_{4}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{\mathcal{R}[\beta^{R}]+1}{\lvert u\rvert}.

(4.43)

This is done by further taking into account that the schematic product appearing is actually
\psi_{g}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})=\underline{\hat{\chi}}\cdot\psi_{g}+\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\eta
. As such, we use the improvement obtained in Proposition
4.7
for the
\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}
-term as well as the improvement obtained in Proposition
4.5
for
\eta
. The term
\underline{\hat{\chi}}
is less anomalous than
\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}
and hence the above bound is easier to obtain. Continuing the estimates, there holds

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\cdot\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert}.

(4.44)

For the fifth term, there holds

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}})\nabla^{i_{4}}\underline{\eta}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\cdot\frac{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\cdot\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert}.

(4.45)

For the sixth term, there holds

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{i_{4}}\underline{\eta}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\cdot\frac{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}{a}\cdot\frac{\Gamma^{3}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert}.

(4.46)

For the seventh term, there holds

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-2}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\nabla^{i_{4}}\underline{\eta}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\cdot\frac{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}{a}\cdot\frac{\Gamma^{3}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\Gamma^{3}}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\lesssim\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert}.

(4.47)

Finally, for the last term, there holds

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{i_{5}}\underline{\eta}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\cdot\frac{\Gamma^{3}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert}.

(4.48)

Putting everything together, we have

\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\underline{\eta}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{\mathcal{R}[\beta^{R}]+\underline{\mathcal{R}}[\underline{\beta}^{R}]+1}{\lvert u\rvert},

whence the result follows.

\square

This concludes the estimates on Ricci coefficients.

4.3
Estimates on the Yang-Mills fields

Proposition 4.9

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
3.1
), there holds

\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{0\leq i\leq N+4}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{i}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\underline{\mathbb{YM}}[\rho^{F},\sigma^{F}]+1.

Proof.

Recall the null evolution equation for
\underline{\alpha}^{F}

\displaystyle\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\alpha^{F}+\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\alpha^{F}=\widehat{\nabla}\rho^{F}+\prescript{*}{}{\widehat{\nabla}}\sigma^{F}-2\prescript{*}{}{\eta}\sigma^{F}+2\eta\rho^{F}+2\underline{\omega}\alpha^{F}-\hat{\chi}\cdot\underline{\alpha}^{F}.

(4.49)

An application of the commutation identity (
2.87
) yields

\begin{split}&\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\alpha^{F}+\frac{I+1}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\alpha^{F}\\
=&\widehat{\nabla}^{I+1}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})+\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}+1}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{2}}\alpha^{F}\\
&+\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}+1}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\alpha^{F}\hskip 5.69054pt\\
&+\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}\alpha^{F}\\
&+\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\alpha^{F}+\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}+1}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}+1}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\\
&+\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\eta\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})+\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\underline{\omega}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\alpha^{F}\\
&+\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\widehat{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}+\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\hat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\widehat{\underline{\chi}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\alpha^{F}\\
=&\mathfrak{G}_{1},\end{split}

(4.50)

where we denote the right hand side by
\mathfrak{G}_{1}
for convenience. An application of the weighted transport inequality (
3.6
) yields

\displaystyle|u|^{I}||\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\alpha^{F}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq|u_{\infty}|^{I}||\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\alpha^{F}||_{L^{2}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{I}||\mathfrak{G}_{1}||_{L^{2}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}.

(4.51)

Now we recall the definitions of the scale-invariant norms

||\phi||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}:=a^{-s_{2}(\phi)}|u|^{2s_{2}(\phi)}||\phi||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

to yield

\displaystyle||\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\alpha^{F}||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}=a^{-\frac{I}{2}}|u|^{I}||\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\alpha^{F}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})},

(4.52)

since
s_{2}(\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\alpha^{F})=s_{2}(\alpha^{F})+I\times\frac{1}{2}=0+\frac{I}{2}=\frac{I}{2}
. Similarly
s_{2}(\mathfrak{G}_{1})=s_{2}(\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\alpha^{F})=0+1+I\times\frac{1}{2}=\frac{I}{2}+1
and therefore

\displaystyle||\mathfrak{G}_{1}||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}=a^{-\frac{I+2}{2}}|u|^{I+2}||\mathfrak{G}_{1}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}.

(4.53)

In terms of the scale invariant norms, the weighted transport inequality (
4.51
) for
\alpha^{F}

reads

\begin{split}&a^{-\frac{1}{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{I}\alpha^{F}||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&a^{-\frac{1}{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{I}\alpha^{F}||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{sc}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\mathfrak{G}_{1}||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\leq&a^{-\frac{1}{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{I}\alpha^{F}||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{sc}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I+1}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}\\
+&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}+1}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{2}}\alpha^{F}||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}\\
+&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}+1}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\alpha^{F}||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}\\
+&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}\alpha^{F}||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}\\
+&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\alpha^{F}||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}\\
+&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}+1}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}+1}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}\\
+&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\eta\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}\\
+&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\underline{\omega}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\alpha^{F}||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}\\
+&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\widehat{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}\\
+&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\hat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\widehat{\underline{\chi}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\alpha^{F}||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}.\end{split}

(4.54)

We now control each term by means of the bootstrap assumption (
3.1
). Notice that the first term
\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I+1}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}
has the top-most derivative (i.e.,
N+5
) and therefore we bound it in
\mathcal{L}^{2}_{sc}(\underline{H}^{(u_{\infty},u)})
. An application of Cauchy-Schwartz yields

\begin{split}&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I+1}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}\\
