\leq&\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I+1}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}du^{{}^{\prime}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\lesssim&||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I+1}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{sc}(\underline{H}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}\frac{1}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I+1}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{sc}(\underline{H}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lesssim\underline{\mathbb{YM}}[\rho^{F},\sigma^{F}].\end{split}

(4.55)

We estimate the next term:

\begin{split}&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}+1}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{2}}\alpha^{F}||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}\\
=&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}+1}(\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}+\frac{2}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{2}}\alpha^{F}||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}^{2}\lesssim\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{|u|}\lesssim 1\end{split}

(4.56)

due to integrability of
\frac{1}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}
away from zero. Now consider the next term:

\begin{split}&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}+1}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\alpha^{F}||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}\\
=&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}+1}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},(\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}+\frac{2}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}-\frac{2}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}))\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\alpha^{F}||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}\\
\lesssim&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}^{3}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}^{3}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}\Gamma^{3}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\Gamma^{3}\lesssim 1.\end{split}

(4.57)

Similarly, for the next term we have:

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}\alpha^{F}||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{\prime}|^{2}}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}^{4}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}^{3}\lesssim\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{4}}\Gamma^{4}+\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u|^{3}}\Gamma^{3}\lesssim 1.

(4.58)

For the next term:

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\alpha^{F}||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}^{3}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}^{2}\lesssim\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{3}}\Gamma^{3}+\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}\Gamma^{2}\lesssim 1.

(4.59)

We proceed with the seventh term:

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}+1}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}+1}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}^{2}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}\Gamma^{2}\lesssim 1.

(4.60)

For the eighth term:

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\eta\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}^{2}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}^{3}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}\Gamma^{2}+\frac{a}{|u|^{3}}\Gamma^{3}\lesssim 1.

(4.61)

For the ninth term:

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\underline{\omega}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\alpha^{F}||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}^{2}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}^{3}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}\Gamma^{2}+\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u|^{3}}\Gamma^{3}\lesssim 1.

(4.62)

The penultimate term reads

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\widehat{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}^{2}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}^{3}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}\Gamma^{2}+\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u|^{3}}\Gamma^{3}\lesssim 1,

(4.63)

while the last term reads

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\hat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\widehat{\underline{\chi}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\alpha^{F}||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}\frac{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}^{3}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}\frac{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}^{2}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}\Gamma^{3}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\Gamma^{2}\lesssim 1.

(4.64)

Collecting all the terms, we have

\displaystyle a^{-\frac{1}{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{I}\alpha^{F}||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\underline{\mathbb{YM}}[\rho^{F},\sigma^{F}]+1,

(4.65)

since
a^{-\frac{1}{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{I}\alpha^{F}||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{sc}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}\lesssim 1
. This concludes the proof.

\square

Remark 6

For the non-extremal cases
0\leq i\leq N+3
, we have the improved estimate since the topmost (
N+5
) derivatives of
(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})
do not appear in the commuted null evolution equation for
\alpha^{F}

\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{0\leq i\leq N+3}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{i}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim 1,

which implies, by Sobolev embedding, the following
L^{\infty}
estimate:

\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{0\leq i\leq N+1}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{i}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim 1.

This will be useful later on, for example in Proposition
4.6
.

Proposition 4.10

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
3.1
), there holds

\sum_{0\leq i\leq N+4}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{i}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\underline{\mathbb{YM}}[\underline{\alpha}^{F}]+1.

Proof.

We use the null evolution equation

\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\rho^{F}+\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\rho^{F}=-\widehat{\text{div}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}+\left(\eta-\underline{\eta}\right)\cdot\underline{\alpha}^{F},

(4.66)

\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\sigma^{F}+\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\sigma^{F}=-\widehat{\text{curl}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}+\left(\eta-\underline{\eta}\right)\cdot\prescript{*}{}{\underline{\alpha}}^{F}.

(4.67)

and the commutation relation to get the following schematic null evolution equation for the pair
(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})
:

\begin{split}&\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})+(\frac{I}{2}+1)\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\\
=&\widehat{\nabla}^{I+1}\underline{\alpha}^{F}+\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}+1}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{2}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\\
+&\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}+1}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\widehat{\underline{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\\
+&\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\\
+&\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\hskip 1.42262pt\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\\
+&\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}+1}\hat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\\
+&\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}+1}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\\
+&\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\\
+&\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F}),\end{split}

(4.68)

which is compactly written as follows

\displaystyle\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})+(\frac{I}{2}+1)\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})=\mathfrak{G}_{2}.

(4.69)

An application of the weighted transport inequality from Proposition
3.6
yields

|u|^{I+1}||\widehat{\nabla}^{I}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim|u_{\infty}|^{I+1}||\widehat{\nabla}^{I}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{2}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{I+1}||\mathfrak{G}_{2}||_{L^{2}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}.

(4.70)

Recalling the definitions of the scale-invariant norms

||\phi||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}:=a^{-s_{2}(\phi)}|u|^{2s_{2}(\phi)}||\phi||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

we arrive at

\displaystyle||\widehat{\nabla}^{I}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}=a^{-\frac{I+1}{2}}|u|^{I+1}||\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\alpha^{F}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

(4.71)

since
s_{2}(\widehat{\nabla}^{I}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F}))=s_{2}((\rho^{F},\sigma^{F}))+I\times\frac{1}{2}=\frac{I}{2}+\frac{1}{2}
. Similarly
s_{2}(\mathfrak{G}_{2})=s_{2}(\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\rho^{F})=\frac{1}{2}+1+I\times\frac{1}{2}=\frac{I}{2}+\frac{3}{2}
and therefore

\displaystyle||\mathfrak{G}_{2}||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}=a^{-\frac{I+3}{2}}|u|^{I+3}||\mathfrak{G}_{2}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}.

(4.72)

Therefore, the weighted transport inequality from Proposition
3.6
yields

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{I}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{I}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{sc}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\mathfrak{G}_{2}||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{I}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{sc}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I+1}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{2}_{Sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}+1}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{2}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{2}_{Sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}+1}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\widehat{\underline{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\hskip 1.42262pt\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}+1}\hat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}+1}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}.

(4.73)

Now we estimate each term separately.

\begin{split}&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I+1}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{2}_{Sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}\\
\lesssim&\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I+1}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}du^{{}^{\prime}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\underline{\mathbb{YM}}[\underline{\alpha}^{F}]\cdot\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lesssim\underline{\mathbb{YM}}[\underline{\alpha}^{F}],\end{split}

(4.74)

while

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}+1}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{2}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{2}_{Sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}+1}(\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}+\frac{2}{|u|}-\frac{2}{|u|})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{2}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{2}_{Sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}+1}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{2}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{2}_{Sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\cdot\frac{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}{a}\cdot\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|u|}\Gamma^{2}\lesssim 1.

(4.75)

Here we note a crucial point.
\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}
appears in a differentiated (angular) manner and therefore, we can simply replace it by
\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}
allowing us to obtain an integrable factor
\frac{1}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}
. The next term is estimated as follows:

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}+1}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\widehat{\underline{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}^{3}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}^{3}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}\Gamma^{3}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\Gamma^{3}\lesssim 1.

(4.76)

The next term:

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{U_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}^{2}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}\Gamma^{2}\lesssim 1.

(4.77)

The next term is estimated as follows:

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\hskip 1.42262pt\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}^{2}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}^{3}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\Gamma^{2}+\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}\Gamma^{3}\lesssim 1.

(4.78)

For the next terms we have:

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}+1}\hat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}^{3}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\Gamma^{3}\lesssim 1,

(4.79)

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}+1}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}^{3}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}^{3}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\Gamma^{3}\lesssim 1,

(4.80)

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}

(4.81)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}^{2}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{4}}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}^{3}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}\Gamma^{2}+\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{3}}\Gamma^{3}\lesssim 1.

(4.82)

Note that in this term, the true non-linear characteristics of the Yang-Mills equations show up. Finally, we have

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{4}}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}^{3}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{5}}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}^{4}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u|^{3}}\Gamma^{3}+\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{4}}\Gamma^{5}\lesssim 1.

(4.83)

Putting everything together yields the desired estimate, namely

\sum_{0\leq i\leq N+4}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{i}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\underline{\mathbb{YM}}[\underline{\alpha}^{F}]+1.

\square

Remark 7

For the non-extremal cases
0\leq i\leq N+3
, we have the improved estimate since the topmost (
N+5
) derivatives of
\underline{\alpha}^{F}
do not appear in the commuted null evolution equation for
(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})

\sum_{0\leq i\leq N+3}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{i}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim 1+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\lesssim 1,

which implies, by Sobolev embedding, the following
L^{\infty}
estimate:

\sum_{0\leq i\leq N+1}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{i}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim 1.

Proposition 4.11

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
3.1
), there holds

\sum_{0\leq i\leq N+4}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{i}\underline{\alpha}^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\mathbb{YM}[\rho^{F},\sigma^{F}]+\underline{\mathbb{YM}}[\rho^{F},\sigma^{F}]+1.

Proof.

Let us recall the null evolution equation for
\underline{\alpha}^{F}
:

\displaystyle\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\underline{\alpha}^{F}+\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\chi\underline{\alpha}^{F}=-\widehat{\nabla}\rho^{F}+\prescript{*}{}{\widehat{\nabla}}\sigma^{F}-2\prescript{*}{}{\underline{\eta}}\sigma^{F}-2\underline{\eta}\rho^{F}+2\omega\underline{\alpha}^{F}-\underline{\hat{\chi}}\cdot\alpha^{F}.

(4.84)

Commuting with the gauge-covariant operator
\widehat{\nabla}^{I}
(we restrict to
0\leq I\leq N+4
here), using Proposition
3.6
, we get

\displaystyle\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\underline{\alpha}^{F}=

\displaystyle\widehat{\nabla}^{I+1}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}+1}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}+1}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\mbox{tr}\chi\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\underline{\eta}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\sigma^{F},\rho^{F})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\omega\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\widehat{\underline{\chi}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\alpha^{F}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}+1}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}.

(4.85)

Now recall that
s_{2}(\underline{\alpha}^{F})=1
and therefore
s_{2}(\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\underline{\alpha}^{F})=\frac{I}{2}+1
. Similarly
\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\underline{\alpha}^{F}=\frac{I}{2}+1
. Definition of the scale-invariant norms yields

\displaystyle||\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}=a^{-\frac{I+2}{2}}|u|^{I+2}||\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}.

(4.86)

Therefore an application of the scale-invariant transport inequality along
e_{4}
yields

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{I}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{I}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,0})}+\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{I}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,0})}+\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I+1}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}+1}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}+1}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\mbox{tr}\chi\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\underline{\eta}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\sigma^{F},\rho^{F})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\omega\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\ +

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\widehat{\underline{\chi}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\alpha^{F}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}+1}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}.

(4.87)

Now we estimate each term separately. For the first term there holds:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I+1}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}\lesssim\mathbb{YM}[\rho^{F},\sigma^{F}].

(4.88)

The next term is estimated as follows:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}+1}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}+1}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\frac{1}{|u|}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}^{2}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}\lesssim\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{|u|}|\underline{u}|\lesssim 1.

(4.89)

For the third term we have:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\mbox{tr}\chi\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\frac{1}{|u|}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}^{2}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}\lesssim\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{|u|}|\underline{u}|\lesssim 1.

(4.90)

For the fourth term we have:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\underline{\eta}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\sigma^{F},\rho^{F})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\frac{1}{|u|}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}^{2}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}\lesssim\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{|u|}|\underline{u}|\lesssim 1.

(4.91)

For the fifth term there holds:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\omega\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\frac{1}{|u|}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}^{2}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}\lesssim\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{|u|}|\underline{u}|\lesssim 1.

(4.92)

For the next term we have:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\widehat{\underline{\chi}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\alpha^{F}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{I}\underline{\hat{\chi}}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}a^{-\frac{1}{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{I}\alpha^{F}||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}\lesssim\underline{\mathbb{YM}}[\rho^{F},\sigma^{F}]+1,

(4.93)

where we have used Proposition
4.3
to estimate the
\widehat{\underline{\chi}}
term and Proposition
4.9
to estimate the
\alpha^{F}
term.
For the seventh term:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}+1}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}^{3}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}|\underline{u}|}{|u|^{2}}\Gamma^{3}\lesssim 1.

(4.94)

For the eighth term:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}^{3}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}\lesssim\frac{a|\underline{u}|}{|u|^{2}}\Gamma^{3}\lesssim 1.

(4.95)

Finally, for the ninth term we have:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\frac{a}{|u|}a^{-\frac{1}{2}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{I-1}\alpha^{F}||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{I-1}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}+\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}\Gamma^{3}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{I-1}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}+1\lesssim\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{I-1}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}+1

(4.96)

Here we have made use of the improved bounds from the remark on
\alpha^{F}
from Proposition
4.9
, since (
4.3
) does not contain top-order derivatives. This is a crucial point, asserting that the Yang-Mills non-linearities are under control. Collecting all the terms, we have:

\displaystyle\sum_{0\leq i\leq N+4}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{i}\underline{\alpha}^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

(4.97)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathbb{YM}[\rho^{F},\sigma^{F}]+\underline{\mathbb{YM}}[\rho^{F},\sigma^{F}]+1+\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||\sum_{0\leq i\leq N+4}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{i}\underline{\alpha}^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}.

(4.98)

An application of Grönwall’s inequality yields

\sum_{0\leq i\leq N+4}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{i}\underline{\alpha}^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\mathbb{YM}[\rho^{F},\sigma^{F}]+\underline{\mathbb{YM}}[\rho^{F},\sigma^{F}]+1,

since
|\underline{u}|\lesssim 1
. This concludes the proof of the lemma.

\square

Proposition 4.12

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
3.1
), there holds

\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{0\leq i\leq N+2}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{i}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim 1.

Proof.

Because of the fact that there is no null Yang-Mills equation for
\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}
, we commute with
\widehat{\nabla}_{3}
. There holds

\displaystyle\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}+\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}

\displaystyle=\hskip 1.42262pt\psi_{g}\hskip 1.42262pt\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\hskip 1.42262pt\alpha^{F}+(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi})(\psi_{g},\underline{\hat{\chi}})(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})+\nabla(\eta,\underline{\eta})\alpha^{F}+\psi_{g}\widehat{\nabla}(\alpha^{F},\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle+\widehat{\nabla}^{2}\alpha^{F}+\nabla(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi})(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})+(\hat{\chi},\psi_{g})\widehat{\nabla}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})+\alpha^{F}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle+\alpha^{F}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})+\alpha\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\alpha}^{F}+(\rho,\sigma)\alpha^{F}+\beta^{R}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})+(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}

\displaystyle:

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{13}T_{j}.

(4.99)

We commute with
i
angular derivatives, Using (
2.87
). We obtain:

\displaystyle\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}+\frac{i+1}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\psi_{g}\nabla^{i_{4}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{5}}\alpha^{F}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi})\nabla^{i_{4}}(\psi_{g},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{5}}(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}+1}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\alpha^{F}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\alpha^{F},\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}+2}\alpha^{F}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}+1}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}+1}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{5}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\alpha\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\rho,\sigma)\nabla^{i_{4}}\alpha^{F}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\beta^{R}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}+1}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-2}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{5}}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}:=G_{i}.

(4.100)

An application of Proposition
3.6
, expressed in scale-invariant norms, yields

\begin{split}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}G_{i}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}.\end{split}

(4.101)

We restrict attention to
0\leq i\leq N+2
and treat each of the 18 terms separately.

For the first term, we have

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\cdot\frac{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\frac{\Gamma^{3}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{\Gamma^{3}}{\lvert u\rvert}\lesssim 1.

(4.102)

For the second term, we have

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi})\nabla^{i_{4}}(\psi_{g},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\cdot\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert\cdot a^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot\frac{\Gamma^{3}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a\Gamma^{3}}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\lesssim 1.

(4.103)

For the third term, there holds

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}+1}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\lesssim 1.

(4.104)

This is because, for
i_{3}
up to
N+2
, we can control
\nabla^{i_{3}+1}(\eta,\underline{\eta})
in terms of the

\textstyle\mathbb{L}

norm. For the fourth term we have

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\alpha^{F},\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\cdot\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\lesssim 1.

(4.105)

For the fifth term, there holds

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}+2}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\frac{\Gamma}{a\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim 1.

(4.106)

Note, crucially, that for
0\leq i_{3}\leq N+2
we can bound
\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}+2}\alpha^{F}
using the

\textstyle\mathbb{L}

norm. For the sixth and seventh terms, there holds

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}+1}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}+1}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim 1.

(4.107)

For the eighth term, we have

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{5}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\cdot a^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot\frac{\Gamma^{3}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a\Gamma^{3}}{\lvert u\rvert^{3}}\lesssim 1.

(4.108)

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\cdot a^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a\Gamma^{3}}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\lesssim 1.

(4.109)

For the tenth and eleventh terms -and for
i
always up to
N+2
- we have

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\alpha\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\cdot\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lesssim 1

(4.110)

and

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\rho,\sigma)\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\cdot\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim 1.

(4.111)

For the twelfth term, we have

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\beta^{R}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\cdot\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\lesssim 1.

(4.112)

For the thirteenth and fourteenth terms, we have

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}a\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\frac{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert}\lesssim 1.

(4.113)

For the fifteenth and sixteenth terms, we have

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}+1}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-2}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\cdot a^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot\frac{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}{a}\frac{\Gamma^{3}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma^{3}}{\lvert u\rvert}\lesssim 1.

(4.114)

Finally, the seventeenth and eighteenth terms are estimated as follows:

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{5}}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\cdot\frac{\Gamma^{3}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim 1.

(4.115)

Adding the estimates together, we obtain the desired result.

\square

Proposition 4.13

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
3.1
), there holds

\sum_{0\leq i\leq N+2}\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{i}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim 1.

Proof.

As with the preceding Proposition, there is no null Yang-Mills equation for
\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}
and so, to get an estimate, we commute with
\widehat{\nabla}_{4}
. Schematically, we have

\displaystyle\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}=

\displaystyle(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi},\underline{\hat{\chi}})(\psi_{g},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})+\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}+\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}+(\psi_{g},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\nabla(\psi_{g},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}})\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}+(\underline{\alpha},\underline{\beta}^{R},\rho,\sigma)(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})+(\psi_{g},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}+\widehat{\nabla}^{2}\underline{\alpha}^{F}.

(4.116)

Commuting with
i
angular derivatives using (
2.86
), we have

\displaystyle\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\nabla^{i_{4}}(\psi_{g},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{5}}(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{5}}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}+1}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}+1}(\psi_{g},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\Psi_{\underline{u}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}+2}\underline{\alpha}^{F}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-2}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{5}}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}:=H_{i}.

(4.117)

Passing to scale-invariant norms, there holds

\displaystyle\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}H_{i}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}.

(4.118)

From now on, we restrict attention to
0\leq i\leq N+2
. The first term contains a triple anomaly:

\displaystyle\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\nabla^{i_{4}}(\psi_{g},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{5}}(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\frac{\lvert u\rvert}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\frac{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}{a}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{\Gamma^{3}}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\Gamma[\underline{\hat{\chi}}]\Gamma[\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}]\Gamma[\alpha^{F}].

(4.119)

At this point, we can bound the product
\Gamma[\underline{\hat{\chi}}]\Gamma[\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}]\Gamma[\alpha^{F}]
by
1
, using the improvements obtained in Propositions
4.3
,
4.7
and
4.9
respectively.

For the fourth term, there holds

\displaystyle\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}+1}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i+1}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+1=i+1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}+1}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\frac{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}{a}\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lesssim 1.

(4.120)

The rest of the terms are not borderline and can be bounded above by 1 as before. The result follows.

\square

4.4
Estimates on the curvature components

Proposition 4.14

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
3.1
), there holds

\sum_{0\leq i\leq N+2}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\alpha}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim 1.

Proof.

Recall the Bianchi equation for
\alpha
:

\displaystyle\nabla_{3}\alpha+\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\alpha=

\displaystyle\nabla\hat{\otimes}\beta+4\underline{\omega}\alpha-3\left(\hat{\chi}\rho+\prescript{*}{}{\hat{\chi}}\sigma\right)+(\zeta+4\eta)\hat{\otimes}\beta

\displaystyle-D_{4}R_{AB}+\frac{1}{2}\left(D_{B}R_{4A}+D_{A}R_{4B}\right)+\frac{1}{2}\left(D_{4}R_{43}-D_{3}R_{44}\right)\not{g}_{AB}.

(4.121)

Schematically, the above rewrites as

\displaystyle\nabla_{3}\alpha+\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\alpha=

\displaystyle\nabla\beta^{R}+\psi_{g}\alpha+\hat{\chi}(\rho,\sigma)+\psi_{g}\beta^{R}+\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}\widehat{\nabla}\alpha^{F}+(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})\widehat{\nabla}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}

\displaystyle+(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})(\alpha^{F},\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})+\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}.

(4.122)

Commuting with
i
angular derivatives using (
2.87
), we arrive at

\displaystyle\nabla_{3}\nabla^{i}\alpha+\frac{i+1}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{i}\alpha

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\nabla^{i+1}\beta^{R}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}+1}\beta^{R}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\alpha

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi})\nabla^{i_{4}}(\rho,\sigma,\beta^{R})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}+1}\alpha^{F}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}+1}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\alpha^{F},\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{5}}(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}\

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}})\nabla^{i_{4}}\alpha

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{i_{4}}\alpha

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-2}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\nabla^{i_{4}}\alpha:=T_{i}^{1}+\dots+T_{i}^{11}.

(4.123)

Passing to scale-invariant norms and using the weighted transport equality from Proposition
3.6
, we can estimate as follows.

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\alpha}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\alpha\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u_{\infty},u})}+\sum_{1\leq j\leq 12}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}T_{i}^{j}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}.

(4.124)

The expressions for
T_{i}^{1},T_{i}^{2},T_{i}^{3},T_{i}^{4},T_{i}^{9},T_{i}^{10},T_{i}^{11}
do not contain Yang-Mills components and their treatment is exactly the same as in
[
4
]
, bounded by
1
. For the rest of the terms, there holds:

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\left(\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}T_{i}^{5}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}+\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}T_{i}^{6}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\right)\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim 1.

(4.125)

Moreover, there holds

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}T_{i}^{7}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma^{3}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim 1.

(4.126)

For the eighth term, we can bound

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}T_{i}^{8}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lesssim 1.

(4.127)

The result follows.

\square

Proposition 4.15

For
\Psi_{u}
defined as in Section
2.7
, there holds

\sum_{0\leq i\leq N+3}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\Psi_{u}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\mathcal{R}[\alpha]+1.

Proof.

Each of the
\Psi_{u}
satisfies the following schematic equation:

\nabla_{4}\Psi_{u}=\nabla\left(\Psi_{u},\alpha\right)+(\psi,\underline{\hat{\chi}})\left(\Psi_{u},\alpha\right)+(\alpha_{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})\widehat{\nabla}(\alpha_{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})+(\psi,\hat{\chi},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}).

(4.128)

Commuting with
i
angular derivatives using
2.86
, we obtain

\displaystyle\nabla_{4}\nabla^{i}\Psi_{u}=

\displaystyle\nabla^{i+1}(\Psi_{u},\alpha)+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}+1}(\Psi_{u},\alpha)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\psi,\underline{\hat{\chi}})\nabla^{i_{4}}(\Psi_{u},\alpha)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}+1}(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\psi,\hat{\chi},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{5}}(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{i_{4}}\Psi_{u}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-2}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{i_{4}}\Psi_{u}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{i_{5}}\Psi_{u}.

(4.129)

Passing to scale-invariant norms and estimating, we have

\displaystyle\hskip 19.91692pt\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\Psi_{u}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\nabla^{i+1}\alpha}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\nabla^{i+1}\Psi_{u}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}+1}(\Psi_{u},\alpha)}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\nabla^{i_{4}}(\Psi_{u},\alpha)}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}+1}(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\psi,\hat{\chi},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{5}}(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{i_{4}}\Psi_{u}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-2}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{i_{4}}\Psi_{u}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{5}}\Psi_{u}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

(4.130)

We restrict attention to
0\leq i\leq N+3
. For the first term, we have,

\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\nabla^{i+1}\alpha}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\nabla^{i+1}\alpha}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{1/2}=\mathcal{R}[\alpha],

(4.131)

by using Hölder’s inequality. For the second term, since the
\Psi_{u}
are regular with respect to scaling, we conclude that

\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\nabla^{i+1}\Psi_{u}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathcal{R}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}R\lesssim 1,

(4.132)

by the bootstrap assumptions (
3.1
). For the third term, we have
i_{3}+1\leq i
, hence everything can be closed using the

\textstyle\mathbb{L}

total norm. We have

\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}+1}(\Psi_{u},\alpha)}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lesssim\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert}\lesssim 1.

(4.133)

The fourth term can be estimated by
