\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\nabla^{i_{4}}(\Psi_{u},\alpha)}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\frac{\lvert u\rvert}{a}\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim 1.

(4.134)

For the fifth term, we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}+1}(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+1=i+1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}+1}(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\frac{a\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert}\lesssim 1.

(4.135)

For the sixth term (which is the most borderline one), we have the following estimate (the worst possible scenario is when we have the triple anomaly
\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\lvert\alpha^{F}\rvert^{2}
, all other cases are bounded above by
1
as before):

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\psi,\hat{\chi},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{5}}(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\frac{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}{a}\cdot a\cdot\frac{{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}(\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}){\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}(\alpha^{F}){\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}(\alpha^{F})}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}(\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}){\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}(\alpha^{F}){\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}(\alpha^{F}).

(4.136)

Using Remark
6
from Proposition
4.9
, since
0\leq i\leq N+3
, we see an improvement for
{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}(\alpha^{F}),
whence we can bound
{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{\infty}(\alpha^{F})+{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{2}(\alpha^{F})\lesssim 1
. Moreover, for
{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}(\mbox{tr}\chi)
, we make use of Proposition
4.7
to bound
{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}(\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\lesssim 1
. For the seventh term, there holds

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{i_{4}}\Psi_{u}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim 1.

(4.137)

For the eighth term, there holds

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-2}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{i_{4}}\Psi_{u}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}\Gamma^{3}}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim 1.

(4.138)

Finally, for the ninth term, there holds

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{5}}\Psi_{u}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\frac{a\Gamma^{3}}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim 1.

(4.139)

Putting all of the above together, the result follows.

\square

Proposition 4.16

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
3.1
), there holds

\sum_{0\leq i\leq N+2}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\underline{\alpha}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim 1.

Proof.

Recall the Bianchi equation for
\underline{\alpha}
:

\begin{split}\nabla_{4}\underline{\alpha}+\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\chi\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\alpha}=&-\nabla\hat{\otimes}\underline{\beta}+4\omega\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\alpha}-3\left(\underline{\hat{\chi}}\rho-\prescript{*}{}{\underline{\hat{\chi}}}\sigma\right)+\left(\zeta-4\underline{\eta}\right)\hat{\otimes}\underline{\beta}\\
&-D_{3}R_{AB}+\frac{1}{2}\left(D_{A}R_{3B}+D_{B}R_{3A}\right)+\frac{1}{2}\left(D_{3}R_{34}-D_{4}R_{43}\right)\not{g}_{AB}.\end{split}

(4.140)

Schematically, the above rewrites as

\displaystyle\nabla_{4}\underline{\alpha}=\nabla\Psi_{u}+\psi_{g}(\underline{\alpha},\underline{\beta}^{R})+\underline{\hat{\chi}}(\rho,\sigma)+\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}\widehat{\nabla}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}+(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}+\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}.

(4.141)

Commuting with
i
angular derivatives, we arrive at

\displaystyle\hskip 19.91692pt\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\underline{\alpha}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\nabla^{i+1}\underline{\alpha}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\nabla^{i+1}\Psi_{u}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}+1}\Psi_{u}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\nabla^{i_{4}}(\Psi_{\underline{u}})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}+1}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\psi,\hat{\chi},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{5}}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{i_{4}}\underline{\alpha}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-2}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{i_{4}}\underline{\alpha}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{5}}\underline{\alpha}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

(4.142)

In the above expression, all terms except the seventh one can be bounded above by
1
, in the same way as in the preceding Proposition. For the seventh term, we have

\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{\lvert u\rvert}{a}\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim 1,

(4.143)

as the

\textstyle\mathbb{L}

norm bootstraps up to
N+2
angular derivatives of
\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}
. The result follows.

\square

5
Elliptic Estimates for the top-order derivatives of the Ricci coefficients

While estimating the energy norms for curvature, we will need control of the
\mathcal{L}^{2}_{sc}(H,\underline{H})
norms for
N+5
derivatives of the Ricci coefficients. We obtain such estimates by means of the elliptic equations. Let us define the following norm on which we must make a bootstrap assumption:

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}_{N+5,2}:=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}\nabla^{N+5}\widehat{\chi}\|_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{sc}(H^{(0,\underline{u})}_{u})}+\|(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}\nabla^{N+5}(\mbox{tr}\chi,\omega)\|_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{sc}(H^{(0,\underline{u})}_{u})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\|(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}\nabla^{N+5}\eta\|_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{sc}(\underline{H}^{(u_{\infty},u)}_{\underline{u}})}+\frac{a}{|u|}\|(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}\nabla^{N+5}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\|_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{sc}(H^{(0,\underline{u})}_{u})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\|(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}\nabla^{N+5}\underline{\omega}\|_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{sc}(\underline{H}^{(u_{\infty},u)}_{\underline{u}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}\|(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}\nabla^{N+5}\widehat{\underline{\chi}}\|_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}\|(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}\nabla^{N+5}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\|_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}

(5.1)

The bootstrap assumption reads

\mathcal{O}_{N+5,2}\lesssim\Gamma_{\text{top}}\lesssim a^{1/1000}.

(5.2)

Throughout this section, we shall be repeatedly invoking a pivotal Proposition, which lies at the heart of the rationale behind the elliptic estimates. We include a statement and refer the reader to
[
4
]
for a proof.

Proposition 5.1

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
3.1
), let
\phi
be a totally symmetric,
(r+1)-
covariant tensorfield on a two-sphere
(\mathbb{S}^{2},\gamma)
satisfying the following:

\mbox{div }\phi=f,\hskip 5.69054pt\mbox{curl }\phi=g,\hskip 5.69054pt\mbox{tr}\hskip 1.42262pt\phi=h.

Then, for
0\leq i\leq N+5
, there holds:

\displaystyle\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\phi}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim a^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{j=0}^{i-1}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}(f,g)}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\sum_{j=0}^{i-1}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}(\phi,h)}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}.

We begin with the estimates for
\hat{\chi},\hskip 1.42262pt\mbox{tr}\chi
.

Proposition 5.2

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
3.1
) the following estimates hold for the top derivatives of the ricci coefficients
(\mbox{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})

\displaystyle a^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}\nabla^{N+5}\widehat{\chi}\|_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim 1+\mathcal{R}[\tilde{\beta}]+\mathcal{R}[\alpha]+\mathbb{YM}[\alpha^{F}]+\underline{\mathbb{YM}}[\rho^{F},\sigma^{F}],

\displaystyle\|(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}\nabla^{N+5}\mbox{tr}\chi\|_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim 1+\mathcal{R}[\tilde{\beta}]+\mathcal{R}[\alpha]+\mathbb{YM}[\alpha^{F}]+\underline{\mathbb{YM}}[\rho^{F},\sigma^{F}].

Proof.

Recall the null-transport equation for
\mbox{tr}\chi

\displaystyle\nabla_{4}\mbox{tr}\chi+\frac{1}{2}(\mbox{tr}\chi)^{2}=-|\hat{\chi}|^{2}-|\alpha^{F}|^{2}-2\omega\mbox{tr}\chi

(5.3)

and commute with
\widehat{\nabla}^{I}
to obtain

\displaystyle\nabla_{4}\nabla^{I}\mbox{tr}\chi=

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\mbox{tr}\chi\nabla^{J_{4}}\mbox{tr}\chi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\hat{\chi}\nabla^{J_{4}}\hat{\chi}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\alpha^{F}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\omega\nabla^{J_{4}}\mbox{tr}\chi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{J_{4}}\mbox{tr}\chi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}+1}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{J_{4}}\mbox{tr}\chi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I-2}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\alpha^{F}\nabla^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{J_{5}+1}\mbox{tr}\chi.

(5.4)

Applying the transport inequality from Proposition
3.5
with respect to the scale-invariant norm, we have:

\displaystyle||a^{\frac{N+4}{2}}\nabla^{N+5}\mbox{tr}\chi||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle||a^{\frac{N+4}{2}}\nabla^{N+5}\mbox{tr}\chi||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,0})}+\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{N+4}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=N+5}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\mbox{tr}\chi\nabla^{J_{4}}\mbox{tr}\chi||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{N+4}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=N+5}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\hat{\chi}\nabla^{J_{4}}\hat{\chi}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{N+4}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=N+5}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\alpha^{F}\nabla^{J_{4}}\alpha^{F}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{N+4}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=N+5}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\omega\nabla^{J_{4}}\mbox{tr}\chi||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{N+4}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=N+5}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{J_{4}}\mbox{tr}\chi||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{N+4}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=N+4}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{J_{4}}\mbox{tr}\chi||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{N+4}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=N+3}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\alpha^{F}\nabla^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{J_{5}+1}\mbox{tr}\chi||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle||a^{\frac{N+4}{2}}\nabla^{N+5}\mbox{tr}\chi||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,0})}+\frac{a}{|u|}O(\alpha^{F})_{\infty}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}a^{-\frac{1}{2}}||a^{\frac{N+4}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{N+4}\alpha^{F}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}O(\hat{\chi})_{\infty}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{N+4}{2}}\nabla^{N+5}\hat{\chi}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}+1,

(5.5)

where we observe that the most dangerous terms are the ones containing top-order derivatives. The remaining terms are estimated by means of the
L^{2}
connection and Yang-Mills estimates (Propositions
4.8
-
4.9
). Treating the initial data as
O(1)
, we write

\displaystyle||a^{\frac{N+4}{2}}\nabla^{N+5}\mbox{tr}\chi||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}O(\hat{\chi})_{\infty}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{N+4}{2}}\nabla^{N+5}\hat{\chi}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}+\frac{a}{|u|}O(\alpha^{F})_{\infty}\mathbb{YM}[\alpha^{F}].

(5.6)

We now need the
L^{2}_{sc}
estimate for the top derivative of
\hat{\chi}
. We utilize the elliptic equation for
\hat{\chi}
to this end. Once we obtain an
L^{2}_{sc}
estimate for
\hat{\chi}
, the idea is to utilize a Grönwall inequality to complete the estimate for
\mbox{tr}\chi
. Recall the elliptic equation for
\hat{\chi}
:

\displaystyle\text{div}\hat{\chi}=\frac{1}{2}\nabla\mbox{tr}\chi-\frac{1}{2}(\eta-\underline{\eta})\cdot(\hat{\chi}-\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\chi\gamma)-\tilde{\beta}+\frac{1}{2}\mathfrak{T}(e_{4},\cdot).

(5.7)

Apply the elliptic estimate from Proposition

5.1
to obtain

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}\nabla^{N+5}\hat{\chi}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{I\leq N+5}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{I}\mbox{tr}\chi||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\sum_{I\leq N+4}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{I}\tilde{\beta}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{I\leq N+4}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}=I}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\nabla^{J_{2}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{I\leq N+4}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}=I}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{1}}\alpha^{F}\cdot\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{2}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{I\leq N+4}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{I}\hat{\chi}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}.

(5.8)

Now, after substituting (
5
) into (
5
), an application of Cauchy-Schwartz and Grönwall’s inequality together with the
L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})
estimates for the connection and curvature components proved in Section 4, we obtain

\displaystyle||a^{\frac{N+4}{2}}\nabla^{N+5}\mbox{tr}\chi||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+\mathcal{R}[\tilde{\beta}]+\mathcal{R}[\alpha]+\mathbb{YM}[\alpha^{F}]+\underline{\mathbb{YM}}[\rho^{F},\sigma^{F}])e^{|\underline{u}|\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\mathcal{R}[\tilde{\beta}]+\mathcal{R}[\alpha]+\mathbb{YM}[\alpha^{F}]+\underline{\mathbb{YM}}[\rho^{F},\sigma^{F}],

(5.9)

since
|\underline{u}|\lesssim 1
.
Here we have made use of Proposition
4.6
to estimate the lower order norm
\sum_{I\leq N+4}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{I}\mbox{tr}\chi||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}
. Using this estimate, we obtain

\displaystyle a^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}\nabla^{N+5}\widehat{\chi}\|_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim 1+\mathcal{R}[\tilde{\beta}]+\mathcal{R}[\alpha]+\mathbb{YM}[\alpha^{F}]+\underline{\mathbb{YM}}[\rho^{F},\sigma^{F}].

(5.10)

This concludes the proof of the lemma.

\square

Proposition 5.3

Under the assumption of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumption (
3.1
)-(
5.2
), the following estimates for
\omega
hold:

\displaystyle||a^{\frac{N+4}{2}}\nabla^{N+5}\omega||_{L^{2}_{sc}(H_{u}^{0,\underline{u})})}\lesssim 1+\mathcal{R}[\tilde{\beta}].

(5.11)

Proof.

We first introduce
\omega^{{\dagger}}
, defined as the solution of the following equation

\displaystyle\nabla_{3}\omega^{{\dagger}}=\frac{1}{2}\sigma,

(5.12)

with
\omega^{\dagger}=0
on
H_{u_{\infty}}
. We now introduce the new notation
\langle\omega\rangle
for the pair
(-\omega,\omega^{{\dagger}})
and define
\kappa
as follows:

\displaystyle\kappa:=^{*}\mathcal{D}\langle\omega\rangle-\frac{1}{2}\tilde{\beta}=\nabla\omega+^{*}\nabla\omega^{{\dagger}}-\frac{1}{2}\tilde{\beta}.

(5.13)

We intend to obtain a transport equation for
{}^{*}\mathcal{D}\langle\omega\rangle
. We have the following commutation relation:

\displaystyle[\nabla_{3},^{*}\mathcal{D}](-\omega,\omega^{{\dagger}})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}(\nabla\omega+^{*}\nabla\omega^{{\dagger}})+\underline{\hat{\chi}}\cdot(-\nabla\omega+^{*}\nabla\omega^{{\dagger}})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}(\eta+\underline{\eta})\nabla_{3}\omega+\frac{1}{2}(^{*}\eta+^{*}\underline{\eta})\nabla_{3}\omega^{{\dagger}}.

(5.14)

A
\nabla_{3}
equation for
\omega^{{\dagger}}
(
5.12
) has been defined. The
\nabla_{3}
equation for
\omega
reads

\displaystyle\nabla_{3}\omega=2\omega\underline{\omega}+\frac{3}{4}|\eta-\underline{\eta}|^{2}+\frac{1}{4}(\eta-\underline{\eta})\cdot(\eta+\underline{\eta})-\frac{1}{8}|\eta+\underline{\eta}|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\rho+\frac{1}{4}\mathfrak{T}_{43}

which we can write in the following schematic notation

\displaystyle\nabla_{3}\omega=\frac{1}{2}\rho+\psi_{g}\psi_{g}+|(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})|^{2}.

(5.15)

We can further denote the term
\psi_{g}\psi_{g}+|(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})|^{2}
by
F
. An explicit computation yields

\displaystyle\nabla_{3}~^{*}\mathcal{D}\langle\omega\rangle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}(\nabla\rho+^{*}\nabla\sigma)+\nabla F-\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}^{*}\mathcal{D}\langle\omega\rangle+\underline{\hat{\chi}}\cdot(-\nabla\omega+^{*}\nabla\omega^{{\dagger}})

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}(\eta+\underline{\eta})(\frac{1}{2}\rho+F)+\frac{1}{4}(^{*}\eta+^{*}\underline{\eta})\sigma.

(5.16)

This transport equation contains derivatives of the Weyl curvatures
\rho
and
\sigma
, which do not allows us to close the estimates on account of regularity. This is precisely the reason why a modified variable
\kappa
was constructed. Indeed, note the following:

\displaystyle\nabla_{3}\kappa=\nabla_{3}~^{*}\mathcal{D}\langle\omega\rangle-\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{3}\tilde{\beta},

(5.17)

and therefore
\frac{1}{2}(\nabla\rho+^{*}\nabla\sigma)
in
\nabla_{3}~^{*}\mathcal{D}_{1}\langle\omega\rangle
is cancelled point-wise by
-\frac{1}{2}(\nabla\rho+^{*}\nabla\sigma)
in
\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{3}\tilde{\beta}
. Now we denote the Weyl curvature components
(\rho,\sigma,\tilde{\beta})
by
\Psi
and Yang-Mills components
(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})
by
\Upsilon
. Collecting all the terms, we obtain the following evolution equation for
\kappa
:

\displaystyle\nabla_{3}\kappa+\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\kappa=(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi})\Psi+(\psi_{g},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\nabla\psi_{g}+\Upsilon\cdot\widehat{\nabla}(\Upsilon,\alpha^{F})+(\alpha_{F},\Upsilon)\cdot\widehat{\nabla}\Upsilon

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle(\psi_{g},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi},\hat{\chi})\cdot(\alpha_{F},\Upsilon)\cdot\Upsilon+\psi_{g}\psi_{g}\psi_{g}.

(5.18)

Using the commutation lemma (
2.1
), we obtain for
I\leq N+4
(since
\kappa\sim\nabla\omega+\tilde{\beta}
, we only need to estimate up to
N+4
derivatives of
\kappa
):

\displaystyle\nabla_{3}\nabla^{I}\kappa+\frac{I+1}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{I}\kappa

\displaystyle=\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}\psi_{g}^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi})\nabla^{J_{4}}\Psi

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}\psi_{g}^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\psi_{g},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\nabla^{J_{4}+1}\psi_{g}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}\psi_{g}^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\Upsilon\nabla^{J_{4}+1}(\Upsilon,\alpha_{F})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}\psi_{g}^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}(\alpha_{F},\Upsilon)\cdot\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}+1}\Upsilon

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}\psi_{g}^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\psi_{g},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi},\hat{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\alpha_{F},\Upsilon)\cdot\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}\Upsilon

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}\psi_{g}^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\psi_{g}\nabla^{J_{4}}\psi_{g}\nabla^{J_{5}}\psi_{g}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}\psi_{g}^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\psi_{g},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}})\nabla^{J_{4}}\kappa

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+1=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}\psi_{g}^{J_{2}+1}\nabla^{J_{3}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{J_{4}}\kappa:=\mathcal{F}_{1}

(5.19)

Applying the weighted transort inequality from Proposition
3.6
on
\kappa
with
\lambda_{0}=\frac{I+1}{2}
, we get

\displaystyle|u|^{I}||\nabla^{I}\kappa||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim|u_{\infty}|^{I}||\nabla^{I}\kappa||_{L^{2}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{I}||\mathcal{F}_{1}||_{L^{2}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262ptdu^{{}^{\prime}}.

(5.20)

We want to convert this to its scale-invariant form. Noting
s_{2}(\kappa)=s_{2}(\nabla\omega,\tilde{\beta})=\frac{1}{2}
, we have
s_{2}(\nabla^{I}\kappa)=\frac{I+1}{2}
. Therefore

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{I}\kappa||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}=a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}|u|^{I+1}||\nabla^{I}\kappa||_{L^{2}(u,\underline{u})}

(5.21)

and hence

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{I}\kappa||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim

\displaystyle a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}|u|\left(|u_{\infty}|^{I}||\nabla^{I}\kappa||_{L^{2}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{I}||\mathcal{F}_{1}||_{L^{2}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}|u_{\infty}|^{I+1}||\nabla^{I}\kappa||_{L^{2}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}+a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{I+1}||\mathcal{F}_{1}||_{L^{2}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{I}\kappa||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\mathcal{F}_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},u})}du^{{}^{\prime}},

(5.22)

where we have used the fact that
|u^{{}^{\prime}}|\in[u_{\infty},u]
and
|u|\leq|u|_{\infty}
. We now use the expression for
\mathcal{F}_{1}
to estimate term by term:

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{I}\kappa||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{I}\kappa||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}\

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}+J_{6}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}\psi_{g}^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\psi_{g}^{J_{4}}\nabla^{J_{5}}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi})\nabla^{J_{6}}\Psi||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}\psi_{g}^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\psi_{g},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\nabla^{J_{4}+1}\psi_{g}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}\psi_{g}^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\Upsilon\nabla^{J_{4}+1}(\Upsilon,\alpha_{F})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}\psi_{g}^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}(\alpha_{F},\Upsilon)\cdot\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}+1}\Upsilon||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}\psi_{g}^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\psi_{g},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi},\hat{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\alpha_{F},\Upsilon)\cdot\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}\Upsilon||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}\psi_{g}^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\psi_{g}\nabla^{J_{4}}\psi_{g}\nabla^{J_{5}}\psi_{g}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}\psi_{g}^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\psi_{g},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}})\nabla^{J_{4}}\kappa||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+1=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}\psi_{g}^{J_{2}+1}\nabla^{J_{3}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{J_{4}}\kappa:=\mathcal{F}_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}.

(5.23)

Integrating along
\underline{u}
, we have:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{I}\kappa||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{I}\kappa||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}+J_{6}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}\psi_{g}^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\psi_{g}^{J_{4}}\nabla^{J_{5}}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi})\nabla^{J_{6}}\Psi||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}\psi_{g}^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\psi_{g},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\nabla^{J_{4}+1}\psi_{g}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}\psi_{g}^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\Upsilon\nabla^{J_{4}+1}(\Upsilon,\alpha_{F})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}\psi_{g}^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}(\alpha_{F},\Upsilon)\cdot\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}+1}\Upsilon||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}\psi_{g}^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\psi_{g},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi},\hat{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\alpha_{F},\Upsilon)\cdot\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}\Upsilon||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}\psi_{g}^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\psi_{g}\nabla^{J_{4}}\psi_{g}\nabla^{J_{5}}\psi_{g}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}\psi_{g}^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\psi_{g},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}})\nabla^{J_{4}}\kappa||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+1=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}\psi_{g}^{J_{2}+1}\nabla^{J_{3}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{J_{4}}\kappa:=\mathcal{F}_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}.

(5.24)

Now we estimate term by term, as follows (we shall often omit, when there is no confusion, the
\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime},\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}
at the end of integrals, for brevity):

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{I}\kappa||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{I}\widetilde{\beta}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}+\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{I+1}\omega||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}[\tilde{\beta}]+\mathcal{I}_{0}\lesssim 1+\mathcal{R}[\widetilde{\beta}],

(5.25)

by Cauchy-Schwartz, where
I\leq N+5
. The next terms read

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}+J_{6}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}\psi_{g}^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\psi_{g}^{J_{4}}\nabla^{J_{5}}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi})\nabla^{J_{6}}\Psi||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}\mathcal{R},

(5.26)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}\psi_{g}^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\psi_{g},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\nabla^{J_{4}+1}\psi_{g}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\Gamma_{top}+\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}\Gamma_{top}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{1000}}}{|u|}+\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{1}{1000}}}{|u|^{2}}\lesssim 1,

(5.27)

since
|u|<a
. Continuing, we have:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}\psi_{g}^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\Upsilon\nabla^{J_{4}+1}(\Upsilon,\alpha_{F})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}\mathbb{YM}[\Upsilon]+\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}\mathbb{YM}[\alpha^{F}],

(5.28)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}\psi_{g}^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}(\alpha_{F},\Upsilon)\cdot\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}+1}\Upsilon||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}\mathbb{YM}[\Upsilon],

(5.29)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}\psi_{g}^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\psi_{g},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi},\hat{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\alpha_{F},\Upsilon)\cdot\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}\Upsilon||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\Gamma\lesssim 1,

(5.30)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}\psi_{g}^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\psi_{g}\nabla^{J_{4}}\psi_{g}\nabla^{J_{5}}\psi_{g}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{3}}\Gamma\lesssim 1,

(5.31)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}\psi_{g}^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\psi_{g},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}})\nabla^{J_{4}}\kappa||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I}\kappa||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}\right)du^{{}^{\prime}},

(5.32)

while for the final term we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+1=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}\psi_{g}^{J_{2}+1}\nabla^{J_{3}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{J_{4}}\kappa||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I}\kappa||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}\right)du^{{}^{\prime}}.

(5.33)

A collection of all the terms yields

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{I}\kappa||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\mathcal{R}[\widetilde{\beta}]+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I}\kappa||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}\right)du^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I}\kappa||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}\right)du^{{}^{\prime}},

(5.34)

which, after an application of Grönwall’s inequality, yields

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{I}\kappa||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}\lesssim 1+\mathcal{R}[\widetilde{\beta}].

(5.35)

Now recall the elliptic system

\displaystyle\text{div}\nabla\omega=\text{div}\kappa+\frac{1}{2}\nabla\tilde{\beta},

\displaystyle\text{div}\nabla\omega^{{\dagger}}=\text{curl}\kappa+\frac{1}{2}\text{curl}\tilde{\beta}.

(5.36)

We can apply the
\text{div}-\text{curl}
estimates from Proposition
5.1
to obtain

\displaystyle||a^{\frac{N+4}{2}}\nabla^{N+5}\omega||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\sum_{J=0}^{N+4}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{J}\kappa||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\sum_{J=0}^{N+4}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{J}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{J=0}^{N+4}||(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{J}(\omega,\omega^{{\dagger}})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})},

(5.37)

which, after integrating along the
\underline{u}
direction and applying Grönwall’s inequality, yields

\displaystyle||a^{\frac{N+4}{2}}\nabla^{N+5}\omega||_{L^{2}_{sc}(H_{u}^{0,\underline{u})})}\lesssim 1+\mathcal{R}[\tilde{\beta}].

(5.38)

This concludes the proof of the lemma.

\square

Proposition 5.4

Under the assumptions of the main theorem (
1.2
) and the bootstrap assumptions (
5.2
), the following estimates for
\eta
and
\underline{\eta}
hold:

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}||a^{\frac{N+4}{2}}\nabla^{N+5}\eta||_{L^{2}_{sc}(H^{(0,\underline{u})}_{u})}\lesssim 1+\mathcal{R},

\displaystyle||a^{\frac{N+4}{2}}\nabla^{N+5}\eta||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\underline{H}^{(u_{\infty},u)}_{\underline{u}})}\lesssim 1+\mathcal{R},

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}||a^{\frac{N+4}{2}}\nabla^{N+5}\underline{\eta}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(H^{(0,\underline{u})}_{u})}\lesssim 1+\mathcal{R}.

Proof.

In order to prove these estimates, we introduce the mass aspect functions

\displaystyle\mu=-\text{div}~\eta-\rho,\hskip 5.69054pt\underline{\mu}:=-\text{div}~\underline{\eta}-\rho.

(5.39)

We want to obtain a
\nabla_{4}
evolution equation for
\mu
and a
\nabla_{3}
evolution equation for
\underline{\mu}
. We explicitly compute

\displaystyle\nabla_{4}\mu

\displaystyle=-\nabla_{4}\text{div}~\eta-\nabla_{4}\rho

\displaystyle=-\text{div}\nabla_{4}\eta-\nabla_{4}\rho-[\nabla_{4},\text{div}]\eta

\displaystyle=\text{div}(\chi\cdot(\eta-\underline{\eta}))+\text{div}\widetilde{\beta}-\text{div}\widetilde{\beta}-\frac{3}{2}\mbox{tr}\chi\rho+\mathcal{E}_{1}

\displaystyle=\nabla(\mbox{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})(\eta-\underline{\eta})+(\mbox{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\nabla(\eta,\underline{\eta})-\mbox{tr}\chi\rho+\mathcal{E}_{1},

(5.40)

where
\mathcal{E}_{1}
reads

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{1}\sim\left(\widetilde{\beta}+\alpha^{F}\cdot(\rho^{F}+\sigma^{F})\right)\eta+(\mbox{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})(\eta+\underline{\eta})(\eta+\underline{\eta})+\alpha^{F}\cdot(\rho^{F}+\sigma^{F})(\mbox{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})

\displaystyle+(\mbox{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\nabla\eta+\widehat{\nabla}\alpha^{F}\cdot(\rho^{F}+\sigma^{F})+\alpha^{F}\cdot(\widehat{\nabla}\rho^{F}+\widehat{\nabla}\sigma^{F}).

(5.41)

Notice the reason behind constructing the function
\mu
instead of directly working with
\eta
. Since the
\nabla_{4}
equation for
\eta
contains
\widetilde{\beta}
, one needs the same level of regularity of
\widetilde{\beta}
as that of the Ricci coefficients in order to apply the transport inequality therefore the estimates do not close. However, as we have just observed, the spatial derivative of
\widetilde{\beta}
cancels pointwise in the
\nabla_{4}
evolution equation for
\mu
i.e.,
\nabla_{4}
equation for
\mu
only contains algebraic terms in Weyl curvatures. We now commute the
\nabla_{4}
equation for
\mu
with
\nabla^{I},~I=N+4
and get:

\displaystyle\nabla_{4}\nabla^{I}\mu=

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}+1}(\mbox{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\nabla^{J_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\mbox{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\nabla^{J_{4}+1}(\eta,\underline{\eta})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\mbox{tr}\chi\nabla^{J_{4}}\rho

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\widetilde{\beta}\nabla^{J_{4}}\eta

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{J_{5}}\eta

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}(\mbox{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\nabla^{J_{5}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{J_{5}}(\mbox{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}+1}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}+1}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{J_{4}}\mathcal{\eta}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}+1}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{J_{4}}\mathcal{\eta}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{J_{5}}\eta

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\alpha^{F}\nabla^{J_{4}}\mathcal{\eta}.

(5.42)

We now apply the
\nabla_{4}
transport inequality to obtain

\displaystyle||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I}\mu||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I}\mu||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,0})}+\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla_{4}\nabla^{I}\mu||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla_{4}\nabla^{I}\mu||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}},

(5.43)

since
\nabla^{I}\mu|_{\underline{H}_{\underline{u}=0}}=0
due to the data being Minkowski on
\underline{H}_{\underline{u}=0}
(notice that
\nabla_{4}\mu=0
on
\underline{H}_{\underline{u}=0}
). We therefore obtain

\displaystyle||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I}\mu||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}+1}(\mbox{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\nabla^{J_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\mbox{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\nabla^{J_{4}+1}(\eta,\underline{\eta})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\mbox{tr}\chi\nabla^{J_{4}}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\widetilde{\beta}\nabla^{J_{4}}\eta||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{J_{5}}\eta||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}(\mbox{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\nabla^{J_{5}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{J_{5}}(\mbox{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}+1}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}+1}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{J_{4}}\mu||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}+1}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{J_{4}}\mu||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{J_{5}}\mu||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\alpha^{F}\nabla^{J_{4}}\mu||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}.

(5.44)

We estimate each term separately as follows:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}+1}(\mbox{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\nabla^{J_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{|u|a^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{|u|}\lesssim 1,

(5.45)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\mbox{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\nabla^{J_{4}+1}(\eta,\underline{\eta})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{\Gamma\Gamma_{top}}{a}+\frac{\Gamma\Gamma_{top}}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lesssim a^{-1+\frac{1}{1000}}+a^{-\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{1000}}\lesssim 1,

(5.46)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\mbox{tr}\chi\nabla^{J_{4}}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}\lesssim\frac{1}{|u|}\Gamma^{2}\lesssim 1,

(5.47)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\widetilde{\beta}\nabla^{J_{4}}\eta||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}\lesssim\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{|u|}\lesssim 1,

(5.48)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{J_{5}}\eta||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}\Gamma^{3}\lesssim 1,

(5.49)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}(\mbox{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\nabla^{J_{5}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

(5.50)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{\Gamma^{3}}{|u|^{2}}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma^{3}}{|u|^{2}}\lesssim 1,

(5.51)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{J_{5}}(\mbox{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

(5.52)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma^{3}}{|u|^{2}}+\frac{a\Gamma^{3}}{|u|^{2}}\lesssim 1,

(5.53)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}+1}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\mathbb{YM}~\Gamma\lesssim 1,

(5.54)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}+1}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\mathbb{YM}~\Gamma\lesssim 1,

(5.55)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{J_{4}}\mu||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}+\frac{1}{|u|})\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||\nabla^{I}\mu||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}\lesssim\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||\nabla^{I}\mu||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})},

(5.56)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}+1}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{J_{4}}\mu||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}+1}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)(\nabla^{J_{4}+1}\eta+\nabla^{J_{4}}\rho)||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

(5.57)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1+a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}\Gamma^{3}+\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}\Gamma^{3}\lesssim 1,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{J_{5}}\mu||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})(\nabla^{J_{5}+1}\eta+\nabla^{J_{5}}\rho)||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}\Gamma^{3}+\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}\Gamma^{3}\lesssim 1

(5.58)

and finally

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\alpha^{F}\nabla^{J_{4}}\mu||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\alpha^{F}(\nabla^{J_{4}+1}\eta+\nabla^{J_{4}}\rho)||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\Gamma^{2}+\frac{a}{|u|}\mathcal{R}[\alpha]\lesssim 1+\mathcal{R}[\alpha].

Collecting all the terms together and an application of the Grönwall’s inequality yields

\displaystyle||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I}\mu||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim 1+\mathcal{R}[\alpha].

(5.59)

Now the elliptic equation

\displaystyle\text{div}~\eta=-\mu-\rho,\hskip 5.69054pt\text{curl}~\eta=\sigma\epsilon+\frac{1}{2}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\wedge\hat{\chi}

(5.60)

along with Proposition
5.1

yield:

\displaystyle||a^{\frac{N+5}{2}}\nabla^{N+5}\eta||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim a^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{I=0}^{N+4}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I}\mu||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+a^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{I=0}^{N+4}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I}(\rho,\sigma)||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+a^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{I=0}^{N+4}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I}(\underline{\hat{\chi}}\hat{\chi})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\sum_{I=0}^{N+4}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I}\eta||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

(5.61)

which, upon integrating along
\underline{u}
, applying Cauchy-Schwartz and taking into account the estimates from Section 4 for
I\leq N+4
, yield the result

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}||a^{\frac{N+4}{2}}\nabla^{N+5}\eta||_{L^{2}_{sc}(H^{(0,\underline{u})}_{u})}\lesssim 1+\mathcal{R}.

(5.62)

Similarly, the elliptic estimate (
5
) together with the estimate on the mass aspect function and integration along
\underline{H}
yield

\displaystyle||a^{\frac{N+4}{2}}\nabla^{N+5}\eta||_{L^{2}_{sc}(\underline{H}^{(u_{\infty},u)}_{\underline{u}})}\lesssim 1+\mathcal{R}.

(5.63)

We now consider the
\nabla_{3}
evolution equation for
\underline{\mu}
. Explicit calculations yield

\displaystyle\nabla_{3}\underline{\mu}

\displaystyle=-\text{div}\nabla_{3}\underline{\eta}-\nabla_{3}\rho+[\text{div},\nabla_{3}]\underline{\eta}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\text{div}(\underline{\chi}\cdot(\underline{\eta}-\eta))-\text{div}\widetilde{\underline{\beta}}+\text{div}\widetilde{\underline{\beta}}+\frac{3}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\rho+\mathcal{E}_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}(-\text{div}\underline{\eta}-\rho)+\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\rho+\mathcal{E}_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\underline{\mu}+\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\rho+\mathcal{E}_{2},

(5.64)

where
\mathcal{E}_{2}
reads

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{2}\sim(\eta+\underline{\eta})\widetilde{\underline{\beta}}+\widehat{\nabla}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\cdot(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})+\underline{\alpha}^{F}\cdot\widehat{\nabla}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})+\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}(\rho^{F}\cdot\rho^{F}+\sigma^{F}\cdot\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle+\widetilde{\underline{\beta}}\underline{\eta}+\underline{\alpha}^{F}\cdot(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})(\underline{\eta},\eta)+(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})(\eta-\underline{\eta})(\eta-\underline{\eta})+(\eta,\underline{\eta})\widetilde{\underline{\beta}}.

(5.65)

Commuting the operator
\nabla^{I}
for
I=N+4
with the
\nabla_{3}
evolution equation for
\mu
yields

\displaystyle\nabla_{3}\nabla^{I}\underline{\mu}+\frac{I+2}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{I}\mu=

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\nabla^{J_{4}}\widetilde{\underline{\beta}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{J_{4}}\rho

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}+1}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\nabla^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\nabla^{J_{4}+1}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{J_{5}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\nabla^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{J_{5}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\nabla^{J_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\nabla^{J_{5}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\nabla^{J_{4}}\widetilde{\underline{\beta}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\hskip 1.42262pt\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}})\nabla^{J_{4}}\underline{\mu}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}+1}\hat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{J_{4}}\underline{\mu}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}+1}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\underline{\mu}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}\underline{\mu}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\underline{\mu}.

(5.66)

We now apply the transport inequality in the
e_{3}
direction with
\lambda_{0}=\frac{I+2}{2}
(equivalently
\lambda_{1}=I+1
) which gives

\displaystyle|u|^{I+1}||\nabla^{I}\underline{\mu}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim|u_{\infty}|^{I+1}||\nabla^{I}\underline{\mu}||_{L^{2}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{I+1}||\nabla_{3}\nabla^{I}\underline{\mu}||_{L^{2}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}.

(5.67)

Now recall that
s_{2}(\underline{\mu})=\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{2}=1,

s_{2}(\nabla^{I}\underline{\mu})=\frac{I}{2}+1
, and
s_{2}(\nabla_{3}\nabla^{I}\underline{\mu})=\frac{I}{2}+2
and therefore

\displaystyle||\nabla^{I}\underline{\mu}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}=a^{-\frac{I}{2}-1}|u|^{I+2}||\nabla^{I}\underline{\mu}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}.

(5.68)

The scale invariant transport inequality reads

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I}\underline{\mu}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u_{\infty}|}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I}\underline{\mu}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla_{3}\nabla^{I}\underline{\mu}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u_{\infty}|}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I}\underline{\mu}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\nabla^{J_{4}}\widetilde{\underline{\beta}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{J_{4}}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}+1}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\nabla^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\nabla^{J_{4}+1}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{J_{5}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\nabla^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{J_{5}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\nabla^{J_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\nabla^{J_{5}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\nabla^{J_{4}}\widetilde{\underline{\beta}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\hskip 1.42262pt\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}})\nabla^{J_{4}}\underline{\mu}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}+1}\hat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{J_{4}}\underline{\mu}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}+1}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\underline{\mu}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}\underline{\mu}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\underline{\mu}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}.

(5.69)

We now estimate each term separately as follows:

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\nabla^{J_{4}}\widetilde{\underline{\beta}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim 1+\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{3}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{6}}du^{{}^{\prime}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I}\widetilde{\underline{\beta}}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}}\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim 1,

(5.70)

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{J_{4}}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}\lesssim 1+\frac{a}{|u|}\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I}\rho||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\mathcal{R}[\rho],

(5.71)

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}+1}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\nabla^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{3}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{6}}du^{{}^{\prime}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I+1}\alpha^{F}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim 1,

(5.72)

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\nabla^{J_{4}+1}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u|^{\frac{5}{2}}}\mathbb{YM}[\rho^{F},\sigma^{F}]\lesssim 1,

(5.73)

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{J_{5}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}\Gamma\lesssim 1,

(5.74)

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\nabla^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{J_{5}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{4}}\Gamma\lesssim 1,

(5.75)

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\nabla^{J_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\nabla^{J_{5}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}\Gamma\lesssim 1,

(5.76)

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\nabla^{J_{4}}\widetilde{\underline{\beta}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u|^{\frac{5}{2}}}\mathcal{R}[\widetilde{\underline{\beta}}]\lesssim 1,

(5.77)

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\hskip 1.42262pt\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}})\nabla^{J_{4}}\underline{\mu}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I}\underline{\mu}||||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}},

(5.78)

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}+1}\hat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{J_{4}}\underline{\mu}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}\left(||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I}\underline{\eta}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}+||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I-1}\rho||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}\right)du^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}\Gamma+\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}\Gamma\lesssim 1,

(5.79)

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}+1}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\underline{\mu}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}\Gamma+\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}\Gamma\lesssim 1,

(5.80)

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}\underline{\mu}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{4}}+\frac{a^{\frac{5}{2}}}{|u|^{4}}\Gamma\lesssim 1.

(5.81)

Collecting all the terms and an application of Grönwall yields

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I}\underline{\mu}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+\frac{a}{|u_{\infty}|}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I}\underline{\mu}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\mathcal{R}[\rho])e^{\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\frac{a}{|u_{\infty}|}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I}\underline{\mu}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\mathcal{R}[\rho]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\mathcal{R}[\rho].

(5.82)

Now we utilize the elliptic equations

\displaystyle\text{div}~\underline{\eta}=-\underline{\mu}-\rho,~\text{curl}~\underline{\eta}=-\sigma\epsilon-\underline{\hat{\chi}}\wedge\hat{\chi}

(5.83)

to obtain, using Proposition
5.1
,

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}||a^{\frac{N+4}{2}}\nabla^{N+5}\underline{\eta}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{I=0}^{N+4}\left(\frac{a}{|u|}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I}\underline{\mu}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I}(\rho,\sigma)||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I}(\underline{\hat{\chi}}\underline{\chi})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+a^{-\frac{1}{2}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I}\underline{\eta}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\right),

(5.84)

which, after integrating along the
e_{4}
direction, yields

(5.85)

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}||a^{\frac{N+4}{2}}\nabla^{N+5}\underline{\eta}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(H^{(0,\underline{u})}_{u})}\lesssim 1+\mathcal{R}.

The claim follows.

\square

Proposition 5.5

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
3.1
)-(
5.2
), there hold:

\displaystyle\|(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}\nabla^{N+5}\underline{\omega}\|_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{sc}(\underline{H}^{(u_{\infty},u)}_{\underline{u}})}\lesssim 1+\mathcal{R}+\mathbb{YM}.

(5.86)

Proof.

Similarly to the
\omega
estimates, we define the auxiliary entity
\underline{\omega}^{{\dagger}}
to be zero on
\underline{H}_{0}
whilst verifying the following
\nabla_{4}
equation:

\displaystyle\nabla_{4}\underline{\omega}^{{\dagger}}=\frac{1}{2}\sigma.

(5.87)

Now we define
\underline{\kappa}
as follows

\displaystyle\underline{\kappa}=-\nabla\underline{\omega}+~^{*}\nabla\underline{\omega}^{{\dagger}}-\frac{1}{2}\widetilde{\underline{\beta}}.

(5.88)

Recall the transport equation for
\underline{\omega}

\displaystyle\nabla_{4}\underline{\omega}=2\omega\underline{\omega}+\frac{3}{4}|\eta-\underline{\eta}|^{2}-\frac{1}{4}(\eta-\underline{\eta})\cdot(\eta+\underline{\eta})-\frac{1}{8}|\eta+\underline{\eta}|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\rho+\frac{1}{4}\mathfrak{T}_{43}.

(5.89)

An explicit computation yields

\displaystyle\nabla_{4}\underline{\kappa}

\displaystyle=-\nabla\nabla_{4}\underline{\omega}+^{*}\nabla\nabla_{4}\underline{\omega}^{{\dagger}}-\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{4}\widetilde{\underline{\beta}}+[\nabla,\nabla_{4}]\underline{\omega}+[\nabla_{4},~^{*}\nabla]\underline{\omega}^{{\dagger}}
