\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}\nabla\rho+\frac{1}{2}\nabla\sigma-\frac{1}{2}(-\nabla\rho+\prescript{*}{}{\nabla}\sigma)+\mathcal{E}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{E},

(5.90)

where
\mathcal{E}
reads

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}\sim

\displaystyle\underline{\omega}\nabla\omega+\omega\nabla\underline{\omega}+(\eta,\underline{\eta})\nabla(\eta,\underline{\eta})+\rho^{F}\cdot\widehat{\nabla}\rho^{F}+\sigma^{F}\cdot\widehat{\nabla}\sigma^{F}+\widetilde{\beta}(\underline{\omega},\underline{\omega}^{{\dagger}})+\alpha^{F}\cdot(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})(\underline{\omega},\underline{\omega}^{{\dagger}})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle(\eta,\underline{\eta})\omega\underline{\omega}(\eta\eta+\eta\underline{\eta}+\underline{\eta}\underline{\eta})+(\eta,\underline{\eta})(\rho,\sigma)+(\eta,\underline{\eta})|\rho^{F},\sigma^{F}|^{2}+(\mbox{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\nabla(\underline{\omega},\underline{\omega}^{{\dagger}})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle(\mbox{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\underline{\eta}(\underline{\omega},\underline{\omega}^{{\dagger}})+(\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\alpha^{F}\cdot(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})+(\mbox{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\underline{\alpha}^{F}\cdot(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})+\mbox{tr}\chi\widetilde{\underline{\beta}}+\omega\widetilde{\underline{\beta}}+\underline{\hat{\chi}}\widetilde{\beta}.

(5.91)

We now commute
\nabla^{I},I=N+4
with the
\nabla_{4}
evolution equation for
\underline{\kappa}

to obtain

\displaystyle\nabla_{4}\nabla^{I}\underline{\kappa}=

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\underline{\omega}\nabla^{J_{4}+1}\omega

(5.92)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}+1}\underline{\omega}\nabla^{J_{4}}\omega

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\nabla^{J_{4}+1}(\eta,\underline{\eta})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\rho^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}+1}\rho^{F}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\sigma^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}+1}\sigma^{F}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\widetilde{\beta}\nabla^{J_{4}}(\underline{\omega},\underline{\omega}^{{\dagger}})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{J_{5}}(\underline{\omega},\underline{\omega}^{{\dagger}})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\nabla^{J_{4}}(\omega,\underline{\omega})\nabla^{J_{5}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\nabla^{J_{4}}(\rho,\sigma)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\mbox{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}+1}(\underline{\omega},\underline{\omega}^{{\dagger}})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\mbox{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\nabla^{J_{4}}\underline{\eta}\nabla^{J_{5}}(\underline{\omega},\underline{\omega}^{{\dagger}})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\mbox{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\mbox{tr}\chi\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\widetilde{\underline{\beta}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\omega\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\widetilde{\underline{\beta}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\widetilde{\underline{\beta}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{J_{4}}\underline{\kappa}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}+1}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{J_{4}}\underline{\kappa}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{J_{5}}\underline{\kappa}.

(5.93)

Now we apply the scale invariant
\nabla_{4}
transport inequality

\displaystyle||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I}\kappa||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

(5.94)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I}\kappa||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,0})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\underline{\omega}\nabla^{J_{4}+1}\omega||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}+1}\underline{\omega}\nabla^{J_{4}}\omega||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\nabla^{J_{4}+1}(\eta,\underline{\eta})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\rho^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}+1}\rho^{F}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\sigma^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}+1}\sigma^{F}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\widetilde{\beta}\nabla^{J_{4}}(\underline{\omega},\underline{\omega}^{{\dagger}})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{J_{5}}(\underline{\omega},\underline{\omega}^{{\dagger}})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\nabla^{J_{4}}(\omega,\underline{\omega})\nabla^{J_{5}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\nabla^{J_{4}}(\rho,\sigma)||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\mbox{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}+1}(\underline{\omega},\underline{\omega}^{{\dagger}})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\mbox{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\nabla^{J_{4}}\underline{\eta}\nabla^{J_{5}}(\underline{\omega},\underline{\omega}^{{\dagger}})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\mbox{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\mbox{tr}\chi\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\widetilde{\underline{\beta}})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\omega\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\widetilde{\underline{\beta}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\widetilde{\underline{\beta}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{J_{4}}\underline{\kappa}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}+1}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{J_{4}}\underline{\kappa}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{J_{5}}\underline{\kappa}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}.

(5.95)

Now we estimate each term separately. We have

\displaystyle||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I}\kappa||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,0})}\lesssim\mathcal{I}^{0}\lesssim 1,

(5.96)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\underline{\omega}\nabla^{J_{4}+1}\omega||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\frac{1}{|u|}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I+1}\omega||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}\lesssim 1,

(5.97)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}+1}\underline{\omega}\nabla^{J_{4}}\omega||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\frac{1}{|u|}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I+1}\underline{\omega}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}},

(5.98)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\nabla^{J_{4}+1}\eta||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\frac{1}{a}\frac{a}{|u|}||a^{\frac{N+4}{2}}\nabla^{N+5}\eta||_{L^{2}_{sc}(H^{(0,\underline{u})}_{u})}\lesssim 1+\frac{1}{a}\mathcal{R},

(5.99)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\nabla^{J_{4}+1}\underline{\eta}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\frac{1}{a}\frac{a}{|u|}||a^{\frac{N+4}{2}}\nabla^{N+5}\underline{\eta}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(H^{(0,\underline{u})}_{u})}\lesssim 1+\frac{1}{a}\mathcal{R},

(5.100)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\rho^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}+1}\rho^{F}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\frac{1}{|u|}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I+1}\rho^{F}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(H^{(0,\underline{u})}_{u})}\lesssim 1+\frac{\mathbb{YM}}{|u|},

(5.101)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\sigma^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}+1}\sigma^{F}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\frac{1}{|u|}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I+1}\sigma^{F}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(H^{(0,\underline{u})}_{u})}\lesssim 1+\frac{\mathbb{YM}}{|u|},

(5.102)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\widetilde{\beta}\nabla^{J_{4}}(\underline{\omega},\underline{\omega}^{{\dagger}})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\frac{1}{|u|}(\mathcal{R}+\Gamma)\lesssim 1+\frac{\mathcal{R}}{|u|},

(5.103)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{J_{5}}(\underline{\omega},\underline{\omega}^{{\dagger}})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}\Gamma\lesssim 1,

(5.104)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\nabla^{J_{4}}(\omega,\underline{\omega})\nabla^{J_{5}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}\Gamma\lesssim 1,

(5.105)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\nabla^{J_{4}}(\rho,\sigma)||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\frac{1}{|u|}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I}(\rho,\sigma)||_{L^{2}_{sc}(H^{(0,\underline{u})}_{u})}\lesssim 1+\frac{1}{|u|}\mathcal{R}[\rho,\sigma],

(5.106)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}\Gamma\lesssim 1,

(5.107)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\mbox{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}+1}(\underline{\omega},\underline{\omega}^{{\dagger}})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I+1}(\underline{\omega},\underline{\omega}^{{\dagger}})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}},

(5.108)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\mbox{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\nabla^{J_{4}}\underline{\eta}\nabla^{J_{5}}(\underline{\omega},\underline{\omega}^{{\dagger}})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}\Gamma\lesssim 1,

(5.109)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\Gamma\lesssim 1,

(5.110)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\mbox{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\Gamma\lesssim 1,

(5.111)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\mbox{tr}\chi\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\widetilde{\underline{\beta}})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}\lesssim 1+\frac{1}{|u|}\mathcal{R}[\widetilde{\underline{\beta}}]

(5.112)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\omega\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\widetilde{\underline{\beta}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}\lesssim 1+\frac{1}{|u|}\mathcal{R}[\widetilde{\underline{\beta}}],

(5.113)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\widetilde{\underline{\beta}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}\lesssim 1+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathcal{R}[\widetilde{\underline{\beta}}],

(5.114)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{J_{4}}\underline{\kappa}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I}\underline{\kappa}||||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}},

(5.115)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}+1}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{J_{4}}\underline{\kappa}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I}\underline{\kappa}||||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

(5.116)

and finally

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{J_{5}}\underline{\kappa}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I}\underline{\kappa}||||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}.

(5.117)

Here we have utilized the elliptic estimate for
\omega
from proposition (
5.3
). In addition, the
L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})
norm of
\nabla^{J}\underline{\omega}^{{\dagger}}
for
J\leq N+4
may be estimated directly from the evolution equation (
5.87
) along with the trivial boundary condition for
\underline{\omega}^{{\dagger}}
along
\underline{H}_{0}
. Collecting all the terms together, we have

\displaystyle||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I}\underline{\kappa}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I+1}(\underline{\omega},\underline{\omega}^{{\dagger}})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I}\underline{\kappa}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I}\underline{\kappa}||||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}+\mathbb{YM}+\mathcal{R},

(5.118)

which, upon using Grönwall’s inequality, yields

\displaystyle||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I}\underline{\kappa}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim 1+\mathbb{YM}+\mathcal{R}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I+1}(\underline{\omega},\underline{\omega}^{{\dagger}})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}},

(5.119)

due to the fact that
|\underline{u}|\lesssim 1
. We now notice that the elliptic equations

\displaystyle-\nabla\underline{\omega}+~^{*}\nabla\underline{\omega}^{{\dagger}}=-\underline{\kappa}-\frac{1}{2}\widetilde{\underline{\beta}}

(5.120)

yield the
\text{div}-\text{curl}
system

\displaystyle\text{div}\nabla\underline{\omega}=-\text{div}\underline{\kappa}-\frac{1}{2}\text{div}\widetilde{\underline{\beta}},~\text{curl}\nabla\underline{\omega}=0,

(5.121)

\displaystyle\text{curl}\underline{\omega}^{{\dagger}}=\text{curl}\underline{\kappa}+\frac{1}{2}\text{curl}\widetilde{\underline{\beta}},~\text{div}\nabla\underline{\omega}^{{\dagger}}=0.

(5.122)

An application of the elliptic estimates and transport estimates for
\underline{\kappa}
yield

\displaystyle||a^{\frac{I+1}{2}}\nabla^{I+1}(\underline{\omega},\underline{\omega}^{{\dagger}})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{J\leq N+4}||a^{\frac{J}{2}}\nabla^{J}\underline{\kappa}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\sum_{J\leq N+4}||a^{\frac{J}{2}}\nabla^{J}\widetilde{\underline{\beta}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J\leq N+4}||a^{\frac{J}{2}}\nabla^{J}(\underline{\omega},\underline{\omega}^{{\dagger}})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\mathbb{YM}+\mathcal{R}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I+1}(\underline{\omega},\underline{\omega}^{{\dagger}})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

(5.123)

and therefore

\displaystyle||a^{\frac{I+1}{2}}\nabla^{I+1}(\underline{\omega},\underline{\omega}^{{\dagger}})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim 1+\mathbb{YM}+\mathcal{R}.

(5.124)

Now an integration of the square of the previous inequality (
5.124
) with respect to the measure
\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}
yields

\displaystyle\|(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}\nabla^{N+5}\underline{\omega}\|_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{sc}(\underline{H}^{(u_{\infty},u)}_{\underline{u}})}\lesssim 1+\mathcal{R}+\mathbb{YM}.

(5.125)

This concludes the proof of the lemma.

\square

Proposition 5.6

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
3.1
)-(
5.2
), there hold:

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}\nabla^{N+5}\underline{\hat{\chi}}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim 1,

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}\nabla^{N+5}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\mathcal{R}+\underline{\mathcal{R}}+\mathbb{YM}+1.

Proof.

We start the proof by recalling the
\nabla_{3}
transport equation for
\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}

\displaystyle\nabla_{3}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}+\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}=\frac{2}{|u|^{2}}(\Omega^{-1}-1)+|\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}|^{2}+2\underline{\omega}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}-|\underline{\hat{\chi}}|^{2}-|\underline{\alpha}^{F}|^{2}.

(5.126)

We now commute
\nabla^{I},I=N+5
with
\nabla_{3}
:

\displaystyle\nabla_{3}\nabla^{I}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}+(\frac{I}{2}+1)\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\Omega^{-1}-1)

(5.127)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}\nabla^{J_{4}}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\underline{\omega}\nabla^{J_{4}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\nabla^{J_{4}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\hskip 1.42262pt\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}})\nabla^{J_{4}}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}+1}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}+1}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\nabla^{J_{4}}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{J_{5}}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\nabla^{J_{4}}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}:=\mathcal{E}.

(5.128)

An application of the weighted transport inequality from Proposition
3.6
yields

\displaystyle|u|^{I+1}||\nabla^{I}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim|u_{\infty}|^{I+1}||\nabla^{I}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}||_{L^{2}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{I+1}||\mathcal{E}||_{L^{2}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}.

(5.129)

We now calculate the relevant signatures. We have
s_{2}(\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}})=1
and therefore
s_{2}(\nabla^{I}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}})=\frac{I}{2}+1
and
s_{2}(\mathcal{E})=s_{2}(\nabla_{3}\nabla^{I}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}})=\frac{I}{2}+2
. Therefore

\displaystyle|u|^{I+1}||\nabla^{I}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle a^{\frac{I}{2}+1}|u|^{-(I+2)}||\nabla^{I}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})},

(5.130)

\displaystyle|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{I+1}||\mathcal{E}||_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle a^{\frac{I}{2}+2}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{-(I+4)}||\mathcal{E}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}.

(5.131)

The transport inequality in terms of the scale invariant norm reads
8
8

8

The factor of
a/|u|
is kept with
||\nabla^{I}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u_{\infty}|}a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}||\nabla^{I}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}

(5.132)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\Omega^{-1}-1)||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}\nabla^{J_{4}}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\underline{\omega}\nabla^{J_{4}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\nabla^{J_{4}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\hskip 1.42262pt\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}})\nabla^{J_{4}}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}+1}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}+1}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\nabla^{J_{4}}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{J_{5}}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}.

(5.133)

Now we estimate each term separately as follows:

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u_{\infty}|}a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}||\nabla^{I}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}})}\lesssim 1,

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\frac{1}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\omega\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle 1+\mathcal{\underline{\mathcal{R}}},

(5.135)

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}\nabla^{J_{4}}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\nabla^{I}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}},

(5.136)

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\underline{\omega}\nabla^{J_{4}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\nabla^{I}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||\nabla^{I}\underline{\omega}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\nabla^{I}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}du^{{}^{\prime}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\nabla^{I}\underline{\omega}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u})}}du^{{}^{\prime}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\nabla^{I}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}+\mathcal{R}+\mathbb{YM},

(5.137)

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\nabla^{J_{4}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\nabla^{I}\underline{\hat{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})},

(5.138)

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{3}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{6}}du^{{}^{\prime}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}}\underline{u})}}du^{{}^{\prime}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|u|^{\frac{5}{2}}}\mathbb{YM}[\underline{\alpha}^{F}]\lesssim 1,

(5.139)

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\hskip 1.42262pt\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}})\nabla^{J_{4}}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\nabla^{I}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\nabla^{I}\underline{\hat{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\nabla^{I}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}},

(5.140)

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}+1}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\nabla^{J_{4}}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{-2}\Gamma\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim 1,

(5.141)

and for the last term we have

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{J_{5}}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{4}}\Gamma\lesssim\frac{a}{|u|^{3}}\Gamma\lesssim 1.

(5.142)

A collection of all the terms yields

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}||\nabla^{I}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\mathcal{R}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\nabla^{I}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}

(5.143)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\nabla^{I}\underline{\hat{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\nabla^{I}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\nabla^{I}\underline{\hat{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\nabla^{I}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}.

(5.144)

An application of Grönwall’s inequality leads to

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}||\nabla^{I}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(1+\mathcal{R}+\underline{\mathcal{R}}+\mathbb{YM}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\nabla^{I}\underline{\hat{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\nabla^{I}\underline{\hat{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}\right)e^{\frac{a}{|u|^{2}}+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}+\frac{1}{|u|}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\mathcal{R}+\underline{\mathcal{R}}+\mathbb{YM}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\nabla^{I}\underline{\hat{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\nabla^{I}\underline{\hat{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}.

(5.145)

Now we return to the elliptic constraint equation

\displaystyle\text{div}\hat{\underline{\chi}}\sim\frac{1}{2}\nabla\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}-\frac{1}{2}(\underline{\eta}-\eta)\cdot(\hat{\underline{\chi}}-\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\gamma)-\widetilde{\underline{\beta}}+\underline{\alpha}^{F}\cdot(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})

(5.146)

which yields

\displaystyle||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\nabla^{I}\underline{\hat{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\sum_{J\leq I-1}||a^{\frac{J}{2}}\nabla^{I+1}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\sum_{J\leq I-1}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I}\widetilde{\underline{\beta}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}

(5.147)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{J\leq I-1}||a^{\frac{J}{2}}\nabla^{J}\underline{\hat{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\sum_{J\leq I-1}||a^{\frac{J}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}=J}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\nabla^{J_{2}}(\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi},\underline{\hat{\chi}})||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}.

Now substitute the estimate for
\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}
from (
5
) into (
5.147
) and apply Grönwall to obtain

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u|}a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}||\nabla^{I}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim(1+\mathcal{R}+\underline{\mathcal{R}}+\mathbb{YM})e^{\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}+\frac{1}{|u|}}\lesssim 1+\mathcal{R}+\underline{\mathcal{R}}+\mathbb{YM}.

(5.148)

Now (
5.147
) yields

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\nabla^{I}\underline{\hat{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}\sum_{J\leq I-1}||a^{\frac{J}{2}}\nabla^{I+1}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}\sum_{J\leq I-1}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I}\widetilde{\underline{\beta}}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+\mathcal{R}+\underline{\mathcal{R}}+\mathbb{YM})\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}du^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{4}}du^{{}^{\prime}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}\sum_{J\leq I-1}||a^{\frac{I}{2}}\nabla^{I}\widetilde{\underline{\beta}}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+1\lesssim 1.

This concludes the proof of the lemma.

\square

Remark 8

In the same way as above, one can obtain the following estimates:

\begin{split}&\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}\nabla^{N+5}\underline{\hat{\chi}}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
+&\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{3}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}\nabla^{N+5}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\mathcal{R}+\underline{\mathcal{R}}+1.\end{split}

(5.149)

6
Energy Estimates Weyl and Yang-Mills curvature components

In this section we finish the final estimates, namely those on the energy associated with the Weyl and Yang-Mills curvature. There are several ways to execute this step. Since both the Einstein and the Yang-Mills equations are derivable from a Lagrangian, one would expect the existence of stress-energy tensors. For pure gravity, however, one cannot simply find a canonical stress-energy tensor due to the equivalence principle. Nevertheless, one can utilize the Bel-Robinson tensor to construct suitable energy currents or use the quasi-local type energy constructed by
[
33
,
34
]
. In the case of Yang-Mills theory, there exists a canonical stress-energy tensor that can be used directly to construct necessary energies. However, in the current context where we impose a double-null foliation, the use of Bel-Robionson or Yang-Mills stress energy tensors can be avoided altogether, since, in this setting, both the Bianchi and the Yang-Mills equations can be cast into a coupled symmetric hyperbolic system, as we have already shown in Proposition
2.1
. Therefore, we can use the direct integration by parts approach. To this end, we require the following pair of propositions which we prove using Lemma
2.2
and Proposition
2.1
.

Proposition 6.1

If
\varphi\in\text{section}(^{k}\otimes T^{*}S_{u,\underline{u}}\otimes\mathfrak{P}_{Ad,\mathfrak{g}})
and
\lambda_{1}=2\lambda_{0}-1
, then the following estimates hold:

\displaystyle 2\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2\lambda_{1}}\langle\varphi,(\widehat{\nabla}_{3}+\lambda_{0}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\varphi\rangle

\displaystyle=\int_{H_{u}(0,\underline{u})}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\varphi|^{2}-\int_{H_{u_{\infty}}(0,\underline{u})}|u_{\infty}|^{2\lambda_{1}}|\varphi|^{2}

\displaystyle+\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2\lambda_{1}}f|\varphi|^{2},

where
f
verifies the estimate
f\lesssim\Gamma|u|^{-2}
.

Proof.

The proof follows in an exact same way as that of proposition (7.3) of
[
4
]
. The only difference is that
\varphi
is a section of the fibered vector bundle
{}^{k}\otimes T^{*}S_{u,\underline{u}}\otimes\mathfrak{P}_{Ad,\mathfrak{g}}
. However, note that
|\varphi|^{2}
is a gauge-invariant object and therefore

\displaystyle\nabla_{3}|\varphi|^{2}=2\langle\varphi,\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\varphi\rangle,

(6.1)

due to the fact that the gauge covariant connection
\widehat{\nabla}_{3}
is metrics compatible.

\square

We will need Proposition
6.1
for proving the following proposition.

Proposition 6.2

For a Bianchi pair
(\Psi_{1},\Psi_{2})\in\left\{(\alpha,\widetilde{\beta}),(\widetilde{\beta},(\rho,\sigma)),((\rho,\sigma),\widetilde{\underline{\beta}}),(\widetilde{\underline{\beta}},\underline{\alpha})\right\}\cup\left\{(\alpha_{F},(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})),((\rho^{F},\sigma^{F}),\underline{\alpha}^{F})\right\}
satisfying

\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\Psi_{1}+\left(\frac{i+1}{2}+s_{2}(\Psi_{1})\right)\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\Psi_{1}-\widehat{\mathcal{D}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\Psi_{2}=P_{i},

\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\Psi_{2}-\prescript{*}{}{\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\Psi_{1}=Q_{i},

the following holds true:

\begin{split}&\int_{H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})}}\lVert{\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\Psi_{1}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\int_{\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)}}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\Psi_{2}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{H_{u_{\infty}}^{(0,\underline{u})}}\lVert{\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\Psi_{1}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\int_{\underline{H}_{0}^{(u_{\infty},u)}}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\Psi_{2}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},0})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\\
&+\iint_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\Psi_{1},P_{i}\rangle}\rVert_{L^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\iint_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\Psi_{2},Q_{i}\rangle}\rVert_{L^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}.\end{split}

(6.2)

Proof.

This property essentially indicates the symmetric hyperbolic character of the null Yang-Mills equations. First note the following relation:

\displaystyle\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\Psi_{1},\widehat{\mathcal{D}}\Psi_{2}\rangle+\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\Psi_{2},~^{*}\widehat{\mathcal{D}}\Psi_{2}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}\nabla\langle\Psi_{1},\Psi_{2}\rangle-\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle^{*}\widehat{\mathcal{D}}\Psi_{1},\Psi_{2}\rangle+\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\Psi_{2},^{*}\widehat{\mathcal{D}}\Psi_{1}\rangle-\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})\langle\Psi_{1},\Psi_{2}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})\langle\Psi_{1},\Psi_{2}\rangle,

due to the fact that
\langle\Psi_{1},\Psi_{2}\rangle
is gauge-invariant (see Lemma
2.2
). Similar identities hold for the higher derivatives as well:

\displaystyle\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{1},\widehat{\mathcal{D}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2}\rangle+\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2},~^{*}\widehat{\mathcal{D}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2}\rangle=-\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{1},\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2}\rangle.

(6.3)

With these identities in mind, we apply Proposition
6.1
with
\lambda_{0}=\frac{I+1}{2}+s_{2}(\Psi_{1})
and
\lambda_{1}=2\lambda_{0}-1=I+2s_{2}(\Psi_{2})
to yield

\displaystyle 2\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{1},(\widehat{\nabla}_{3}+(\frac{I+1}{2}+s_{2}(\Psi_{1}))\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{1}\rangle=

\displaystyle\int_{H_{u}(0,\underline{u})}|u|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{1}|^{2}-\int_{H_{u_{\infty}}(0,\underline{u})}|u_{\infty}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|\nabla^{I}\Psi_{1}|^{2}+\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}f|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\psi_{1}|^{2},

(6.4)

where
f
verifies
|f|\lesssim\Gamma|u|^{-2}
. For
\Psi_{2}
we have a simpler expression using the integration lemma:

\displaystyle 2\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2},\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2}\rangle=\int_{\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}(u_{\infty},u)}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2}|^{2}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\int_{\underline{H}_{0}(u_{\infty},u)}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2}|^{2}+\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}(2\omega-\mbox{tr}\chi)|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2}|^{2}.

(6.5)

Adding the previous two equations together we get

\displaystyle\int_{H_{u}(0,\underline{u})}|u|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{1}|^{2}+\int_{\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}(u_{\infty},u)}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2}|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{H_{u_{\infty}}(0,\underline{u})}|u_{\infty}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|\nabla^{I}\Psi_{1}|^{2}+\int_{\underline{H}_{0}(u_{\infty},u)}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2}|^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 2\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{1},(\widehat{\nabla}_{3}+(\frac{I+1}{2}+s_{2}(\Psi_{1}))\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{1}\rangle

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 2\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2},\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2}\rangle-\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}f|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}(2\omega-\mbox{tr}\chi)|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2}|^{2}.

(6.6)

Now we utilize the equations of motion to yield

\displaystyle\int_{H_{u}(0,\underline{u})}|u|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{1}|^{2}+\int_{\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}(u_{\infty},u)}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2}|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{H_{u_{\infty}}(0,\underline{u})}|u_{\infty}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|\nabla^{I}\Psi_{1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{\underline{H}_{0}(u_{\infty},u)}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2}|^{2}+2\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{1},\widehat{\mathcal{D}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2}+P\rangle

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 2\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2},~^{*}\widehat{\mathcal{D}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{1}+Q\rangle-\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}f|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}(2\omega-\mbox{tr}\chi)|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2}|^{2}.

(6.7)

An application of (
6.3
) yields

\displaystyle\int_{H_{u}(0,\underline{u})}|u|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{1}|^{2}+\int_{\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}(u_{\infty},u)}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2}|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{H_{u_{\infty}}(0,\underline{u})}|u_{\infty}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|\nabla^{I}\Psi_{1}|^{2}+\int_{\underline{H}_{0}(u_{\infty},u)}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2}|^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 2\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{1},P\rangle

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 2\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}\langle(\eta+\underline{\eta})\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{1},\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2}\rangle+2\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2},Q\rangle

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}f|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{1}|^{2}-\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}(2\omega-\mbox{tr}\chi)|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2}|^{2}.

(6.8)

Now notice that
P
and
Q
are lower order terms. In this integration by parts procedure, all the principal terms are cancelled. Using the definition of the scale invariant norms and the boot-strap assumptions (
3.1
), we observe that
|\omega|\lesssim|u|^{-1},|\eta,\underline{\eta}|\lesssim|u|^{-2},~|f|\lesssim|u|^{-2}
, and
|\mbox{tr}\chi|\lesssim|u|^{-1}
. We need to apply Grönwall’s inequality twice. Notice the following important points

\displaystyle|\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}(2\omega-\mbox{tr}\chi)|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2}|^{2}|\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}|u|^{-1}\int_{\underline{H}(u^{\infty},u)}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2}|^{2}|,

(6.9)

\displaystyle|\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}\langle(\eta+\underline{\eta})\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{1},\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2}\rangle|\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{-2}\int_{H(0,\underline{u})}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}|u|^{-2}\int_{\underline{H}(u_{\infty},u)}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2}|^{2},

(6.10)

\displaystyle|\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}f|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{1}|^{2}|\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{-2}\int_{H(0,\underline{u})}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{1}|^{2}

(6.11)

since
0>u^{{}^{\prime}}\in[u_{\infty},u]
. Therefore applying Grönwall in
u^{{}^{\prime}}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{H_{u}(0,\underline{u})}|u|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{1}|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\bigg(\int_{H_{u_{\infty}}(0,\underline{u})}|u_{\infty}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|\nabla^{I}\Psi_{1}|^{2}+\int_{\underline{H}_{0}(u_{\infty},u)}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2}|^{2}

(6.12)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 2|\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{1},P\rangle|+2|\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2},Q\rangle|

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}|u|^{-1}\int_{\underline{H}(u^{\infty},u)}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2}|^{2}|\bigg)e^{\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{-2}du^{{}^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\bigg(\int_{H_{u_{\infty}}(0,\underline{u})}|u_{\infty}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|\nabla^{I}\Psi_{1}|^{2}+\int_{\underline{H}_{0}(u_{\infty},u)}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2}|^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 2|\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{1},P\rangle|+2|\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2},Q\rangle|

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}|u|^{-1}\int_{\underline{H}(u^{\infty},u)}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2}|^{2}|\bigg),

due to integrability of
|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{-2}
at infinity. Now we need to apply the Grönwall in the
\underline{u}
direction. We obtain

\displaystyle\int_{\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}(u_{\infty},u)}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2}|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\bigg(\int_{H_{u_{\infty}}(0,\underline{u})}|u_{\infty}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|\nabla^{I}\Psi_{1}|^{2}+\int_{\underline{H}_{0}(u_{\infty},u)}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2}|^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 2|\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{1},P\rangle|+2|\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2},Q\rangle|\bigg)e^{\frac{\underline{u}}{|u|}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{H_{u_{\infty}}(0,\underline{u})}|u_{\infty}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|\nabla^{I}\Psi_{1}|^{2}+\int_{\underline{H}_{0}(u_{\infty},u)}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2}|^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 2|\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{1},P\rangle|+2|\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2},Q\rangle|.

Putting everything together, we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{H_{u}(0,\underline{u})}|u|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{1}|^{2}+\int_{\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}(u_{\infty},u)}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2}|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{H_{u_{\infty}}(0,\underline{u})}|u_{\infty}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|\nabla^{I}\Psi_{1}|^{2}+\int_{\underline{H}_{0}(u_{\infty},u)}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2}|^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle|\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{1},P\rangle|+|\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2},Q\rangle|.

(6.13)

In order to convert this inequality in terms of scale invariant norms, we need to recall the signature of each term involved and the signature difference between
\Psi_{1}
and
\Psi_{2}
. Firstly note the following

\displaystyle s_{2}(\Psi_{2})=s_{2}(\Psi_{1})+\frac{1}{2},~s_{2}(\nabla^{I}\Psi_{1})=\frac{I}{2}+s_{2}(\Psi_{1}),~s_{2}(P)=s_{2}(\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{1})=\frac{I}{2}+1+s_{2}(\Psi_{1})

\displaystyle s_{2}(Q)=s_{2}(~^{*}\widehat{\mathcal{D}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{1})=\frac{I+1}{2}+s_{2}(\Psi_{1}),

(6.14)

which imply

\displaystyle s_{2}(\nabla^{I}\Psi_{2})=\frac{I}{2}+s_{2}(\Psi_{2})=\frac{I+1}{2}+s_{2}(\Psi_{1}).

(6.15)

We consider each term separately:

\displaystyle\int_{H_{u}(0,\underline{u})}|u|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{1}|^{2}=\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}|u|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}||\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}|u|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}a^{I+2s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|u|^{-2I-4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}||\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}a^{I+2s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}||\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}},

(6.16)

\displaystyle\int_{\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}(u_{\infty},u)}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2}|^{2}=\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}||\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}a^{I+1+2s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|u|^{-2(I+1)-4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}||\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}a^{I+1+2s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{-2}||\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}},

(6.17)

\displaystyle\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{1},P\rangle|=\int_{u,\underline{u}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}||\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{1},P\rangle||_{L^{1}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{u,\underline{u}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}a^{I+1+2s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{-2I-1-4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}||\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{1},P\rangle||_{L^{1}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{u,\underline{u}}a^{I+1+2s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{-1}||\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{1},P\rangle||_{L^{1}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}},

(6.18)

and

\displaystyle\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2},Q\rangle|=\int_{u,\underline{u}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}||\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2},Q\rangle||_{L^{1}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{u,\underline{u}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2I+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}a^{I+1+2s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{-2I-1-4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}||\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2},Q\rangle||_{L^{1}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{u,\underline{u}}a^{I+1+2s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{-1}||\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2},Q\rangle||_{L^{1}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}.

(6.19)

Collecting all the terms yield

\begin{split}&\int_{H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})}}\lVert{\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{1}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\int_{\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)}}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{H_{u_{\infty}}^{(0,\underline{u})}}\lVert{\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{1}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\int_{\underline{H}_{0}^{(u_{\infty},u)}}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},0})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\\
+&\iint_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{1},P\rangle}\rVert_{L^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\iint_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2},Q\rangle}\rVert_{L^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}.\end{split}

(6.20)

\square

Before embarking on the energy estimates, we provide a final helpful proposition, which can be found for example in
[
12
]
.

Proposition 6.3

Let
f(x,y),\hskip 1.42262ptg(x,y)
be positive functions defined on a rectangle
U:=\begin{Bmatrix}0\leq x\leq x_{0},\hskip 1.42262pt0\leq y\leq y_{0}\end{Bmatrix}.
Suppose there exist constants
J,c_{1},c_{2}
such that

f(x,y)+g(x,y)\lesssim J+c_{1}\int_{0}^{x}f(x^{\prime},y)\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}x^{\prime}+\int_{0}^{y}g(x,y^{\prime})\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}y^{\prime},

for all
(x,y)\in U
. Then there holds

\forall(x,y)\in U:\hskip 14.22636ptf(x,y)+g(x,y)\lesssim J\mathrm{e}^{c_{1}x+c_{2}y}.

6.1
Energy Estimates for the Yang-Mills components

We begin with the pair
(\alpha^{F},\ (\rho^{F},\sigma^{F}))
.

Proposition 6.4

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions, there holds, for
0\leq i\leq N+5
:

\begin{split}&\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}\\
\lesssim&\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u_{\infty}}^{(0,\underline{u})})}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{0}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}+1.\end{split}

(6.21)

Proof.

We consider the schematic equations for
\alpha^{F},(-\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})
:

\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\alpha^{F}+\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\alpha^{F}-\mathcal{D}(-\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})=\eta(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})+\underline{\omega}\alpha^{F}+\hat{\chi}\underline{\alpha}^{F},

(6.22)

\widehat{\nabla}_{4}(-\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})-\prescript{*}{}{\mathcal{D}}\alpha^{F}=\mbox{tr}\chi(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})+(\eta,\underline{\eta})\alpha^{F}.

(6.23)

Commuting with
i
angular derivatives, we arrive at the following two:

\begin{split}&\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\alpha^{F}+\frac{i+1}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\alpha^{F}-\mathcal{D}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}(-\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\\
=&\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}+1}(-\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\eta\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\underline{\omega}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\alpha^{F}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\hat{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\alpha^{F}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\alpha^{F}\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-2}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\alpha^{F}\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{5}}\alpha^{F}\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\alpha^{F}:=A_{i},\end{split}

(6.24)

\begin{split}&\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}(-\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})-\prescript{*}{}{\mathcal{D}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\alpha^{F}\\
=&\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}+1}\alpha^{F}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\mbox{tr}\chi\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\alpha^{F}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{5}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-2}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{5}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F}):=B_{i}.\end{split}

(6.25)

Making use of Proposition
6.2
, we arrive at

\begin{split}&\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{2}+\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}^{2}\\
\lesssim&\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u_{\infty}}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{2}+\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{0}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}^{2}\\
+&\iint_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{\langle(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\alpha^{F},(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}A_{i}\rangle}\rVert_{L^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
+&\iint_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{\langle(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F}),(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}B_{i}\rangle}\rVert_{L^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}.\end{split}

(6.26)

Call the last two spacetime integrals above
\widetilde{N}
and
\widetilde{M}
respectively. For the first one, we have, using Hölder’s inequality,

\begin{split}\widetilde{N}=&\iint_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{\langle(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\alpha^{F},(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}A_{i}\rangle}\rVert_{L^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}A_{i}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\cdot\sup_{u^{\prime}}\lVert(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\alpha^{F}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u^{\prime}}^{(0,\underline{u})})}.\end{split}

(6.27)

We shall work on the term

H_{i}:=\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}A_{i}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

The term
A_{i}
comprises nine summands, as seen in (
6.24
). We bound each of the corresponding terms in in
H_{i}
individually.

For the first term:

\begin{split}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}+1}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime},\end{split}

(6.28)

we observe the following. If
i_{3}+1=N+5
, we estimate
\psi_{g}
in
L^{\infty}_{(sc)}
and
(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}\widehat{\nabla}^{N+5}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})
in
L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u^{\prime}}^{(0,\underline{u})})
. Otherwise, we can bound the entire term using estimates on the

\textstyle\mathbb{L}

norm. Therefore we estimate, using the bootstrap assumptions:

\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}+1}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{\Gamma^{2}M^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}+\frac{\Gamma^{4}}{a^{2}\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}.

(6.29)

Similarly, for the second term, we have

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\eta\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}\eta\widehat{\nabla}^{N+5}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
+&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{N+5}\eta}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\frac{\Gamma^{4}}{a\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\\
\lesssim&\frac{\Gamma^{2}M^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}+\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\frac{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}{a^{2}}\cdot\Gamma_{\text{top}}^{2}+\frac{\Gamma^{4}}{a\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}},\end{split}

(6.30)

where in the last line we have utilised the bootstrap assumption

\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}\nabla^{N+5}\eta\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u^{\prime}}^{(0,\underline{u})})}\lesssim\Gamma_{\text{top}}.

For the third term, we have

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\underline{\omega}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}\underline{\omega}\widehat{\nabla}^{N+5}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
+&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}\alpha^{F}\nabla^{N+5}\underline{\omega}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\frac{\Gamma^{4}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\\
\lesssim&\frac{a\Gamma^{2}M^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}+\frac{\Gamma^{4}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}+\frac{a\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}\nabla^{N+5}\underline{\omega}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}.\end{split}

(6.31)

For the fourth term, we have

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\hat{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}\hat{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{N+5}\underline{\alpha}^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
+&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\nabla^{N+5}\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\frac{\Gamma^{4}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\\
\lesssim&\frac{a\Gamma^{2}\Gamma_{\text{top}}^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}+\frac{\Gamma^{4}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}+\frac{a\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}\widehat{\nabla}^{N+5}\underline{\alpha}^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}.\end{split}

(6.32)

In the fifth term, the worst term that is encountered is
