\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\widehat{\nabla}^{N+5}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
+&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}\alpha^{F}\nabla^{N+5}\underline{\hat{\chi}}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\frac{\Gamma^{4}}{a}\\
\lesssim&\Gamma^{2}[\underline{\hat{\chi}}]M^{2}[\alpha^{F}]+\frac{\Gamma^{4}}{a}+\frac{a\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}\nabla^{N+5}\underline{\hat{\chi}}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}.\end{split}

(6.33)

For the sixth term, we have

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\frac{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}{a^{2}}\cdot\frac{\Gamma^{6}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}\lesssim\frac{\Gamma^{6}}{a^{2}},\end{split}

(6.34)

since there are at most
N+4
derivatives in the expression, thus each term is controllable under the
{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}-
norm. For the seventh term, we have

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-2}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\frac{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}{a^{2}}\cdot a\cdot\frac{\Gamma^{6}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}\lesssim\frac{\Gamma^{6}}{a}\lesssim 1.\end{split}

(6.35)

The eighth and ninth terms are similarly controlled as follows:

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{5}}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
+&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\Gamma^{6}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}+\frac{a\hskip 1.42262pt\Gamma^{4}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}.\end{split}

(6.36)

Putting equations (
6.28
)-(
6.36
) together, we arrive at

\begin{split}H_{i}\lesssim 1+\Gamma[\underline{\hat{\chi}}]M[\alpha^{F}]&+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\Gamma}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}\nabla^{N+5}(\underline{\omega},\underline{\hat{\chi}})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
&+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\Gamma}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}\widehat{\nabla}^{N+5}\underline{\alpha}^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.\end{split}

(6.37)

The terms above involving integrals cannot be controlled along outgoing null hypersurfaces, but nevertheless, going back to (
6.27
), we see that

\begin{split}&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\cdot\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}\nabla^{N+5}(\underline{\omega},\underline{\hat{\chi}})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}\nabla^{N+5}(\underline{\omega},\underline{\hat{\chi}})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\lesssim&\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{\lvert u\rvert^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\mathcal{R}+\underline{\mathcal{R}}+1\right)\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma R}{\lvert u\rvert^{\frac{1}{2}}},\end{split}

(6.38)

where we have made use of Hölder’s inequality, the bootstrap assumptions as well as (
5.149
). Similarly, we have

\begin{split}&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\cdot\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}\widehat{\nabla}^{N+5}\underline{\alpha}^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}\widehat{\nabla}^{N+5}\underline{\alpha}^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\lesssim&\frac{a\hskip 1.42262pt\Gamma}{\lvert u\rvert^{\frac{3}{2}}}\hskip 1.42262pt\sup_{0\leq\underline{u}\leq 1}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}\widehat{\nabla}^{N+5}\underline{\alpha}^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}\lesssim\frac{a\hskip 1.42262pt\Gamma\hskip 1.42262ptM}{\lvert u\rvert^{\frac{3}{2}}}\lesssim 1.\end{split}

(6.39)

Putting everything together, we get

\widetilde{N}\lesssim(\Gamma[\underline{\hat{\chi}}]M[\alpha^{F}]+\Gamma R+1)\cdot\sup_{u^{\prime}}\lVert(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\alpha^{F}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u^{\prime}}^{(0,\underline{u})})}.

(6.40)

Moving on to
\widetilde{M}
,
we similarly obtain

\begin{split}\widetilde{M}=&\iint_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{\langle(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\alpha^{F},(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}B_{i}\rangle}\rVert_{L^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}B_{i}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\cdot\sup_{u^{\prime}}\lVert(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}^{\prime}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}.\end{split}

(6.41)

We now work on the spacetime integral in the inequality above. Here, there holds

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}+1}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}+1}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\cdot\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\cdot\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}\widehat{\nabla}^{N+5}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}+\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\cdot\frac{\Gamma^{4}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}\cdot\left(\frac{1}{a}\lVert(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}\widehat{\nabla}^{N+5}\alpha^{F}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u^{\prime}}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{2}\right)+1\lesssim\frac{a^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\Gamma^{2}\hskip 1.42262ptM[\alpha^{F}]^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert^{3}}+1\lesssim 1.\end{split}

(6.42)

The second term is contained in the fourth, so we skip it for now. For the third term, there holds

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\cdot\frac{a\hskip 1.42262pt\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}\widehat{\nabla}^{N+5}(\eta,\underline{\eta})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
+&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\cdot\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}\widehat{\nabla}^{N+5}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\cdot\frac{\Gamma^{4}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}\cdot\frac{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}{a^{2}}\cdot\Gamma_{\text{top}}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a\hskip 1.42262pt\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}\cdot a\cdot M[\alpha^{F}]^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}+1\lesssim\frac{\Gamma^{2}\Gamma_{\text{top}}^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert}+\frac{a^{2}\Gamma^{2}M[\alpha^{F}]^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert^{3}}+1\\
\lesssim&1.\end{split}

(6.43)

For the fourth term, we have

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\cdot\frac{a\hskip 1.42262pt\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{a}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}\widehat{\nabla}^{N+5}\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\\
+&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\cdot\frac{\hskip 1.42262pt\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}\widehat{\nabla}^{N+5}\mbox{tr}\chi}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\\
+&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}\widehat{\nabla}^{N+5}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}+\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\frac{\Gamma^{4}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\frac{a^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\Gamma^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\Gamma_{\text{top}}^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert^{3}}+\frac{a\Gamma^{2}\Gamma_{\text{top}}^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert^{3}}+\frac{a^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\Gamma^{2}\hskip 1.42262ptM[\rho^{F},\sigma^{F}]^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert^{3}}+\frac{a\hskip 1.42262pt\Gamma^{4}}{\lvert u\rvert^{3}}\lesssim 1.\end{split}

(6.44)

For the fifth term, we have

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-2}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\cdot\frac{a\hskip 1.42262pt\Gamma^{6}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim 1.\end{split}

(6.45)

The last two terms are, similarly, bounded above by
1
. Consequently, one has

\widetilde{M}\lesssim\sup_{u^{\prime}}\lVert(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}^{\prime}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}.

(6.46)

Taking (
6.40
) and (
6.46
) into account and multiplying throughout by
a^{-1}
, we get

\begin{split}&a^{-1}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{2}+a^{-1}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}^{2}\\
\lesssim&a^{-1}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u_{\infty}}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{2}+a^{-1}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{0}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}^{2}+a^{-1}(\widetilde{N}+\widetilde{M})\\
\lesssim&a^{-1}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u_{\infty}}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{2}+a^{-1}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{0}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}^{2}\\
+&a^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\Gamma\hskip 1.42262ptM+\Gamma\hskip 1.42262ptR+1)\cdot M+a^{-\frac{1}{2}}\cdot M\\
\lesssim&a^{-1}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u_{\infty}}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{2}+a^{-1}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{0}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}^{2}+1.\end{split}

(6.47)

By taking the roots, we translate the above into the desired conclusion in a straightforward fashion. The result follows.

\square

Proposition 6.5

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
3.1
), (
5.2
), we have, for
0\leq i\leq N+5
:

\begin{split}&\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}+\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\underline{\alpha}^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}\\
\lesssim&\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u_{\infty}}^{(0,\underline{u})})}+\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\underline{\alpha}^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{0}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}+1.\end{split}

(6.48)

Proof.

The proof follows along the same lines as the preceding Proposition. We begin with the schematic equations for
((-\rho^{F},\sigma^{F}),\underline{\alpha}^{F}):

\begin{split}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}(-\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})+\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}(-\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})-\mathcal{D}\underline{\alpha}^{F}=(\eta,\underline{\eta})\cdot\underline{\alpha}^{F},\end{split}

(6.49)

\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\underline{\alpha}^{F}-\prescript{*}{}{\mathcal{D}}(-\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})=(\mbox{tr}\chi,\omega)\underline{\alpha}^{F}+(\eta,\underline{\eta})(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})+\underline{\hat{\chi}}\hskip 1.42262pt\alpha^{F}.

(6.50)

Commuting these two equations with
i
gauge-covariant angular derivatives we arrive at

\begin{split}&\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}(-\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})+\frac{i+1}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}(-\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})-\mathcal{D}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\underline{\alpha}^{F}=\\
&\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}+1}\underline{\alpha}^{F}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(-\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(-\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-2}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(-\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{5}}(-\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(-\rho^{F},\sigma^{F}):=\Delta_{i}\end{split}

(6.51)

\begin{split}&\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\underline{\alpha}^{F}-\prescript{*}{}{\mathcal{D}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}(-\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\\
=&\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}+1}(-\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\mbox{tr}\chi,\omega)\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla(\eta,\underline{\eta})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\nabla^{i_{4}}\alpha^{F}\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-2}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{5}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}:=E_{i}.\end{split}

(6.52)

We thus arrive, using Proposition
6.2
, at

\begin{split}&\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{2}+\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\underline{\alpha}^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}^{2}\\
\lesssim&\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u_{\infty}}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{2}+\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\underline{\alpha}^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{0}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}^{2}+\Xi_{i}+O_{i},\end{split}

(6.53)

where

\Xi_{i}:=\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\Delta_{i}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

(6.54)

and

O_{i}:=\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}E_{i}(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\underline{\alpha}^{F}}\rVert_{L^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}.

(6.55)

Focusing on
\Xi_{i}
, we have

\begin{split}&\hskip 8.53581pt\Xi_{i}\\
\lesssim&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\Delta_{i}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\Delta_{i}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\Delta_{i}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot\sup_{u_{\infty}\leq u^{\prime}\leq u}\lVert(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u^{\prime}}^{(0,\underline{u})})}.\end{split}

(6.56)

For the first term of the spacetime integral in the line above, as in previous Propositions, we have

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}+1}\underline{\alpha}^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\cdot\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\underline{\alpha}^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
+&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\cdot\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\nabla^{i-1}(\eta,\underline{\eta})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\frac{a\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}.\end{split}

(6.57)

Secondly, we have

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\cdot\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\underline{\alpha}^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
&+\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\cdot\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}(\eta,\underline{\eta})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\cdot\frac{\Gamma^{4}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
&\lesssim\frac{a\Gamma^{2}(\Gamma^{2}+\Gamma_{\text{top}}^{2})}{\lvert u\rvert^{3}}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}.\end{split}

(6.58)

For the third term, the most borderline case is when we have

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(-\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\cdot\frac{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}{a}\cdot\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}(-\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
+&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\nabla^{i}\underline{\hat{\chi}}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+1\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}},\end{split}

(6.59)

where we have used the bootstrap assumptions (
3.1
) as well as equation (
5.149
) from Remark
8
.

Out of the fourth and fifth terms, the most borderline one is the fifth. We estimate it as follows:

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-2}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\cdot\frac{\Gamma^{6}}{a}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}.\end{split}

(6.60)

The sixth and seventh terms are similarly bounded above by
a^{-\frac{1}{2}}
, noting that since they involve up to
i-1
derivatives, no elliptic estimates are involved in their estimation. Finally, we have

\Xi_{i}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{4}}}\cdot\sup_{u_{\infty}\leq u^{\prime}\leq u}\lVert(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u^{\prime}}^{(0,\underline{u})})}\lesssim 1,

(6.61)

by the bootstrap assumptions (
3.1
). Finally, for
O_{i}
, we have:

\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}E_{i}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot\sup_{0\leq\underline{u}\leq 1}\lVert(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}^{\prime}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}.

(6.62)

For the first term in the spacetime integral above, we estimate

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}+1}(-\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\cdot\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}(-\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+1\\
\lesssim&\frac{aM^{2}\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert^{3}}+1\lesssim 1.\end{split}

(6.63)

The second and third terms can be similarly bounded above by
1
using just the bootstrap assumptions (
3.1
) and (
5.2
). The most dangerous term is the fourth one. Indeed, distinguishing between those terms which are top-order and those who are not, we have

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\cdot\frac{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}{a}\frac{\Gamma[\underline{\hat{\chi}}]^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
+&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}\Gamma[\alpha^{F}]^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\underline{\hat{\chi}}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
+&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\cdot\frac{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}{a}\cdot\frac{\Gamma^{2}[\underline{\hat{\chi}}]\Gamma^{2}[\alpha^{F}]}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\Gamma[\underline{\hat{\chi}}]^{2}\underline{\mathbb{YM}}[\alpha^{F}]^{2}+\Gamma[\alpha^{F}]^{2}\left(\mathcal{R}+\underline{\mathcal{R}}+1\right)+1\\
\lesssim&\underline{\mathbb{YM}}[\alpha^{F}]^{2}+\left(\mathcal{R}+\underline{\mathcal{R}}+1\right)^{2}+1\lesssim\left(\mathcal{R}+\underline{\mathcal{R}}+1\right)^{2}.\end{split}

(6.64)

The remaining terms are not of top order and hence can be bounded above by 1 in a by now standard way. Ultimately, we have

\begin{split}O_{i}\lesssim&\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}E_{i}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}^{\prime}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\lesssim&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}E_{i}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}^{\prime}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&(\mathcal{R}+\underline{\mathcal{R}}+1)^{2}+\frac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}^{\prime}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}.\end{split}

(6.65)

Combining equations (
6.53
), (
6.61
) and (
6.65
), we arrive at

\begin{split}&\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{2}+\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\underline{\alpha}^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}^{2}\\
\lesssim&\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u_{\infty}}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{2}+\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\underline{\alpha}^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{0}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}^{2}\\
+&(\mathcal{R}+\underline{\mathcal{R}}+1)^{2}+\frac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i-1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}^{\prime}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime},\end{split}

(6.66)

from where an application of Grönwall’s inequality yields the desired result.

\square

6.2
Energy estimates for
\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}

In this section we obtain energy estimates for up to
N+4
derivatives of
\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}
and
\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}
. These will be useful in the energy estimates for
\alpha
and
\underline{\alpha}
respectively.

Proposition 6.6

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
3.1
) and (
5.2
), there holds, for all
0\leq i\leq N+4
:

\begin{split}&\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{i}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{i}(-\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\rho^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}\\
&\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{i}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u_{\infty}}^{(0,\underline{u})})}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{i}(-\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\rho^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{0}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{3}}}.\end{split}

(6.67)

Proof.

We begin by noticing the equations

\begin{split}&\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}+\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}-\widehat{\mathcal{D}}(\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\rho^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\sigma^{F})\\
=&\psi_{g}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\alpha^{F}+(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi})(\psi_{g},\underline{\hat{\chi}})(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})+\nabla(\eta,\underline{\eta})\alpha^{F}+\psi_{g}\widehat{\nabla}(\alpha^{F},\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})+(\alpha^{F},\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla\psi_{g}\\
+&(\nabla\psi_{g},\nabla\hat{\chi})(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})+(\hat{\chi},\psi_{g})\widehat{\nabla}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})+\alpha^{F}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})+\alpha^{F}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})+\alpha\hskip 1.42262pt\underline{\alpha}^{F}\\
&+(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}+(\rho,\sigma)\alpha^{F}+\beta^{R}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F}),\end{split}

(6.68)

\begin{split}&\widehat{\nabla}_{4}(\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\rho^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\sigma^{F})-\prescript{*}{}{\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}=\\
&\beta^{R}\alpha^{F}+\alpha^{F}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\alpha^{F}+\alpha^{F}\cdot\alpha^{F}+\psi_{g}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}\\
&+(\hat{\chi},\psi_{g})\widehat{\nabla}\alpha^{F}+(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi})\psi_{g}\alpha^{F}+(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi})(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi})(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\\
&+\psi_{g}\psi_{g}(\alpha^{F},\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})+(\nabla\omega)\alpha^{F}.\end{split}

(6.69)

Commuting with
i
gauge-covariant angular derivatives, we arrive at

\displaystyle\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}+\frac{i+1}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}-\widehat{\mathcal{D}}(\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\rho^{F},\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle=\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}+1}(\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\rho^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\psi_{g}\nabla^{i_{4}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{5}}\alpha^{F}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi})\nabla^{i_{4}}(\psi_{g},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{5}}(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}+1}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\alpha^{F}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\alpha^{F},\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}+1}(\mbox{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}+1}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{5}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\alpha\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\rho,\sigma)\nabla^{i_{4}}\alpha^{F}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\beta^{R}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}+1}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-2}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{5}}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}:=G_{i},

(6.70)

\displaystyle\widehat{\nabla}_{4}(\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\rho^{F},\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\sigma^{F})-\prescript{*}{}{\mathcal{D}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}+1}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\beta^{R}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\alpha^{F}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{5}}\alpha^{F}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\alpha^{F}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\psi_{g}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\psi_{g})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\widehat{\nabla}\alpha^{F}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi})\nabla^{i_{4}}\psi_{g}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{5}}\alpha^{F}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi})\nabla^{i_{4}}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{5}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\psi_{g}\nabla^{i_{4}}\psi_{g}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{5}}(\alpha^{F},\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}+1}\omega\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\alpha^{F}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\rho^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\rho^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\rho^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{5}}(\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\rho^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\rho^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\sigma^{F}):=H_{i}.

(6.71)

We have

\begin{split}&\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{i}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{2}+\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{i}(-\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\rho^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}^{2}\\
&\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{i}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u_{\infty}}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{2}+\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{i}(-\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\rho^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{0}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}^{2}+X+Y,\end{split}

(6.72)

where

X:=\iint_{\mathcal{D}(u,\underline{u})}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{\langle(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{i}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F},a^{\frac{i}{2}}G_{i}\rangle}\rVert_{L^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime},

(6.73)

Y:=\iint_{\mathcal{D}(u,\underline{u})}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{\langle(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{i}(\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\rho^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\sigma^{F}),a^{\frac{i}{2}}H_{i}\rangle}\rVert_{L^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime},

(6.74)

As before, we have the estimate

X\leq\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}G_{i}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot\sup_{u_{\infty}\leq u^{\prime}\leq-\frac{a}{4}}\lVert(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{i}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u^{\prime}}^{(0,\underline{u})})}.

(6.75)

\square

We begin with the first term. There holds

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}+1}(\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\rho^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{\psi_{g}(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{i}(\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\rho^{F},\ \widehat{\nabla}_{4}\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
+&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1\\
i_{3}<i-1\end{subarray}}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{+}1}(\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\rho^{F},\ \widehat{\nabla}_{4}\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{i}(\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\rho^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{3}}}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{3}}},\end{split}

(6.76)

where we have used the bootstrap assumptions (
3.1
). For the second term, we have

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\psi_{g}\nabla^{i_{4}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{5}}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\cdot\left(\frac{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}{a}\cdot a^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot\frac{\Gamma^{3}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\right)^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{3}}}.\end{split}

(6.77)

For the third term, we have

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi})\nabla^{i_{4}}(\psi_{g},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{5}}(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\cdot\left(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot\frac{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\cdot{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\cdot{\Gamma^{3}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}}\right)^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{3}}}.\end{split}

(6.78)

For the fourth term, we have

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}+1}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\frac{a\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\nabla^{i+1}(\eta,\underline{\eta})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
+&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\frac{\Gamma^{4}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{N+4}\nabla^{N+5}(\eta,\underline{\eta})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{2}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{3}}}\\
\lesssim&\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\cdot\Gamma_{\text{top}}^{2}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{3}}}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{3}}}.\end{split}

(6.79)

Most of the other terms are bounded above by
a^{-\frac{1}{3}}
in a similar fashion. We will give the details for the sixth and fifteenth terms, which present the most difficulties. For the sixth term, the most borderline term appears when
(\mbox{tr}\chi,\hat{\chi})=\hat{\chi}
. We then have

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}+1}\hat{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\cdot\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\nabla^{i+1}\hat{\chi}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\frac{\Gamma^{4}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\Gamma_{\text{top}}[\hat{\chi}]^{2}+\frac{a\Gamma^{4}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{3}}}.\end{split}

(6.80)

Finally, the fifteenth term can be estimated as follows:

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\frac{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}{a^{2}}\cdot a\cdot\frac{\Gamma^{6}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{\Gamma^{6}}{\lvert u\rvert}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{3}}}.\end{split}

(6.81)

As a consequence, using (
6.75
), one can conclude that

X\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{3}}}\cdot\sup_{u_{\infty}\leq u^{\prime}\leq-\frac{a}{4}}\lVert(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{i}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u^{\prime}}^{(0,\underline{u})})}\lesssim 1.

(6.82)

For
Y
, we once again have

Y\leq\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}H_{i}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot\sup_{0\leq\underline{u}\leq 1}\lVert(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{i}(\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\rho^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\sigma^{F})\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u^{\prime}}^{(0,\underline{u})})}.

(6.83)

We begin with the first term in
H_{i}
. We have

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}+1}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\cdot\frac{\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{i}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
&+\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\cdot\frac{a\Gamma^{4}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{3}}},\end{split}

(6.84)

where we have used the bootstrap assumptions. Most of the rest of the terms can be bounded above by
a^{-\frac{1}{3}}
similarly. The terms which are most borderline are the fourth, tenth and thirteenth. We treat them one by one.

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\cdot\frac{a^{2}\Gamma_{2}[\alpha^{F}]^{2}\Gamma_{\infty}[\alpha^{F}]^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim(\mathcal{R}+\underline{\mathcal{R}}+1)^{2}\lesssim R^{2},\end{split}

(6.85)

where again we have used the bootstrap assumptions (
3.1
). For the tenth term, we have

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}+1}\omega\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\cdot\frac{a\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\nabla^{i+1}\omega}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
+&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\frac{\Gamma^{4}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{3}}},\end{split}

(6.86)

where we have used the bootstrap assumptions (
5.2
). For the thirteenth term, there holds

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\psi_{g}\nabla^{i_{4}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{5}}(\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\rho^{F},\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{5}}\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\cdot a\cdot\frac{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}{a^{2}}\cdot\frac{\Gamma^{6}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{4}}\lesssim\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert}\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{3}}}.\end{split}

(6.87)

Combining everything, we arrive at

Y\leq(R+1)\cdot\sup_{0\leq\underline{u}\leq 1}\lVert(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{i}(\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\rho^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\sigma^{F})\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}^{\prime}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}.

(6.88)

Taking into account (
6.72
), (
6.82
) and (
6.87
), we

\begin{split}&\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{i}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{2}+\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{i}(-\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\rho^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}^{2}\\
\lesssim&\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{i}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u_{\infty}}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{2}+\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{i}(-\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\rho^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{0}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}^{2}\\
+&\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{3}}}\cdot\sup_{u_{\infty}\leq u\leq-\frac{a}{4}}\lVert(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{i}\nabla_{4}\alpha^{F}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u^{\prime}}^{(0,\underline{u})})}+(R+1)\cdot\sup_{0\leq\underline{u}\leq 1}\lVert(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{i}(\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\rho^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\sigma^{F})\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}^{\prime}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}.\end{split}

(6.89)

Multiplying everything by
\frac{1}{a}
and using the bootstrap assumptions (
3.1
),(
5.2
) we arrive at

\begin{split}&\frac{1}{a}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{i}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{2}+\frac{1}{a}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{i}(-\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\rho^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}^{2}\\
\lesssim&\frac{1}{a}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{i}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u_{\infty}}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{2}+\frac{1}{a}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{i}(-\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\rho^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{0}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}^{2}\\
+&\frac{1}{a^{\frac{5}{6}}}\cdot\left(\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sup_{u_{\infty}\leq u\leq-\frac{a}{4}}\lVert(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{i}\nabla_{4}\alpha^{F}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u^{\prime}}^{(0,\underline{u})})}\right)\\
+&\frac{R+1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\cdot\left(\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sup_{0\leq\underline{u}\leq 1}\lVert(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{i}(\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\rho^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\sigma^{F})\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}^{\prime}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}\right)\\
\lesssim&\frac{1}{a}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{i}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u_{\infty}}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{2}+\frac{1}{a}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla})^{i}(-\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\rho^{F},\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{0}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}^{2}+1,\end{split}

(6.90)

whence we obtain the result by taking the square root.

Now we need to estimate the energy associated with the mixed derivative of
\underline{\alpha}^{F}
i.e.
\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}
. Before proceeding, we need the following integration lemma.

Proposition 6.7

Let us define
\Psi_{1}:=\frac{a}{|u|}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})
and
\Psi_{2}:=\frac{a}{|u|}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}
. The following Hodge system

\displaystyle\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\Psi_{1}+(\frac{I-1}{2}+1)\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\Psi_{1}=\widehat{\mathcal{D}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\Psi_{2}+\frac{a}{|u|}\mathcal{E}^{I}_{1},

\displaystyle\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\Psi_{2}=~^{*}\widehat{\mathcal{D}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\Psi_{1}+\frac{a}{|u|}\mathcal{E}^{I}_{2}

verifies the energy estimate

\displaystyle\int_{H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})}}\lVert{\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}(\frac{a}{|u|}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\rho^{F},\frac{a}{|u|}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}(\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{H_{u_{\infty}}^{(0,\underline{u})}}\lVert{\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}(\frac{a}{|u_{\infty}|}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\rho^{F},\frac{a}{|u_{\infty}|}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}(\frac{a}{|u|}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},0})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\iint_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}(\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\rho^{F},\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\sigma^{F}),\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\mathcal{E}_{1}\rangle}\rVert_{L^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\iint_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}(\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}),\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\mathcal{E}_{2}\rangle}\rVert_{L^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}.

Proof.

First we denote
\frac{a}{|u|}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})
by
\Psi_{1}
while
\frac{a}{|u|}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}
by
\Psi_{2}
.
Now we commute the evolution equations in the proposition with
\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}
to obtain

\displaystyle\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\Psi_{1}+(\frac{I-1}{2}+1)\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\Psi_{1}=\widehat{\mathcal{D}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\Psi_{2}+\frac{a}{|u|}\mathcal{E}^{I}_{1},

(6.91)

\displaystyle\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\Psi_{2}=~^{*}\widehat{\mathcal{D}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\Psi_{1}+\frac{a}{|u|}\mathcal{E}^{I}_{2}

(6.92)

Now apply Proposition
6.1
with
J=I-2

\lambda_{0}=\frac{J}{2}+s_{2}(\Psi_{1})
,
\lambda_{1}=2\lambda_{0}-1
to obtain the following

\displaystyle 2\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2J-1+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{J}\Psi_{1},(\widehat{\nabla}_{3}+(\frac{J}{2}+s_{2}(\Psi_{1}))\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}\Psi_{1}\rangle=\int_{H_{u}(0,\underline{u})}|u|^{2J-1+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|\widehat{\nabla}^{J}\Psi_{1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-\int_{H_{u_{\infty}}(0,\underline{u})}|u_{\infty}|^{2J-1+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|\widehat{\nabla}^{J}\Psi_{1}|^{2}+\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2J-1+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}f|\widehat{\nabla}^{J}\Psi_{1}|^{2}

(6.93)

and for
\Psi_{2}

\displaystyle 2\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2J-1+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{J}\Psi_{2},\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\widehat{\nabla}^{J}\Psi_{2}\rangle=\int_{\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}(u_{\infty},u)}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2J-1+4s_{2}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{J}\Psi_{2}|^{2}

(6.94)

\displaystyle-\int_{\underline{H}_{0}(u_{\infty},u)}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2J-1+4s_{2}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{J}\Psi_{2}|^{2}+\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2J-1+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}(2\omega-\mbox{tr}\chi)|\widehat{\nabla}^{J}\Psi_{2}|^{2}.

Adding the previous two expressions we obtain

\displaystyle 2\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2J-1+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{J}\Psi_{1},(\widehat{\nabla}_{3}+(\frac{J}{2}+s_{2}(\Psi_{1}))\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}\Psi_{1}\rangle

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 2\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2J-1+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{J}\Psi_{2},\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\widehat{\nabla}^{J}\Psi_{2}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{H_{u}(0,\underline{u})}|u|^{2J-1+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|\widehat{\nabla}^{J}\Psi_{1}|^{2}+\int_{\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}(u_{\infty},u)}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2J-1+4s_{2}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{J}\Psi_{2}|^{2}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\int_{H_{u_{\infty}}(0,\underline{u})}|u_{\infty}|^{2J-1+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|\widehat{\nabla}^{J}\Psi_{1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\int_{\underline{H}_{0}(u_{\infty},u)}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2J-1+4s_{2}}|\widehat{\nabla}^{J}\Psi_{2}|^{2}+\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2J-1+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}f|\widehat{\nabla}^{J}\Psi_{1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2J-1+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}(2\omega-\mbox{tr}\chi)|\widehat{\nabla}^{J}\Psi_{2}|^{2}.

Now use the equations of motion to yield

\displaystyle\int_{H_{u}(0,\underline{u})}|u|^{2J-1+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|\widehat{\nabla}^{J}\Psi_{1}|^{2}+\int_{\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}(u_{\infty},u)}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2J-1+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|\widehat{\nabla}^{J}\Psi_{2}|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{H_{u_{\infty}}(0,\underline{u})}|u_{\infty}|^{2J-1+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|\widehat{\nabla}^{J}\Psi_{1}|^{2}+\int_{\underline{H}_{0}(u_{\infty},u)}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2J-1+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|\widehat{\nabla}^{J}\Psi_{2}|^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 2\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2J-1+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{J}\Psi_{1},\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}\mathcal{E}^{I}_{1}\rangle+2\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2J-1+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{J}\Psi_{2},\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}\mathcal{E}^{I}_{2}\rangle

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2J-1+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}(\eta+\underline{\eta})\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{J}\Psi_{1},\widehat{\nabla}^{J}\Psi_{2}\rangle-\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2J-1+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}f|\widehat{\nabla}^{J}\Psi_{1}|^{2}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2J-1+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}(2\omega-\mbox{tr}\chi)|\widehat{\nabla}^{J}\Psi_{2}|^{2}.

(6.95)

After applying Grönwall’s inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{H_{u}(0,\underline{u})}|u|^{2J-1+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|\widehat{\nabla}^{J}\Psi_{1}|^{2}+\int_{\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}(u_{\infty},u)}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2J-1+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|\widehat{\nabla}^{J}\Psi_{2}|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\bigg(\int_{H_{u_{\infty}}(0,\underline{u})}|u_{\infty}|^{2J-1+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|\nabla^{J}\Psi_{1}|^{2}+\int_{\underline{H}_{0}(u_{\infty},u)}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2J-1+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\Psi_{2}|^{2}.

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle|\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2J-1+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{J}\Psi_{1},\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}\mathcal{E}^{I}_{1}\rangle|+|\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2J-1+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{J}\Psi_{2},\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}\mathcal{E}^{I}_{2}\rangle|\bigg).

(6.96)

Similarly,

\displaystyle\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2J-1+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{J}\Psi_{1},\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}\mathcal{E}^{I}_{1}\rangle|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2J-1+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}a^{J+1+2s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{-2J-1-4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{J}\Psi_{1},\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}\mathcal{E}^{I}_{1}\rangle|_{L^{1}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}a^{J+1+2s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{-2}|\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{J}\Psi_{1},\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}\mathcal{E}^{I}_{1}\rangle|_{L^{1}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

(6.97)

and

\displaystyle\int_{D_{u,\underline{u}}}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2J-1+4s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{J}\Psi_{2},\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}\widehat{\nabla}^{J}\mathcal{E}_{2}\rangle|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{u,\underline{u}}a^{J+1+2s_{2}(\Psi_{1})}|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{-2}|\langle\widehat{\nabla}^{J}\Psi_{2},\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}\widehat{\nabla}^{J}\mathcal{E}_{2}\rangle|_{L^{1}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}.

Collecting all the terms together and noting that
|u|\leq|u^{{}^{\prime}}|
yields the result.

\square

The following proposition allows us to obtain the necessary estimates.

Proposition 6.8

Under the assumption of the main theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
3.1
), the following energy estimate is satisfied for all
0\leq I\leq N+5
:

\begin{split}&\int_{H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})}}\lVert{\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}(\frac{a}{|u|}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\rho^{F},\frac{a}{|u|}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}(\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{H_{u_{\infty}}^{(0,\underline{u})}}\lVert{\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}(\frac{a}{|u_{\infty}|}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\rho^{F},\frac{a}{|u_{\infty}|}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
+&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}(\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},0})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}+1.\end{split}

Proof.

First we compute the error terms
\mathcal{E}_{1}
and
\mathcal{E}_{2}
. An explicit calculation using the null Yang-Mills equations yields

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{1}\sim

\displaystyle\left(\nabla\omega-\underline{\chi}\cdot(\eta+\underline{\eta})+\underline{\omega}(\eta+\underline{\eta})-\underline{\beta}+\epsilon\sigma^{F}\cdot\underline{\alpha}^{F}-\rho^{F}\cdot\underline{\alpha}^{F}\right)\underline{\alpha}^{F}+(\eta-\underline{\eta})\cdot\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla_{3}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}+(\widetilde{\underline{\beta}}+\underline{\alpha}^{F}\cdot(\rho^{F}+\sigma^{F}))\underline{\alpha}^{F}+\underline{\alpha}^{F}\underline{\alpha}^{F}+(\eta+\underline{\eta})\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}-\underline{\chi}\cdot\widehat{\nabla}\underline{\alpha}^{F}+\underline{\chi}\eta\underline{\alpha}^{F},

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{2}\sim

\displaystyle\underline{\alpha}^{F}\rho^{F}+(\eta,\underline{\eta})(-\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})-\widehat{\text{div}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}+(\eta,\underline{\eta})\cdot\underline{\alpha}^{F})-\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}\rho^{F}+\underline{\chi}\eta\rho^{F}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\underline{\alpha}^{F}\sigma^{F}-\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}\sigma^{F}+\underline{\chi}\eta\sigma^{F}+\omega\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}-\underline{\omega}\widehat{\nabla}\rho^{F}+\underline{\omega}~^{*}\widehat{\nabla}\sigma^{F}+(\eta-\underline{\eta})\widehat{\nabla}\underline{\alpha}^{F}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sigma\underline{\alpha}^{F}+\rho^{F}\underline{\alpha}^{F}+(|\rho^{F}|^{2}+|\sigma^{F}|^{2})\underline{\alpha}^{F}+\underline{\omega}(\mbox{tr}\chi\underline{\alpha}^{F}-2\prescript{*}{}{\underline{\eta}}\sigma^{F}-2\underline{\eta}\rho^{F}+2\omega\underline{\alpha}^{F}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\underline{\hat{\chi}}\cdot\alpha^{F})-\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\chi\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}.

Note that the expression of
\mathcal{E}_{1}
contains
\nabla_{3}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}
, which contains
(\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})^{2}
, a double anomaly that might cause problems in closing the estimates later. Therefore, we work with the re-normalized entity
\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}:=\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}+\frac{2}{|u|}
. We write
\nabla_{3}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}
as follows:

\displaystyle\nabla_{3}(\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})=

\displaystyle\nabla_{3}(\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}+\frac{2}{|u|}-\frac{2}{|u|})=\nabla_{3}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}+\frac{2\Omega^{-1}}{|u|^{2}}

\displaystyle\sim

\displaystyle\frac{2}{|u|^{2}}(\Omega^{-1}-1)+|\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}|^{2}+2\underline{\omega}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}-|\underline{\hat{\chi}}|^{2}-|\underline{\alpha}^{F}|^{2}+\frac{2\Omega^{-1}}{|u|^{2}}.

Therefore
\mathcal{E}_{1}
reads

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{1}\sim

\displaystyle\left(\nabla\omega-\underline{\chi}\cdot(\eta+\underline{\eta})+\underline{\omega}(\eta+\underline{\eta})-\underline{\beta}+\epsilon\sigma^{F}\cdot\underline{\alpha}^{F}-\rho^{F}\cdot\underline{\alpha}^{F}\right)\underline{\alpha}^{F}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle(\eta-\underline{\eta})\cdot\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}+(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})(\frac{2}{|u|^{2}}(\Omega^{-1}-1)+|\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}|^{2}+2\underline{\omega}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}-|\underline{\hat{\chi}}|^{2}-|\underline{\alpha}^{F}|^{2}+\frac{2\Omega^{-1}}{|u|^{2}})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle(\widetilde{\underline{\beta}}+\underline{\alpha}^{F}\cdot(\rho^{F}+\sigma^{F}))\underline{\alpha}^{F}+\underline{\alpha}^{F}\underline{\alpha}^{F}+(\eta+\underline{\eta})\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}-\underline{\chi}\cdot\widehat{\nabla}\underline{\alpha}^{F}+\underline{\chi}\eta\underline{\alpha}^{F},

Now using the commutation formulae (
2.86
)-(
2.87
) as appropriate, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{I}_{1}\sim

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}+1}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{2}}\Psi_{1}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}+1}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\widehat{\underline{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\Psi_{1}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\hskip 1.42262pt\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\Psi_{1}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=J-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}+1}\hat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\Psi_{1}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=J-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}+1}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\Psi_{1}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=J-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\Psi_{1}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=J-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}\Psi_{1}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=J}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}+1}\omega\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=J}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{J_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=J}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\underline{\omega}\nabla^{J_{4}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=J}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\widetilde{\underline{\beta}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=J}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\underbrace{\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=J}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}\nabla^{J_{4}}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}\rho^{F}}_{I}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\underbrace{\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\rho^{F}}_{II}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\Omega^{-1}-1)\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\rho^{F}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=J}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\nabla^{J_{4}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}\rho^{F}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=J}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\underline{\omega}\nabla^{J_{4}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}\rho^{F}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=J}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}\rho^{F}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=J}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}\rho^{F}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=J}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}

\displaystyle+
