\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=J}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=J}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=J}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{J_{4}}\eta\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\underbrace{\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=J}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}+1}\underline{\alpha}^{F}}_{III}

(6.98)

and similarly

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{I}_{2}\sim

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=J-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\beta\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\Psi_{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=J-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}\Psi_{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\Psi_{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\chi\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\Psi_{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\rho^{F}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\rho^{F}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})\nabla^{J_{4}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}+1}\underline{\alpha}^{F}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})\nabla^{J_{4}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\underbrace{\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}+1}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})}_{potentially~dangerous~term}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})\nabla^{J_{4}}\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}\sigma^{F}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\omega\nabla^{J_{4}}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\underline{\omega}\nabla^{J_{4}+1}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})\nabla^{J_{4}+1}\underline{\alpha}^{F}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\nabla^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{J_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\underline{\omega}\nabla^{J_{4}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\underline{\omega}\nabla^{J_{4}}\underline{\eta}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\underline{\omega}\nabla^{J_{4}}\omega\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\underline{\omega}\nabla^{J_{4}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}\alpha^{F}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\mbox{tr}\chi\nabla^{J_{4}}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}.

(6.99)

In the error term, we will only estimate the most dangerous and borderline terms. The most dangerous error term is estimated as follows:

\displaystyle\iint_{u,\underline{u}}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{\langle a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F},\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\cdot\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\rho^{F}\rangle}\rVert_{L^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\iint_{u,\underline{u}}\frac{a^{3}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{5}}\frac{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}{a}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{2}_{sc}}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\rho^{F}||_{L^{2}_{sc}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\iint_{u,\underline{u}}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{2}_{sc}}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\rho^{F}||_{L^{2}_{sc}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\iint_{u,\underline{u}}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}(\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{2}_{sc}})||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\rho^{F}||_{L^{2}_{sc}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\iint_{u,\underline{u}}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}\left(\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}}+||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\rho^{F}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}}\right)\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\underline{u}}\int_{u}\frac{a^{3}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{4}}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\int_{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}(\int_{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\rho^{F}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}})\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\int_{\underline{u}}\int_{u}\frac{a^{3}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{4}}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}.

(6.100)

Other ill-behaved (but not necessarily borderline) terms include
\mathcal{E}^{I}_{2}
, for example,
\frac{a}{|u|}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\cdot\widehat{\nabla}^{I+1}\rho^{F}\subset\mathcal{E}^{I}_{2}
and
\frac{1}{|u|}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\Psi_{1}\subset\mathcal{E}^{I}_{1}
among others. These are estimated as follows:

\displaystyle\iint_{u,\underline{u}}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{\langle a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F},\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\underline{\hat{\chi}}\cdot\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\rho^{F}\rangle}\rVert_{L^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\iint_{u,\underline{u}}\frac{a^{\frac{5}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{4}}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}})}}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}||\underline{\hat{\chi}}||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\rho^{F}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}})}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\iint_{u,\underline{u}}\frac{a^{3}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{4}}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}})}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\iint_{u,\underline{u}}\frac{a^{\frac{5}{2}}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{4}}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\rho^{F}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}})}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathbb{YM}^{2}+\frac{a^{\frac{5}{2}}}{|u|^{3}}\mathbb{YM}^{2}\right)\lesssim 1,

(6.101)

\displaystyle\iint_{u,\underline{u}}\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{\langle a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F}),\frac{1}{|u|}a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\rangle}\rVert_{L^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\iint_{u,\underline{u}}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{4}}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|u|}\sup_{u^{{}^{\prime}}\in[u,u_{\infty}]}\int_{H^{(0,\underline{u})}_{u^{{}^{\prime}}}}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}(\widehat{\nabla}_{3}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}\lesssim\frac{1}{|u|}\mathbb{YM}\lesssim 1,

(6.102)

\displaystyle\int_{u,\underline{u}}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}

\displaystyle\lVert{\langle a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\Psi_{1},\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}(\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}+1}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\rangle}\rVert_{L^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\int_{u,\underline{u}}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\Psi_{1}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\mathbb{YM}^{2}[\underline{\alpha}^{F}]+\int_{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\Psi_{1}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}},

(6.103)

\displaystyle\int_{u,\underline{u}}

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}||\langle a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\Psi_{2},\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=J}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\mbox{tr}\chi\nabla^{J_{4}}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\rangle||_{L^{1}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\int_{u,\underline{u}}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{4}}\left(||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\Psi_{2}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}+||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\int_{\underline{u}}\int_{u}\frac{a^{3}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{5}}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}+\frac{1}{a}\int_{\underline{u}}\int_{u}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

(6.104)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1,

\displaystyle\int_{u,\underline{u}}

\displaystyle\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}

(6.105)

\displaystyle||\langle a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\Psi_{1},\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}+J_{5}=J}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}\nabla^{J_{4}}\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{5}}\rho^{F}\rangle||_{L^{1}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\int_{u,\underline{u}}\frac{1}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}\left(||\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\rho^{F}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}+||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\rho^{F}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}\right)du^{{}^{\prime}}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{1}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{3}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\rho^{F}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}+\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}\mathbb{YM}[\underline{\alpha}^{F}]^{2},

(6.106)

where we have used the
L^{2}
estimate for the Yang-Mills curvature
\rho^{F}
. We estimate some of the remaining terms as follows:

\displaystyle\int_{u,\underline{u}}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}||\langle a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\Psi_{1},a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}\frac{1}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{2}}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\rho^{F}\rangle||_{L^{1}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\int_{u,\underline{u}}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{5}}\left(||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\Psi||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}+||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\rho^{F}||_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}\right)du^{{}^{\prime}}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{5}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\Psi||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}+\frac{a^{2}}{|u|^{4}}\mathbb{YM}[\underline{\alpha}^{F}]^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{2}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{5}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}||a^{\frac{I-1}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\Psi||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime},

(6.107)

\displaystyle\int_{u,\underline{u}}\frac{a^{I}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}||\langle\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F},\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\nabla^{J_{3}}\chi\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\rangle||_{L^{1}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\int_{u,\underline{u}}\frac{a^{3}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{5}}||\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}||\hat{\chi}||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\sup_{\underline{u}}\int_{u}\frac{a^{3}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{4}}||\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}||\hat{\chi}||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}du^{{}^{\prime}},

(6.108)

\displaystyle\int_{u,\underline{u}}

\displaystyle\frac{a^{I}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}||\langle\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F},\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}\sum_{J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}=I-1}\nabla^{J_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{J_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{J_{4}}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\rangle||_{L^{1}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\int_{u,\underline{u}}\frac{a^{3}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{5}}||\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}||\alpha^{F}||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}d\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\sup_{\underline{u}}\int_{u}\frac{a^{3}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{4}}||\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}||\hat{\chi}||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 1+\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\sup_{\underline{u}}\int_{u}\frac{a^{3}}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|^{4}}||\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}||^{2}_{L^{2}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}||\hat{\chi}||_{L^{\infty}_{sc}(S_{u^{{}^{\prime}},\underline{u}^{{}^{\prime}}})}du^{{}^{\prime}}.

(6.109)

Collecting all the terms and using Grönwall’s inequality, we obtain

\begin{split}&\int_{H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})}}\lVert{\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}(\frac{a}{|u|}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\rho^{F},\frac{a}{|u|}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}(\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{H_{u_{\infty}}^{(0,\underline{u})}}\lVert{\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}(\frac{a}{|u|_{\infty}}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\rho^{F},\frac{a}{|u|_{\infty}}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\sigma^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
+&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{\widehat{\nabla}^{I-1}(\frac{a}{|u^{{}^{\prime}}|}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},0})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}+1.\end{split}

This completes the energy estimates for
\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\underline{\alpha}^{F}
.

\square

6.3
Energy Estimates for the Weyl/Riemann Curvature components

For
(\Psi_{1},\Psi_{2})\in\begin{Bmatrix}(\alpha,\beta^{R}),(\beta^{R},(\rho,\sigma)),((\rho,\sigma),\underline{\beta}^{R}),(\underline{\beta}^{R},\underline{\alpha})\end{Bmatrix}
the energy estimates are carried out in Bianchi pairs, via the aid of the following proposition:

Proposition 6.9

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
3.1
), for a Bianchi pair
(\Psi_{1},\Psi_{2})
satisfying

\nabla_{3}\nabla^{i}\Psi_{1}+\left(\frac{i+1}{2}+s_{2}(\Psi_{1})\right)\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{i}\Psi_{1}-\mathcal{D}\nabla^{i}\Psi_{2}=P_{i},

\nabla_{4}\nabla^{i}\Psi_{2}-\prescript{*}{}{\mathcal{D}}\nabla^{i}\Psi_{1}=Q_{i},

the following holds true:

\begin{split}&\int_{H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})}}\lVert{\nabla^{i}\Psi_{1}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\int_{\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)}}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{\nabla^{i}\Psi_{2}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{H_{u_{\infty}}^{(0,\underline{u})}}\lVert{\nabla^{i}\Psi_{1}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u_{\infty},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\int_{\underline{H}_{0}^{(u_{\infty},u)}}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{\nabla^{i}\Psi_{2}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},0})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\\
&+\iint_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{\nabla^{i}\Psi_{1}\cdot P_{i}}\rVert_{L^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\iint_{\mathcal{D}_{u,\underline{u}}}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{\nabla^{i}\Psi_{2}\cdot Q_{i}}\rVert_{L^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}.\end{split}

(6.110)

With this Proposition as the main tool, we begin with
(\alpha,\beta^{R})
.

Proposition 6.10

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.2
and the bootstrap assumptions (
3.1
), there holds, for all
0\leq i\leq N+4
:

\begin{split}&\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\alpha}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\beta^{R}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}\\
&\lesssim\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\alpha}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u_{\infty}}^{(0,\underline{u})})}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\beta^{R}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{0}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{3}}}.\end{split}

(6.111)

Proof.

We recall the (schematic) Bianchi equations for
\nabla^{i}\alpha,\nabla^{i}\beta^{R}
:

\displaystyle\nabla_{3}\nabla^{i}\alpha+\frac{i+1}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{i}\alpha-\mathcal{D}\nabla^{i}\beta^{R}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}+1}\beta^{R}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\alpha

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi})\nabla^{i_{4}}(\rho,\sigma,\beta^{R})

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}+1}\alpha^{F}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}+1}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\alpha^{F},\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{5}}(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\widehat{\nabla}_{4}\alpha^{F}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}})\nabla^{i_{4}}\alpha

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{i_{4}}\alpha

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-2}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\nabla^{i_{4}}\alpha:=P_{i}^{1}+\dots+P_{i}^{10}.

(6.112)

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\nabla_{4}\nabla^{i}\beta^{R}-\prescript{*}{}{\mathcal{D}}\nabla^{i}\alpha=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi})\nabla^{i_{4}}(\beta^{R},\alpha)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}+1}\alpha^{F}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{5}}\alpha^{F}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\psi_{g}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{5}}\alpha^{F}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{i_{4}}\beta^{R}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-2}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{i_{4}}\beta^{R}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{i_{5}}\beta^{R}:=Q_{i}^{1}+\dots+Q_{i}^{7}.

(6.113)

Applying Proposition
6.9
, we have

\begin{split}&\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\alpha}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}+\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\beta^{R}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}\\
\lesssim&\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\alpha}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u_{\infty}}^{(0,\underline{u})})}+\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\beta^{R}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{0}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}\\
&+\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}P_{i}\cdot(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\alpha}\rVert_{L^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
&+\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}Q_{i}\cdot(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\beta^{R}}\rVert_{L^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}.\end{split}

(6.114)

By Hölder’s inequality, one has

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}P_{i}\cdot(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\alpha}\rVert_{L^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\leq&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\sum_{j=1}^{10}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}P_{i}^{j}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\cdot\sup_{u^{\prime}}\lVert(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\alpha\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u^{\prime}}^{(0,\underline{u})})},\end{split}

(6.115)

Let us focus on the sum in the above line. For the first three terms, there holds

\sum_{j=1}^{3}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}P_{i}^{j}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma\cdot R}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}.

For the fourth and fifth terms, there holds

\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}P_{i}^{4}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}P_{i}^{5}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma\cdot M}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}.

For the sixth term, there holds

\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}P_{i}^{6}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\Gamma^{3}.

For the seventh term, there holds

\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}P_{i}^{7}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma\cdot M}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}.

For the eighth term, there holds

\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}P_{i}^{8}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\Gamma^{2}+\Gamma R.

For the ninth and tenth terms, there holds

\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}P_{i}^{9}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}P_{i}^{10}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\Gamma^{3}+\Gamma^{2}R.

Putting everything together, there holds

\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}P_{i}(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\alpha}\rVert_{L^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim\Gamma^{3}+\Gamma^{2}R+\Gamma R+1.

(6.116)

Similarly, for the analogous term involving
\beta^{R}
, there holds

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}Q_{i}\cdot(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\beta^{R}}\rVert_{L^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\leq&\sum_{j=1}^{7}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}Q_{i}^{j}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\cdot\sup_{u^{\prime}}\lVert(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\beta^{R}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}^{\prime}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}.\end{split}

(6.117)

We estimate term by term. For the first term, there holds

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}Q_{i}^{1}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a\Gamma(R+\Gamma)}{\lvert u\rvert}

(6.118)

For the second term, there holds

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}Q_{i}^{2}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a\Gamma(M+\Gamma)}{\lvert u\rvert}

(6.119)

For the third and fourth terms, there holds

\begin{split}&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}Q_{i}^{3}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}+\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}Q_{i}^{4}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\frac{a\Gamma(M+\Gamma)}{\lvert u\rvert}\end{split}

(6.120)

For the fifth term there holds

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}Q_{i}^{5}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma(R+\Gamma)}{\lvert u\rvert}

(6.121)

For the sixth term, we have

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}Q_{i}^{6}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a\Gamma^{2}(R+\Gamma)}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}

(6.122)

For the seventh term, we have

\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}Q_{i}^{7}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{\Gamma^{2}(R+\Gamma)}{\lvert u\rvert^{3}}

(6.123)

Putting everything together, we have

\sum_{j=1}^{7}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}Q_{i}^{j}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a\Gamma(R+M+\Gamma)}{\lvert u\rvert}+1.

(6.124)

Combining (
6.116
) and (
6.124
), we have

\begin{split}&\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\alpha}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\beta^{R}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}\\
\lesssim&\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\alpha}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u_{\infty}}^{(0,\underline{u})})}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\beta^{R}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{0}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}\\
&+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}P_{i}\cdot(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\alpha}\rVert_{L^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
&+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}Q_{i}\cdot(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\beta^{R}}\rVert_{L^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\alpha}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u_{\infty}}^{(0,\underline{u})})}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\beta^{R}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{0}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{3}}}.\end{split}

(6.125)

The claim follows.

\square

We now move on to energy estimates for the remaining pairs
(\beta^{R},(\rho,\sigma)),((\rho,\sigma),\underline{\beta}^{R})
and
(\underline{\beta}^{R},\underline{\alpha})
.

Proposition 6.11

Let
(\Psi_{1},\Psi_{2})\in\begin{Bmatrix}(\beta^{R},(\rho,\sigma)),((\rho,\sigma),\underline{\beta}^{R}),(\underline{\beta}^{R},\underline{\alpha})\end{Bmatrix}
. For
0\leq i\leq N+4
, there holds:

\displaystyle\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\Psi_{1}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}+\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\Psi_{2}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}

(6.126)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\Psi_{1}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u_{\infty}}^{(0,\underline{u})})}+\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\Psi_{2}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{0}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}+\frac{1}{a^{\frac{1}{3}}}.

(6.127)

Proof.

The schematic equations for
\Psi_{1},\Psi_{2}
are:

\displaystyle\nabla_{3}\Psi_{1}+\left(\frac{1}{2}+s_{2}(\Psi_{1})\right)\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\Psi_{1}-\mathcal{D}\Psi_{2}=

\displaystyle(\psi,\hat{\chi})\Psi+\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}+\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}\widehat{\nabla}(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle(\psi,\hat{\chi},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}),

(6.128)

\begin{split}\nabla_{4}\Psi_{2}-\prescript{*}{}{\mathcal{D}}\Psi_{1}=(\psi,\underline{\hat{\chi}})(\Psi_{u},\alpha)+(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})\widehat{\nabla}(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})+(\psi,\hat{\chi},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}).\end{split}

(6.129)

Commuting with
i
angular derivatives, for
\Psi_{1}
, we have:

\begin{split}&\nabla_{3}\nabla^{i}\Psi_{1}+\left(\frac{i+1}{2}+s_{2}(\Psi_{1})\right)\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{i}\Psi_{1}-\mathcal{D}\nabla^{i}\Psi_{2}\\
=&\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}+1}\Psi_{2}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi})\nabla^{i_{4}}\Psi\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}+1}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}+1}(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{5}}(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\widetilde{\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}})\nabla^{i_{4}}\Psi_{1}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{i_{4}}\Psi_{1}\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-2}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\nabla^{i_{4}}\Psi_{1}\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{i_{5}}\Psi_{1}:=P_{i}.\end{split}

(6.130)

Analogously, for
\Psi_{2}
, we have

\begin{split}&\nabla_{4}\nabla^{i}\Psi_{2}-\prescript{*}{}{\mathcal{D}}\nabla^{i}\Psi_{1}\\
=&\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}+1}\Psi_{1}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\underline{\hat{\chi}})\nabla^{i_{4}}(\Psi,\alpha)\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}+1}(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{5}}(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{i_{4}}\Psi_{2}\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-2}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\hat{\chi},\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{i_{4}}\Psi_{2}\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{i_{5}}\Psi_{2}:=Q_{i}.\end{split}

(6.131)

Making use of Proposition
6.9
once again, we arrive at

\begin{split}&\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\Psi_{1}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{2}+\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\Psi_{2}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}^{2}\\
\lesssim&\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\Psi_{1}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u_{\infty}}^{(0,\underline{u})})}^{2}+\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\Psi_{2}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{0}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}^{2}\\
&+\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}P_{i}\cdot(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\Psi_{1}}\rVert_{L^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
&+\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}Q_{i}\cdot(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\Psi_{2}}\rVert_{L^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}.\end{split}

(6.132)

For the first spacetime integral in the above, we estimate

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}P_{i}\cdot(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\Psi_{1}}\rVert_{L^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}P_{i}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\cdot\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\Psi_{1}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}.\end{split}

(6.133)

For the first term:

\begin{split}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}+1}\Psi_{2}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime},\end{split}

(6.134)

if
i_{3}+1\geq N+3
, we estimate

\begin{split}&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}+1}\Psi_{2}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\sup_{0\leq\underline{u}^{\prime}\leq\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{3}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i_{3}+1}\Psi_{2}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{1}+i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}}\rVert_{L^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\end{split}

(6.135)

and we can estimate
\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}})^{i_{1}+i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}}\rVert_{L^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}
by
\frac{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}
using the bootstrap assumption (
3.1
), to obtain

\begin{split}&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}+1}\Psi_{2}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\sup_{0\leq\underline{u}^{\prime}\leq\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i_{3}+1}\Psi_{2}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{R}{\lvert u\rvert^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lesssim 1.\end{split}

(6.136)

If, however,
i_{3}+1\leq N+2
, we can control the corresponding
L^{2}_{(sc)}(S)
norm just by the bootstrap assumption (
3.1
) to get the bound

\begin{split}&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}+1}\Psi_{2}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{a\Gamma^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\lesssim 1.\end{split}

(6.137)

For the rest of the terms, we estimate using the same philosophy as appropriate. There holds

\begin{split}&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi})\nabla^{i_{4}}\Psi}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma(R+\Gamma)}{\lvert u\rvert}\lesssim 1.\end{split}

(6.138)

Here in particular, when
i_{4}\geq N+3
, we treat the cases
\Psi=\Psi_{u}
and
\Psi=\Psi_{\underline{u}}
separately. For the third and fourth terms, there holds

\begin{split}&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}+1}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\\
+&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}+1}(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\frac{\Gamma(M+\Gamma)}{\lvert u\rvert}\lesssim 1.\end{split}

(6.139)

For the fifth term, we have

\begin{split}&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\psi,\hat{\chi},\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{5}}(\alpha^{F},\mathcal{Y}_{\underline{u}})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\Gamma[\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}]\Gamma[\alpha^{F}]^{2}\lesssim\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\lesssim 1,\end{split}

(6.140)

where we have used the improvements
\Gamma[\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}]+\Gamma[\alpha^{F}]\lesssim 1
from Propositions
4.7
and
4.9
. For the sixth and seventh terms, we can bound them by one as in previous calculations. For the eighth term, using the fact that
i-2\leq N+2
and the improvements from Proposition
4.15
and Proposition
6.10
, we arrive at

\begin{split}&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-2}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\underline{\hat{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\nabla^{i_{4}}\Psi_{1}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\Gamma[\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}]\Gamma[\alpha^{F}]^{2}\lesssim\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\Gamma[\eta,\underline{\eta}]\Gamma[\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}]\Gamma[\Psi_{1}]\lesssim\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\lVert{(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}(\beta^{R},\underline{\beta}^{R})}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\cdot 1\cdot(\mathcal{R}[\alpha]+1)\\
\lesssim&(\mathcal{R}[\alpha]+1)^{2}\lesssim 1,\end{split}

(6.141)

where in the last line we made use of Proposition
6.10
. For the final term in
P_{i}
, we have

\begin{split}&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\underline{\alpha}^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}}(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F})\nabla^{i_{5}}\Psi_{1}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert}\cdot\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma^{2}(R+\Gamma)}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\lesssim 1.\end{split}

(6.142)

This completes the estimates for the first spacetime integral in (
6.132
). For the second and last one, a double application of Hölder’s inequality yields

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}Q_{i}\cdot(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\Psi_{2}}\rVert_{L^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\left(\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}Q_{i}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\Psi_{2}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}^{\prime}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}\\
\lesssim&\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}Q_{i}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\Psi_{2}\rVert^{2}_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}^{\prime}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\lesssim&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}Q_{i}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\Psi_{2}\rVert^{2}_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}^{\prime}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\end{split}

(6.143)

Define
B:=\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}Q_{i}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}
. We can then estimate
B
as follows:

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}+1}\Psi_{1}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\psi_{g}\nabla^{i}\Psi_{1}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
+&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\nabla\psi_{g}\nabla^{i-1}\Psi_{1}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
+&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\psi_{g}\hskip 1.42262pt\psi_{g}\nabla^{i-1}\Psi_{1}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
+&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1\\
i_{3}<i-2\end{subarray}}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}+1}\Psi_{1}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&1,\end{split}

(6.144)

where in the first three integrals we estimate
\psi_{g},\nabla\psi_{g}
in
L^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})
and
\nabla^{i}\Psi_{1}
in the hypersurface norm
L^{2}_{(sc)}(H_{u}^{(0,\underline{u})})
and in the last integral, since
i-2\leq N+2
, we can estimate
\nabla^{i}\Psi_{1}
in
L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})
using the bootstrap assumption on the norm

\textstyle\mathbb{L}

. For the second term, we similarly have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\psi_{g},\hat{\chi})\nabla^{i_{4}}(\Psi,\alpha)}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{R}[\alpha]+1\right)^{2}\lesssim 1,

(6.145)

where we have used the improvements on
\hat{\chi}
from Proposition
4.3
and the energy estimate from Proposition
6.10
. For the third term, we notice that the worst case is when the term is
a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}+1}\alpha^{F}
. We therefore estimate

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}+1}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i+1}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\\
+&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i\\
i_{4}<i\end{subarray}}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}+1}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}.\end{split}

(6.146)

When
i_{4}<i
, we can control
\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}+1}\alpha^{F}
using the bootstrap assumption on the

\textstyle\mathbb{L}

norm, hence the second integral of (
6.146
) is bounded above by
1
. For the first, we have

\begin{split}&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i+1}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\frac{a^{2}\Gamma[\alpha^{F}]^{2}}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\frac{1}{a}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i+1}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\frac{a^{3}\Gamma[\alpha^{F}]^{2}M[\alpha^{F}]^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert^{3}}.\end{split}

(6.147)

We now make use of the improvements given by Proposition
4.9
and the Yang-Mills energy estimate on
(\alpha^{F},(\rho^{F},\sigma^{F}))
to deduce that

\Gamma[\alpha^{F}]^{2}\lesssim(\underline{\mathbb{Y}\mathbb{M}}[\rho^{F},\sigma^{F}]+1)^{2}\lesssim 1,

(6.148)

M[\alpha^{F}]^{2}\lesssim 1,

(6.149)

so that, finally,

\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\psi_{g}^{i_{2}}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{3}}\alpha^{F}\widehat{\nabla}^{i_{4}+1}\alpha^{F}}\rVert_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}^{2}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\lesssim 1.

(6.150)

The rest of the terms can also be bounded above by
1
, using the same approach. We finally arrive at an estimate of the form

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{\infty}}^{u}\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert}\lVert{a^{\frac{i}{2}}Q_{i}\cdot(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\Psi_{2}}\rVert_{L^{1}_{(sc)}(S_{u^{\prime},\underline{u}^{\prime}})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}u^{\prime}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&1+\frac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\lVert(a^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla)^{i}\Psi_{2}\rVert^{2}_{L^{2}_{(sc)}(\underline{H}_{\underline{u}^{\prime}}^{(u_{\infty},u)})}\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}.\end{split}

(6.151)

From here, collecting all the terms together and using Proposition
6.3
, we arrive at the desired result.

\square

7
The formation of a trapped surface

As in all trapped surface formation results in the absence of symmetry, the formation argument is an ODE argument. It relies heavily, as we shall see, on the estimates obtained during the proof of semi-global existence. In this section we prove Theorem
1.3
.

Proof.

The idea is to obtain estimates for
\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\gamma}^{2}
and
\lvert\alpha^{F}\rvert_{\gamma,\delta}^{2}
. We begin with
\hat{\chi}
. Recall the null structure equation

\nabla_{3}\hat{\chi}+\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\hat{\chi}-2\underline{\omega}\hat{\chi}=\nabla\hat{\otimes}\eta-\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\chi\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\eta\hat{\otimes}\eta.

(7.1)

Contracting once with another
\hat{\chi}
tensor, we obtain

\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{3}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\gamma}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\gamma}^{2}-2\omega\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\gamma}^{2}=\hat{\chi}(\nabla\hat{\otimes}\eta-\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\chi\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\eta\hat{\otimes}\eta).

(7.2)

Using the fact that
\underline{\omega}=-\frac{1}{2}\Omega^{-1}\nabla_{3}\Omega
, we can rewrite the above as

\nabla_{3}(\Omega^{2}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\gamma}^{2})+\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\Omega^{2}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\gamma}^{2}=2\Omega^{2}\hat{\chi}(\nabla\hat{\otimes}\eta-\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\chi\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\eta\hat{\otimes}\eta).

(7.3)

Using
\nabla_{3}=\frac{1}{\Omega}\left(\frac{\partial}{\partial u}+b^{A}\frac{\partial}{\partial\theta^{A}}\right)
, we have

\frac{\partial}{\partial u}(\Omega^{2}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\gamma}^{2})+\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\cdot\Omega^{2}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\gamma}^{2}=2\Omega^{3}\hat{\chi}(\nabla\hat{\otimes}\eta-\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\chi\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\eta\hat{\otimes}\eta)-b^{A}\frac{\partial}{\partial\theta^{A}}(\Omega^{2}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\gamma}^{2}).

(7.4)

Substituting

\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}=\Omega\left(\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}+\frac{2}{\lvert u\rvert}\right)-(\Omega-1)\frac{2}{\lvert u\rvert}-\frac{2}{\lvert u\rvert},

we have

\begin{split}\frac{\partial}{\partial u}(\Omega^{2}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\gamma}^{2})-\frac{2}{\lvert u\rvert}\Omega^{2}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\gamma}^{2}=&2\Omega^{3}\hat{\chi}(\nabla\hat{\otimes}\eta-\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\chi\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\eta\hat{\otimes}\eta)-b^{A}\frac{\partial}{\partial\theta^{A}}(\Omega^{2}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\gamma}^{2})\\
-&\Omega(\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}+\frac{2}{\lvert u\rvert})(\Omega^{2}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\gamma}^{2})+(\Omega-1)\cdot\frac{2}{\lvert u\rvert}\cdot(\Omega^{2}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\gamma}^{2}).\end{split}

(7.5)

We therefore have the following equation for
\hat{\chi}
:

\begin{split}\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\left(\lvert u\rvert^{2}\Omega^{2}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\gamma}^{2}\right)=&2\lvert u\rvert^{2}\Omega^{3}\hat{\chi}(\nabla\hat{\otimes}\eta-\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\chi\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\eta\hat{\otimes}\eta)-\lvert u\rvert^{2}b^{A}\frac{\partial}{\partial\theta^{A}}(\Omega^{2}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\gamma}^{2})\\
&-\lvert u\rvert^{2}\Omega(\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}+\frac{2}{\lvert u\rvert})(\Omega^{2}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\gamma}^{2})+\lvert u\rvert^{2}(\Omega-1)\cdot\frac{2}{\lvert u\rvert}(\Omega^{2}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\gamma}^{2}).\end{split}

(7.6)

For
b^{A}
, there holds

\frac{\partial b^{A}}{\partial\underline{u}}=-4\Omega^{2}\zeta^{A},

(7.7)

where
\zeta=\frac{1}{2}(\eta-\underline{\eta})
. Using the derived estimates on
\eta,\underline{\eta}
, one can then conclude that

\lVert b^{A}\rVert_{L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}},

(7.8)

in the slab of existence. For the right-hand side of (
7.6
), we can obtain, using the various estimates on the Ricci coefficients obtained during the proof of Theorem
1.2
, the following bounds:

\lVert{2\lvert u\rvert^{2}\Omega^{3}\hat{\chi}(\nabla\hat{\otimes}\eta-\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\chi\underline{\hat{\chi}}+\eta\hat{\otimes}\eta)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}},

(7.9)

\lVert{\lvert u\rvert^{2}b^{A}\frac{\partial}{\partial\theta^{A}}(\Omega^{2}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\gamma}^{2})}\rVert_{L^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}},

(7.10)

\lVert{\lvert u\rvert^{2}\Omega(\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}+\frac{2}{\lvert u\rvert})(\Omega^{2}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\gamma}^{2})}\rVert_{L^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\frac{a}{\lvert u^{\prime}\rvert^{2}},

(7.11)

\lVert{\lvert u\rvert^{2}(\Omega-1)\cdot\frac{2}{\lvert u\rvert}(\Omega^{2}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\gamma}^{2})}\rVert_{L^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}.

(7.12)

Putting everything together, we have

\big\lvert\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\left(\lvert u\rvert^{2}\Omega^{2}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\gamma}^{2}\right)\big\rvert\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\ll\frac{a^{\frac{7}{4}}}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}.

(7.13)

As a consequence, one has

\lvert u\rvert^{2}\Omega^{2}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\gamma}^{2}(u,\underline{u},\theta^{1},\theta^{2})-\lvert u_{\infty}\rvert^{2}\Omega^{2}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\gamma}^{2}(u_{\infty},\underline{u},\theta^{1},\theta^{2})\geq-\frac{a^{\frac{7}{4}}}{\lvert u\rvert}+\frac{a^{\frac{7}{4}}}{\lvert u_{\infty}\rvert}.

(7.14)

However, our gauge choice was
{\left.\kern-1.2pt\Omega\right|_{H_{u_{\infty}}}}\equiv 1
, therefore

\lvert u\rvert^{2}\Omega^{2}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\gamma}^{2}(u,\underline{u},\theta^{1},\theta^{2})\geq\lvert u_{\infty}\rvert^{2}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\gamma}^{2}(u_{\infty},\underline{u},\theta^{1},\theta^{2})-\frac{a^{\frac{7}{4}}}{\lvert u\rvert}.

(7.15)

Integrating from 0 to 1 with respect to
\underline{u}
, we have

\int_{0}^{1}\lvert u\rvert^{2}\Omega^{2}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\gamma}^{2}(u,\underline{u}^{\prime},\theta^{1},\theta^{2})\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\geq\int_{0}^{1}\lvert u_{\infty}\rvert^{2}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\gamma}^{2}(u_{\infty},\underline{u}^{\prime},\theta^{1},\theta^{2})\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}-\frac{a^{\frac{7}{4}}}{\lvert u\rvert}.

(7.16)

An analogous procedure using the null Yang-Mills equation for
\alpha^{F}
yields

\big\lvert\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\left(\lvert u\rvert^{2}\Omega^{2}\lvert\alpha^{F}\rvert_{\gamma,\delta}^{2}\right)\big\rvert\lesssim\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}\ll\frac{a^{\frac{7}{4}}}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}},

(7.17)

whence we also get

\int_{0}^{1}\lvert u\rvert^{2}\Omega^{2}\lvert\alpha^{F}\rvert_{\gamma,\delta}^{2}(u,\underline{u}^{\prime},\theta^{1},\theta^{2})\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\geq\int_{0}^{1}\lvert u_{\infty}\rvert^{2}\lvert\alpha^{F}\rvert_{\gamma,\delta}^{2}(u_{\infty},\underline{u}^{\prime},\theta^{1},\theta^{2})\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}-\frac{a^{\frac{7}{4}}}{\lvert u\rvert}.

(7.18)

Indeed, consider the equation

\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\alpha^{F}+\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\alpha^{F}=\widehat{\nabla}\rho^{F}+\prescript{*}{}{\widehat{\nabla}}\sigma^{F}-2\prescript{*}{}{\eta}\sigma^{F}+2\eta\rho^{F}+2\underline{\omega}\alpha^{F}-\hat{\chi}\cdot\underline{\alpha}^{F}

(7.19)

and contract with
\alpha^{F}
to get

\frac{1}{2}\widehat{\nabla}_{3}\lvert\alpha^{F}\rvert_{\gamma,\delta}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\lvert\alpha^{F}\rvert_{\gamma,\delta}^{2}-2\underline{\omega}\lvert\alpha^{F}\rvert_{\gamma,\delta}^{2}=\alpha^{F}(\widehat{\nabla}\rho^{F}+\prescript{*}{}{\widehat{\nabla}}\sigma^{F}-2\prescript{*}{}{\eta}\sigma^{F}+2\eta\rho^{F}+2\underline{\omega}\alpha^{F}-\hat{\chi}\cdot\underline{\alpha}^{F}).

(7.20)

As before, we obtain

\begin{split}&\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\left(\lvert u\rvert^{2}\Omega^{2}\lvert\alpha^{F}\rvert_{\gamma,\delta}^{2}\right)=\\
&2\lvert u\rvert^{2}\Omega^{3}\alpha^{F}(\widehat{\nabla}\rho^{F}+\prescript{*}{}{\widehat{\nabla}}\sigma^{F}-2\prescript{*}{}{\eta}\sigma^{F}+2\eta\rho^{F}+2\underline{\omega}\alpha^{F}-\hat{\chi}\cdot\underline{\alpha}^{F})-\lvert u\rvert^{2}b^{A}\frac{\partial}{\partial\theta^{A}}\left(\lvert u\rvert^{2}\Omega^{2}\lvert\alpha^{F}\rvert_{\gamma,\delta}^{2}\right)\\
-&\lvert u\rvert^{2}\Omega\left(\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}+\frac{2}{\lvert u\rvert}\right)(\Omega^{2}\lvert\alpha^{F}\rvert_{\gamma,\delta}^{2})+\lvert u\rvert^{2}(\Omega-1)\frac{2}{\lvert u\rvert}(\Omega^{2}\lvert\alpha^{F}\rvert_{\gamma,\delta}^{2}).\end{split}

(7.21)

Using the bounds obtained in the preceding sections, there holds

\lVert{2\lvert u\rvert^{2}\Omega^{3}\alpha^{F}(\widehat{\nabla}\rho^{F}+\prescript{*}{}{\widehat{\nabla}}\sigma^{F}-2\prescript{*}{}{\eta}\sigma^{F}+2\eta\rho^{F}+2\underline{\omega}\alpha^{F}-\hat{\chi}\cdot\underline{\alpha}^{F})}\rVert_{L^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}+\frac{a^{2}}{\lvert u\rvert^{3}},

(7.22)

\lVert{\lvert u\rvert^{2}b^{A}\frac{\partial}{\partial\theta^{A}}\left(\lvert u\rvert^{2}\Omega^{2}\lvert\alpha^{F}\rvert_{\gamma,\delta}^{2}\right)}\rVert_{L^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\frac{a^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}},

(7.23)

\lVert{\lvert u\rvert^{2}\Omega\left(\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}+\frac{2}{\lvert u\rvert}\right)(\Omega^{2}\lvert\alpha^{F}\rvert_{\gamma,\delta}^{2})}\rVert_{L^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}},

(7.24)

\lVert{\lvert u\rvert^{2}(\Omega-1)\frac{2}{\lvert u\rvert}(\Omega^{2}\lvert\alpha^{F}\rvert_{\gamma,\delta}^{2})}\rVert_{L^{\infty}_{(sc)}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\frac{a}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}.

(7.25)

Equations (
7.22
)-(
7.25
) yield (
7.17
) and (
7.18
).
Combining (
7.16
) and (
7.18
),we conclude that

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{1}\lvert u\rvert^{2}\Omega^{2}\left(\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\gamma}^{2}+\lvert\alpha^{F}\rvert_{\gamma,\delta}^{2}\right)(u,\underline{u}^{\prime},\theta^{1},\theta^{2})\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\geq&\int_{0}^{1}\lvert u_{\infty}\rvert^{2}\Omega^{2}\left(\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\gamma}^{2}+\lvert\alpha^{F}\rvert_{\gamma,\delta}^{2}\right)(u_{\infty},\underline{u}^{\prime},\theta^{1},\theta^{2})\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}-\frac{2a^{\frac{7}{4}}}{\lvert u\rvert}\geq a-\frac{2a^{\frac{7}{4}}}{\lvert u\rvert}\geq a-8\frac{a^{\frac{7}{4}}}{a}\geq\frac{7a}{8},\end{split}

(7.26)

uniformly for
u\in[u_{\infty},-a/4]
. Pick
u=-a/4
. Then (
7.26
) yields

\begin{split}&(-\frac{a}{4})^{2}\int_{0}^{1}\left(\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\gamma}^{2}+\lvert\alpha^{F}\rvert_{\gamma,\delta}^{2}\right)(-\frac{a}{4},\underline{u}^{\prime},\theta^{1},\theta^{2})\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
\geq&\frac{6}{7}\int_{0}^{1}(-\frac{a}{4})^{2}\Omega^{2}\left(\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\gamma}^{2}+\lvert\alpha^{F}\rvert_{\gamma,\delta}^{2}\right)(u,\underline{u}^{\prime},\theta^{1},\theta^{2})\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\geq\frac{6}{7}\cdot\frac{7a}{8}\geq\frac{3a}{4},\end{split}

(7.27)

where we have made use of the bound
\lVert\Omega-1\rVert_{L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{1}{a}
, for
a
sufficiently large. This implies

\int_{0}^{1}\left(\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\gamma}^{2}+\lvert\alpha^{F}\rvert_{\gamma,\delta}^{2}\right)(-\frac{a}{4},\underline{u}^{\prime},\theta^{1},\theta^{2})\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\geq\frac{3a}{4}\cdot\frac{16}{a^{2}}=\frac{12}{a}.

(7.28)

Consider now the equation

\nabla_{4}\mbox{tr}\chi+\frac{1}{2}(\mbox{tr}\chi)^{2}=-\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\gamma}^{2}-2\omega\mbox{tr}\chi-\lvert\alpha^{F}\rvert_{\gamma,\delta}^{2}.

(7.29)

This can be rewritten, using
\omega=-\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{4}(\text{log}\hskip 1.42262pt\Omega)
, as

\nabla_{4}\mbox{tr}\chi+\frac{1}{2}(\mbox{tr}\chi)^{2}=-\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\gamma}^{2}+\frac{1}{\Omega}\nabla_{4}\Omega\cdot\mbox{tr}\chi-\lvert\alpha^{F}\rvert_{\gamma,\delta}^{2},

(7.30)

which rewrites as

\nabla_{4}(\Omega^{-1}\mbox{tr}\chi)=\Omega^{-1}\left(\nabla_{4}\mbox{tr}\chi-\Omega^{-1}\nabla_{4}\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi\right)=\Omega^{-1}\left(-\frac{1}{2}(\mbox{tr}\chi)^{2}-\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\gamma}^{2}-\lvert\alpha^{F}\rvert_{\gamma,\delta}^{2}\right).

(7.31)

Finally, using the fact that
e_{4}=\Omega^{-1}\frac{\partial}{\partial\underline{u}}
, we arrive at the equation

\frac{\partial}{\partial\underline{u}}(\Omega^{-1}\mbox{tr}\chi)=\frac{1}{2}(\mbox{tr}\chi)^{2}-\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\gamma}^{2}-\lvert\alpha^{F}\rvert_{\gamma,\delta}^{2}.

(7.32)

Since at
\underline{u}=0
the initial data are Minkowskian, we have

(\Omega^{-1}\mbox{tr}\chi)(-\frac{a}{4},0,\theta^{1},\theta^{2})=1^{-1}\cdot\frac{2}{\frac{a}{4}}=\frac{8}{a}.

(7.33)

Integrating (
7.32
), we obtain

\begin{split}(\Omega^{-1}\mbox{tr}\chi)(-\frac{a}{4},1,\theta^{1},\theta^{2})&\leq(\Omega^{-1}\mbox{tr}\chi)(-\frac{a}{4},0,\theta^{1},\theta^{2})-\int_{0}^{1}\lvert\hat{\chi}\rvert_{\gamma}^{2}+\lvert\alpha^{F}\rvert_{\gamma,\delta}^{2}(-\frac{a}{4},\underline{u}^{\prime},\theta^{1},\theta^{2})\hskip 1.42262pt\text{d}\underline{u}^{\prime}\\
&\leq\frac{8}{a}-\frac{12}{a}<0.\end{split}

(7.34)

Notice that the above bound holds pointwise on the sphere
S_{-\frac{a}{4},1}
. Finally, recall the bound

\lVert\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}+\frac{2}{\lvert u\rvert}\rVert_{L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\frac{1}{\lvert u\rvert^{2}}.

(7.35)

As a result,

\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}(-\frac{a}{4},1,\theta^{1},\theta^{2})<0,

(7.36)

for all
(\theta^{1},\theta^{2})\in\mathbb{S}^{2}
. The bounds (
7.34
) and (
7.36
) finally imply that the sphere
S_{-\frac{a}{4},1}
is trapped, which was what we wanted.

\square

8
Concluding Remarks

We conclude with a few brief remarks. The first one we wish to make is that, given the fact that all preceding estimates are independent of
u_{\infty}
, one can take (carefully) the limit
u_{\infty}\to\infty
and thus obtain a trapped surface formation criterion from past null infinity. In that case, the present result could be interpreted as a formation of trapped surfaces due to the focusing of an amalgamation of Yang-Mills waves and gravitational waves. Secondly, going back to the statement of Theorem
1.2
, we see that conditions (1) and (2) are formally independent. This means that, in principle, both
\hat{\chi}
and
{(\alpha^{F})^{P}}_{Q}
can contribute equally to the largeness of the data. As a consequence, our result implies that in a spherically symmetric spacetime (
\hat{\chi}=0
) the mere presence of Yang-Mills "radiation" is enough to form a trapped surface, as long as this radiation is, of course, suitably large. Also, since we noticed in the work of
[
26
]
that Yang-Mills fields can counterbalance the gravitational attraction to yield regular solutions, it would be natural to investigate the precise mathematical condition on the Yang-Mills fields that would lead to a ‘non-trapping’ scenario. Finally, we note that many subsequent problems naturally arise, for example the formation of apparent horizons for the Einstein-Yang-Mills system as well as trapped surface formation questions for different matter models. Important examples of such models are the Einstein-Klein-Gordon system and more ambitiously the Einstein-Euler system, which promises to be the most mathematically difficult, due to the possible formation of shocks and the breakdown of spacetime regularity before trapped surfaces have a chance to form. We intend to investigate these issues in the future.
